# Generative Existence (生生不息论)

**Dependent-family continuation, 7 October 2026.** [The new integration](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-FAMILIES-INTEGRATION.md) extends finite admission to dependent sums and sections and reconstructs arbitrary supplied sections by finite-graph ideals, preserving fixed-base fiber relationships. **33 new Lean theorems / 52 audits pass.** Admitting supports of every ambient finite-fiber family is already equivalent to full predicate admission; pointwise finiteness does not independently warrant the whole assignment. Independent internal review and a focused primary-source assessment are integrated. Full dependent-language coverage, infinite-domain warrant and exclusive root priority remain open.

**生成起点与情境理由，2026年10月7日：** [首轮推导](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-PREMISE-INTEGRATION.md)沿 Ren 批准的显式规范起点，解释稳定维护、重组与结束的共同根据；11项新 Lean 定理检查比较、理由和选择的不同前提。[当前理论](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#from-the-generative-premise-to-situated-reasons)保留真实生成冲突及没有最终最大值的可能，继续研究情境取舍。有限推导完成，规范起点自身的更强辩护与普遍适用仍各有范围。

**Explicit foundation → situated consequences, 7 October 2026.** [The practical formulation](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#from-an-explicit-foundation-to-situated-requirements) states generative significance and choice-specific relevance as an explicit proposed premise, then applies the existing general dependency results. [Argument, counterreview and living correspondence](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERAL-GENERATIVE-REQUIREMENT-INTEGRATION.md#direct-foundational-continuation-and-an-explicit-starting-point) retain alternative realizations, finite fulfillment, dormant capacities and differentiated response. This is a minimal explicit-premise framework; full physical coverage, independent universal warrant and mixed comparisons remain unfinished. No new mathematical proof is claimed.

**生成的规范起点与未来条件，2026年10月7日：** [当前表述](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#an-explicit-ethical-starting-point-and-the-generative-conditions-of-a-future)接续 Ren 关于 generation 重要的 axiom 与未来依存的提议。明确的伦理起点可指导一般推导；实际延续要求回应该未来的必要生成关系，具体途径可不同。研究不再逐个要求新结构证明孤立价值，保留有限完成、整体范围及基础命题进一步辩护。[整合与范围](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-CAPACITY-INTEGRATION.md#15-生成的规范起点与未来的构成条件)不增加数学证明或将公理化等同于无前提完成。

**Finite mathematical admission and general named theories, 7 October 2026:** [the checked continuation](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-COVERAGE-INTEGRATION.md) derives full predicate admission from finite formation through every declared finite type over a supplied finite base. It also extends graph/ideal truth reconstruction to arbitrary typed named signatures, whole theories, satisfiability and semantic consequence. Thirty-six new Lean theorems and 66 audits pass. Independent infinite-domain warrant and foundation-wide mathematical coverage remain unresolved; these scoped advances do not complete the universal root claim.

**重组与建设性结束，2026年10月7日：** [新的完整比较](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-CAPACITY-INTEGRATION.md#11-重组与建设性结束)完成45项新 Lean 定理和12,402条独立状态对应。生成装置可以通过有消耗的材料回收参与后续形成；先前的效率优势随完整后续历史而改变。相同条件下的新分化与重复形成均得到实际路径。[当前伦理表述](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#reconfiguration-and-constructive-endings)据此解释建设性结束，同时保留结构差异与实际生命丰富性的区别。普遍伦理根据仍是第一研究重点，未由这项机制比较完成。

**General continuation → situated generative requirements, 7 October 2026:** [the new proof and integration](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERAL-GENERATIVE-REQUIREMENT-INTEGRATION.md) establish uniform conditional requirements for arbitrary histories and indexed dependencies. Excess actual demand requires support addition or route change; inherited replenishment failure requires replacement. Explicit invariant, progress and implementation certificates separately construct a continuing history. Fresh Lean checks pass 24 new named theorems and 42 declaration audits. The [current ethical formulation](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#general-requirements-and-situated-realization) connects these necessities to justified continuation without making every recipe obligatory. Living correspondence, adequate replacement and universal first warrant remain explicit; the result is not a completed ethics from the bare process interface.

**Recursive warrant run assessment, 7 October 2026:** [the latest audit](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md#14-three-continuation-impasse-audit) preserves the positive generative candidate and completed conditional mechanisms, but records an impasse in this run's universal normative derivation. Current evidence does not establish the constitutive ethical identification and its differentiated practical force. Further substantive argument or evidence is needed; neither impossibility nor completion is claimed, and parallel research retains its authorization.

**Universal common-good inference tested, 7 October 2026:** [the direct continuation](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md#10-can-shared-generation-ground-a-common-practical-good) develops shared generative realization as a candidate good and tests its practical authority. Joint capability can support different lived relations, including adverse ones; universal validity need not serve every generated purpose. The [current ethical section](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#shared-generation-and-common-practical-significance) preserves the positive direction, actual losses and the unfinished first-warrant question. No new mathematical proof or declaration of universal completion is made.

**Universal mathematical grounding remains unproved, 7 October 2026:** [the renewed completion assessment](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-FAMILY-WARRANT-REASSESSMENT.md#6-renewed-completion-request-final-assessment-of-the-available-argument) retains the checked conditional reconstruction while distinguishing full admission, universal mathematical coverage and unique constitutive priority. The latest request did not yield a derivation of these stronger claims; no assumption was added or conjecture promoted to a theorem.

**Deeper recursive formation, 7 October 2026:** the [new integrated study](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md) checks actual output, reconstruction of the selection arrangement and its next operation in one finite history. Eight new Lean theorems retain resource limits and inheritance reversal; two primary experiments support evolved collective recurrence. The [ethical account](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#recursive-formation-and-the-universal-finish-line) develops favorable further formation and its practical bearing while allowing finite completion. The route is more concrete; selection of reconstruction rules and universal ethical warrant remain open.

**共同形成能力，2026年10月7日：** [新增接续结果](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-CAPACITY-INTEGRATION.md#7-从新增途径到共同实现)以35项 Lean 定理和1,109条独立对应记录，证明付出形成成本的共用连接可以让两种原先各自可达的结构在同一有限支持下共同完成。其意义在于实际共同形成，超出对独立选项的计数。[当前伦理接合](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#supported-joint-formation)将其接到后续实际使用与生成条件，保留未固定用途的材料、其他形成和重组的机会。普遍伦理仍待证明；下一检验是生成性专门化怎样继续转化。

**Common mathematical domain warrant, 7 October 2026; direction reassessment:** [the latest audit](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-FAMILY-WARRANT-REASSESSMENT.md) distinguishes one explanation of a whole domain from separate finite recipes for its members. A proposed branching construction repeats checked completion results and does not yet justify full admission. No new theorem is claimed. The relative assembly result remains credited; an independent whole-range warrant or explanatory-dependence argument is still needed.

**A relative mathematical admission argument, 7 October 2026:** [the indexed-assembly continuation](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-ASSEMBLY-INTEGRATION.md) connects a supplied witness relation to its derivative membership through output-preserving abstraction, with an explicit parity formation example. Thirty-one new Lean theorems and 48 audits pass. Unrestricted naturally indexed finite-chain closure still has full predicate strength on `Nat`; whole-family admission and unique root dependence remain unresolved.

**Recursive generative selection, 7 October 2026:** [Ren's proposal](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-recursive-generative-selection.md) is developed in the [selection account](unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-SELECTION.md#recursive-generative-selection-across-scales) and [ethical connection](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#recursive-selection-and-the-generative-direction). New checked mathematics distinguishes collective success from the selection of its contributors, and primary experiments support a conditional route through group formation and life cycles. The stronger proposed tendency concerns evolution of the arrangements that connect future selection to shared renewal. Its repeated reconstruction and universal ethical warrant remain open; local failures do not by themselves refute the wider mechanism, and survival alone does not prove it.

**生成能力与实际接续，2026年10月7日：** [当前论证与形式结果](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-CAPACITY-INTEGRATION.md)通过38项新 Lean 定理和174条独立对应记录，区分任意深的有限完成、同一历史中的反复形成及始终只作准备。接入点在实际新结构中形成，源与消耗明确；固定规则中的深度增长尚不等于形成种类增加。[伦理接合](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#formed-capacity-and-actual-continuation)正面辩护新增有效形成途径的意义，保留未用能力、有限实现和跨范围损失。下一工作须检验新关系如何拓展有效生成及其实践根据；普遍元伦理目标不变。

**有限认识中的情境伦理，2026年10月7日：** [当前表述](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#situated-judgment-under-deep-uncertainty)与[研究整合](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SITUATED-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md)说明帮助怎样同时改变当下生活、后续能力、依赖、传播及后来判断的条件。未知不自动取消已有理由，可修订也不自动授权试行；谁承受错误、证据怎样形成及何时还能修正，需要进入同一根据。正面论证、独立反评审与五项原始来源已整合，无新增数学或实证结果。经 Ren 对对立式框架的质疑，下一连接回到回应怎样共同形成生活、能力与继续条件，并说明其伦理意义；风险与许可成为相应关系形成后的分支。普遍伦理证明仍开放。

**Continuation through generativity, 7 October 2026:** [the current account](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#continuation-depends-on-generativity) develops [Ren's thought](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-continuation-depends-on-generativity.md) that life, systems and humanity depend on being generative. The [integrated study](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GENERATIVITY.md) includes checked conditional renewal necessity, a fixed-form witness limiting the inference to novelty, substantive ethical review and three primary-source assessments. The affirmative candidate is life becoming capable of further differentiated life. Universal ethical warrant remains the next bridge; individual productivity, continual complexity growth and inevitable compliance are not inferred.

**当前伦理判准，2026年10月7日：** [Ren 将生生不息指认为好的整体方向](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md)：持续创造更多生、生成更丰富的可能性和多样性，使复杂组织能够继续发展。生命／生态、心智／关系／生活方式及广义组织生成等多个范围都保留。[正面重构](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SHENGSHENG-DIRECTION-RECONSTRUCTION.md)接续此前“结构形成更多结构、善以宇宙的生为标准”的直接原话，明确跨范围的下一比较；不把旧的“已经值得的可能性”当成这一根据，也不宣告普遍证明完成。

**本然良知的生成演化，2026年10月7日：** [当前理论](unified-2026-10-05/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md)连接19项传递与17项发展重建的已检查声明及早期任务理解证据。[最新实践整合](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIANGZHI-PRACTICAL-INTEGRATION.md)把四类回应变化接到生生正面方向，并以20项新增 Lean 声明检查真实支持交接、失败恢复和有限结束。先撤后接也可成功；交接并非仁的充分条件，原典拟归也未冒称实际交接。下一问题是目的／参与变化与依存冲突时的理由范围及其校准；完整良知与普遍根据仍开放。

Changed choice is not changed valuation, and self-history does not fix concern for others independently of the organization's own law. Prediction does not separate from scoring in behaviour. Valuation can be captured by decoupled signals. One event enacted and undergone can yield opposite valuations under identical learners. Functional valuation, experience and ethical warrant remain distinct; nothing here shows a formed valuation to be good.

**Relief within a chosen project, 6 October 2026:** the [new comparison](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#relief-within-an-already-chosen-project) defends prospective patient-based justification between equally suitable ways of carrying out an existing project. It preserves choice of means and the possibility that justified protections disqualify a consideration for a particular choice. Patient value elsewhere is insufficient to establish the intended practical claim. The candidate now explicitly separates that scope question from permission, attention and answerability; no universal necessity proof is claimed.

**Created reasons and prior patient significance, 6 October 2026:** the [elective-concern continuation](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#what-choosing-to-care-can-create) strengthens the account by allowing agents genuinely to create reasons and relationships. It distinguishes that power from the further claim that every reason to relieve an existing patient must be created through elective concern. Self-direction may support commitments and protected choices without yet establishing that exclusion. The candidate thus seeks a common explanation of recognition and creation. Their grounds and interactions still need justification; the separate defense of prospective moral favor is not converted into a theorem by rejecting one argument against it.

**Constitutive patient and practical significance, 6 October 2026:** the [unified-grounding candidate](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#the-constitutive-candidate-and-its-test) now specifies what must be realized, what experiential whole is proposed as locally bad for its subject, and why an available act's relief could have moral merit before elective concern. The new private-adoption comparison isolates the actor relation without denying genuine choice-created duties. Empirical readings constrain the identification of experience; the constitutive hypotheses remain philosophical. This advances an explicit candidate while leaving necessity, universal realization and stronger rational authority open.

**Patient benefit can precede a helping end, 6 October 2026:** the [revised unified-grounding account](unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#a-direct-reason-can-precede-an-adopted-end) now defends a direct patient-ground as a defeasible ethical judgment. ## Current unified foundation

**Generative language revised, 6 October 2026:** [Ren’s instruction](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-generative-language-revision.md) is now applied to the current exposition. The [grounding account](unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#minimal-grounding-and-universal-scope) begins with activity forming and changing the conditions of further activity; specific roles, quantities and exchanges enter where the explanation establishes them. The [shared method](unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md#keep-foundational-language-at-the-right-level) gives English/Chinese descriptive choices. The [revision record](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-health/succession/wording-revision/README.md) preserves prior formulations, local results and their provenance; the wording changes establish no new scientific result.

**Supported continuation and correction, 6 October 2026:** the [recipient correspondence](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-health/succession/RECIPIENT-CONTINUATION.md) and [new prefix analysis](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-health/succession/CONTINUITY-AND-CORRECTION.md) show why an eventual return may fail an independently specified continuity requirement. When every available response path violates that requirement, correction must change the obstructing relation or revise the adequacy claim. The existing learner already favors service; its access protocol remains restrictive. This sharpens the relation between consequence and correction without asserting recipient physiology, universal ethical necessity or an implemented self-revision.

**Generative evaluation and the ranking correction, 6 October 2026:** the [reconstructed succession argument](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-health/succession/NONDUAL-EVALUATION.md) follows [Ren's objection to reductionism](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-ranking-and-nondual-evaluation.md). Its task-ranking reversal concerns a particular partial comparison and does not refute a universal generative criterion. Actual construction, records, response and support must be understood through their relations; locally supplied objectives do not imply significance outside generation. The [grounding account](unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#what-universality-means-here) records this correction while retaining original results and the stronger explanatory and ethical questions.

**What must survive correction, 6 October 2026:** [the memory/succession analysis](unified-2026-10-05/MEMORY-CORRECTION-AND-SUCCESSION.md) separates a repaired archive from its later justified use, and conditional benchmark performance from joint useful success. Explicit counterexamples reject an unsupported full-log impossibility inference while retaining observation-relative limits. Primary materials and separate mathematical review constrain the remaining empirical test: retain applicable corrections and revise defeated inherited content using accessible evidence. No new learned-agent outcome or complete successor implementation is claimed here.

**External evidence and institutional correction, 6 October 2026:** the [Robodebt application](unified-2026-10-05/EVIDENCE-AND-INSTITUTIONAL-CORRECTION.md) tests the gap between a corrected individual decision and revision of its shared rationale. A primary-source audit and independently reviewed contract require the scope of changed action to match the scope of a justified correction, with authority, resources, implementation and nonappellant coverage exposed. This supplies a concrete governance failure test; it does not establish the proposed design's causal benefit or derive ethical standing from institutional survival. Warranted evaluative correction through actual succession remains the central connection. The [follow-through extension](unified-2026-10-05/EVIDENCE-AND-INSTITUTIONAL-CORRECTION.md#correction-must-also-reach-the-information-people-act-on) tests the outward handoff: correcting an internal rule does not itself update affected people’s available guidance or deliver their remedy. It retains positive implementation evidence and unresolved coverage.

**Why shared architectures become available, 6 October 2026:** [the architecture construction](unified-2026-10-05/ARCHITECTURE-FORMATION.md) replaces the finished-route menu with resource-dependent local graph formation and a second assembly using actual consequence-funded guides. The same component law produces broad and identity-filtered routing, with finite parts, failed histories and separate recipient accounts. Independent checks cover fifty declared comparison arms. Wider production increases primary recipient use but gives no overall retention advantage; higher maintenance and wrong transfer preserve exclusion and damage. This realizes the proposed addition that organized activity changes what can form next. The component recipes, construction schedule and recipient calibration remain explicit; the following continuation now joins learned recipient correction, while co-development of centers and shared regulation remains proposed.

Its [completion conditions](unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#completion-of-the-unified-foundation) require connected derivations, compatible mechanisms, and discriminating evidence. The physical-direction completion plan below is one contributing branch; its milestones do not define completion of the whole foundation.

**Current research path, approved 6 October 2026:** the [accepted work sequence](unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#accepted-work-sequence) begins with organizational dependencies producing consequence-sensitive regulation, then learning and revision of selectors, and their connection to the existing concern and wisdom results. The first acceptance gate requires actual formation, costly operation and consequence-dependent retention. These are open research obligations; the contributions below supply components and constraints.

**Governing research position, clarified by Ren:** the [generative grounding](unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md) seeks one account throughout reality wherever systems form, based on minimal properties of consequential differentiation, structured interaction and composition. Significance, learning, conscience, and constructive or destructive regimes belong inside the explanation. The recent concern games are subsidiary constructions; their supplied ethical anchors and favorable economies do not define the foundation. [Ren’s correction](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-generative-origin-of-significance.md) is preserved separately from the AI-assisted development.

**Previous first-replacement result:** [complete entrance and first replacement](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FULL-SUCCESSOR-RESULT.md) now have a common tagged process and directed bounds. Replacement and reserve-restoring replacement favor opposite entry classes at H10, while both remain too rare to establish reliable regeneration. [RNA task identification](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RNA-PARTIAL-IDENTIFICATION.md) narrows the necessary measurement contract without assuming uniquely known microscopic parameters. The [v0.9 specification](unified-2026-10-05/FORMAL-SPECIFICATION.md) connects these findings to the still-open full goal.

**Previous formalization and discovery:** [all-history formation](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FULL-ENTRY-SUCCESSOR.md) now has a certified entrance comparison, while full successor renewal remains open. [Typed and reserve-sensitive Lean proofs](../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/formal/README.md), a [paid discovery theorem](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/CONSEQUENTIAL-DISCOVERY.md), and an [RNA identification contract](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RNA-IDENTIFICATION-CONTRACT.md) make the next physical, information and evidence obligations concrete. The [v0.8 specification](unified-2026-10-05/FORMAL-SPECIFICATION.md) and [review](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ENTRANCE-DISCOVERY-REVIEW.md) distinguish these completed conditional results from the open universal goal.

**Previous combined-process milestone:** [resource-dependent access to captured support](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PAID-ACCESS-SUCCESSION.md) now pays for formation and replacement of all four carriers at unit positive coefficients. Its complete task has a certified positive comparison at time10 and the opposite early sign; success remains rare. A [rate-class and extinction analysis](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/SUCCESSION-DIRECTION-CRITIQUE.md), [physical constraints](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PAID-ACCESS-PHYSICAL-CONSTRAINTS.md) and [finite-source certificate](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PAID-ACCESS-THERMODYNAMIC-CERTIFICATE.md) make support and scope explicit. The [Lean ledger](../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/formal/README.md) checks the original storage obstruction and minimum capture cost. Specification v0.7 and the [review](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PAID-ACCESS-REVIEW.md) distinguish this finished conditional comparison from open physical identification, reliable ensemble renewal, useful prevalence and universal direction. The prior [integrated eight-capture result](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/INTEGRATED-FORMATION-RENEWAL.md), [retention obstruction](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/USEFUL-RETENTION-MECHANISM.md) and [RNA evidence audit](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PHYSICAL-EVIDENCE-RNA-NETWORK.md) remain intact.

The earlier [physical turnover](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RENEWAL-RETURN.md), [fabricator formation](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ARCHITECTURE-FORMATION-CRITIQUE.md), [one-capture embedding](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/THERMODYNAMIC-EMBEDDING.md) and [direction limits](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/DIRECTION-AND-TURNOVER.md) retain their original proofs and assumptions.

**Current paid-mechanism theorem:** [allocation control](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ALLOCATION-CONTROL.md) changes the interaction that consumes a needed precursor, pays for fabrication and finite response, and proves a joint fresh-support gain from a common regulator-free preparation in a stated finite-rate class. It is initially harmful for the same target before becoming beneficial. This closes one conditional paid episode, while architecture origin, repeated maintenance, physical identification and universal direction remain open. The [new continuation experiment](../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/continuation/README.md) retains all failures of the older gate.

**Preceding mechanistic refinement:** the [opportunity audit](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/REGULATION-OPPORTUNITY-AUDIT.md) distinguishes loss of free fuel from loss of a later productive function. The [response diagnosis](../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/response/README.md) confirms that the tested regulator can consume the last productive unit. The [reviewed biological feedback evidence](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PHYSICAL-EVIDENCE-CERONI.md) supplies a real burden-relief mechanism and costly failure regimes, while leaving formation from absence and universal direction open. Specification v0.3 therefore requires a demonstrated productive opportunity, a paid protection mechanism and joint continuation tests.

**Earlier physical formation test:** [constructed fuel-responsive regulation](../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/README.md) makes sensing and control material parts of the shared-renewal process, with exact accounting and a construction-lag theorem. All prespecified constructed-versus-absent comparisons are unfavorable. This retains a substantive boundary: a locally correct response and available energy do not establish that constructing the response sustains the whole. The [concern-settlement integration review](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/CONCERN-SETTLEMENT-INTEGRATION.md) accepts the completed parallel contribution without treating strategic equilibrium as a physical directional law.

**Current formal bridge:** [joint capabilities from the renewal event law](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/JOINT-CAPABILITY-BRIDGE.md) now connects the physical stochastic model to reliable joint tests. Increased recycling gives productive gains but can trade away reserve readiness; the installed regions are incomparable at one fixed horizon and reliability. This is a scoped result with explicit counterexamples, alongside the completed conditional joint-formation theory.

**Formalization-led completion, 5 October 2026:** the [common specification](unified-2026-10-05/FORMAL-SPECIFICATION.md) and [practical completion plan](unified-2026-10-05/COMPLETION-PLAN.md) make the physical-to-capability-to-direction bridges explicit. The [integration record](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/INTEGRATION.md) preserves separate evidence statuses and tracks active contributions. A complete specification is the first gate; the stronger positive universal goal remains unresolved.

The [current research index](../../RESEARCH.md) connects all recent branches, their repository homes, and their evidence status. It is the starting point for work completed after the synthesis below.

**Current synthesis, 5 October 2026.** The latest sessions are integrated in [Relational generativity and the conditions of wisdom](unified-2026-10-05/THEORY.md), with a [short reading guide](unified-2026-10-05/START-HERE.md), [formal core](unified-2026-10-05/FORMAL-CORE.md), [claim register](unified-2026-10-05/CLAIMS.md), and [source map](unified-2026-10-05/SOURCE-MAP.md). This section governs the current interpretation where earlier formulations below differ.

The proposed common foundation is **activity changing the conditions of further activity**. The object of explanation is a joint situation with differentiated centers and relations: what supports their activity, what that activity changes, and what it leaves them and their successors effectively able to do. Where continued functioning faces unbounded cumulative loss of indispensable finite supports, renewal, replacement, provision, or transformed dependence is necessary. Understanding and correction have supporting conditions of their own.

The current formal development joins boundary-consistent accounting, causal formation of renewal, plural capacity constraints, faithful representations, actual-information limits, funded correction, and succession. In a specified reaction model, renewal machinery can form from zero and the joint process converges to a maintained regime above an exact threshold. A separate institutional construction shows that shared production can persist while an affected participant loses a protected capacity; a repeating protective correction needs recurring funding, prefunded access, timely action, and actual authority. The assumptions of those two models remain distinct.

The integrated certification target is the **actual coupled process**, including its installed observation, selection, intervention, and revision mechanisms. For finite specified models, information-state viability identifies feasible safety policies; a protected invariant with decreasing event-specific ranks characterizes actual safety and recurring service. Computed possibilities do not automatically become installed behavior, and physical implementation must fund memory, computation, and transition costs. These are conditional results using established mathematics, not a universal empirical equation.

The proposed root of wisdom is a situated capacity to recognize and revise consequential dependencies in understanding and conduct while maintaining workable means of further correction. The work preserves the difference between accurate prediction, sufficient decisions, historical truth, model revision, interface construction, and changing commitments. Both small reasoning pilots reached ceiling performance in all conditions, so no special-method advantage is established. Other simulations show that inquiry can consume service resources and that inherited material can help or obstruct construction under fixed budgets.

The ethical core explicitly values affected beings' suffering, agency, and worthwhile futures, including the effective ability to contest conditions governing them. Their standing matters even when aggregate production could continue without them. Under that commitment, 善 concerns supporting and repairing those possibilities and the capacity for warranted correction. **A general tendency of reality toward increasingly inclusive, responsive regeneration remains an open causal hypothesis.** Neither survival, interconnection, a representation theorem, nor recurring motion proves it.

The [synthesis record](unified-2026-10-05/SYNTHESIS-RECORD.md) records checkpoints `003cd2a` and `38f8022`, independent reconstruction, review repairs, and the next discriminating study. The original source files and earlier formulations remain available. The current synthesis does not endorse earlier claims of a uniquely grounded alignment target, unproved global inexhaustibility, or universal moral direction as mathematical conclusions.

## Current exploration of direction

The [direction and concern continuation](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-direction-and-generalized-concern.md) takes the open universal tendency as its research focus. It identifies a specific bridge: representing another center accurately, being affected by its condition, valuing that condition, acting on it, and transmitting the capacity for correction are causally connected but distinct. A cheaper bypass can turn stronger returned consequences into better insulation instead of greater protection.

The [new exploration](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-direction-frontier/EXPLORATION.md) derives a conditional resource threshold for protective response to spread and a resource-dependent trial mechanism that can construct an initially absent response type. The construction repertoire and trial rule are supplied. These results concern specified protection and relative propagation; they do not establish a general increase in wisdom or the protected standing of new recipients.

The constructive frontier is how concern forms and generalizes to unfamiliar affected beings beyond their usefulness, then remains effective, revisable, and transmissible under cost. Causal and ethical warrants differ, while moral learning and conscience can belong to the same natural process being explained. A comparative hypothesis now separates predictive generalization from costly protection of recipients who cannot reward or punish the actor. This is a proposed research advance; the unified formal core and its evidence boundaries remain in force.

## Current theory of the formation and renewal of concern

The [new concern theory](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-concern-theory/THEORY.md), with a [short reading guide](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-concern-theory/START-HERE.md), develops the current frontier into a complete conditional mechanism and a broader candidate architecture. Another’s condition acquires motivational significance when it changes what a process treats as worth doing; direct concern specifically counts the other’s benefit in favor of action and their harm against it, apart from private consequences. Standing is not created by being recognized or made dependent on productive usefulness.

The effective concern coefficient now forms as the product of learned significance and learned recognition, both initially zero. Shared evaluative routing, primitive teaching signals, and informative learning opportunities are declared premises. Exact results characterize protective choice, finite-class widening, memory maintenance, paid outcome correction, and eventual protection across lossy succession. Every-prefix resource accounting and an optional productive-capacity bridge specify conditions for continued operation. Identity-gated learning, cue concealment, harmful intervention, and unfunded care provide separate counterexamples.

This is model-relative closure, not a completed universal science of concern: efficacy revision is implemented, while the origin and revision of feature repertoires, evaluative routing, ethical scope, and authority remain broader explanatory tasks. Five primary research sources provide limited anchors; the [claim register](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-concern-theory/CLAIMS.md), [review and checks](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-concern-theory/RESEARCH-RECORD.md), and [source record](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-formation-and-renewal-of-concern.md) preserve scope, provenance, and remaining questions. A universal tendency toward wiser, more inclusive regeneration remains open.

The [new concern–correction bridge](unified-2026-10-05/CONCERN-AND-CORRECTION.md) integrates this parallel branch into the current synthesis. It derives the conditions under which learned concern and an active participant-controlled alternative jointly induce protection, while retaining harmless exit as a distinct response to inadequate safeguards. Joint developmental closure under strategic interference remains the next research step.

## Current joint formation and renewal

The [joint-formation theory](unified-2026-10-05/JOINT-FORMATION.md) now gives a constructive conditional solution to the previously central challenge. A controller initially assigning no learned value to the patient chooses useful learning while anticipating its later care expenditure; shared learning competes with deliberate identity gating. It then pays for genuine inspection and physical input control, and a persistent operator optimally safeguards. A shared reserve funds complete reconstruction by fresh controllers. The [formal proof](unified-2026-10-05/JOINT-FORMATION-MATH.md) covers all dated decision makers, finite games, and an infinite discounted equilibrium; the [experiment](../../experiments/joint-formation/README.md) retains independent reconstruction and exact failure cases.

The patient supplies no useful input or reciprocal return. Continued protection nevertheless depends on a given account/input-control role, useful learning opportunity, justified feature relation, physical repertoire, and adequate costs of capture and self-insulation. Free identity filtering, cheap forced-open commitment, or recurring attacks on the learning budget can defeat the mechanism. This resolves one joint-existence and renewal problem while rejecting an automatic universal direction toward concern. The model does not derive moral standing from any actor's acquired coefficient.

## Current formation of effective correction

**First completed development.** The [formation study](unified-2026-10-05/FORMATION.md) derives one route by which a participant's materially constructed alternative makes a self-interested operator protect them. It also derives a strategic failure: an operator can pay more now to remove the participant's ability to finance later correction. Under the stated two-period assumptions, first operation needs reserve K+m+ell while removing this particular attack needs K+2m+ell. The [new proofs](unified-2026-10-05/FORMATION-MATH.md) and exact experiment distinguish an affordable start, deferred activation, and immediate protected operation. Physical control, public observability, technology, and interference cost remain supplied; no general origin of rights or concern is claimed.

The [ethical development](unified-2026-10-05/ETHICAL-FOUNDATION.md) gives explicit premises and a positive argument for affected beings' standing beyond usefulness. Standing, influence, and legitimacy have different grounds. A separate countermodel shows that maintained productive renewal can leave harm unaddressed when that harm changes neither selector incentives nor constraints. The general foundation therefore tracks how present activity changes the conditions under which later understanding, choice, and correction can occur. The formation of protection for beings with little leverage, and the discovery of new consequential questions, remain central research frontiers.

## Current development of revisable concern

The [new chapter on revising who and what matters](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-revisable-concern/THEORY.md), with a [short guide](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-revisable-concern/START-HERE.md), advances from efficacy correction to a finite procedure for revising the operative scope of a stated reason. A warranted comparison with an excluded case challenges the process to extend its reason, establish a relevant difference, or openly revise its prior commitment. Unresolved claims, direct intake for unfamiliar stakes, and the possibility of evasion remain explicit. The implemented branch admits an existing reason for new cases; it does not derive every ethical commitment or invent arbitrary new concepts.

The formal results distinguish warranted equivalence, retained evidence, actual delivery, numerical uptake, and authority. Boolean admission has exact finite closure; local averaging has explicit convergence and deadline conditions. A blocked channel does not invalidate a retained reason, while defeating an old certificate need not automatically undo a learned inference. Strategic suppression must be tested jointly: two claims can each resist individual interference yet remain vulnerable through one shared bottleneck. Exact cut conditions, controller cash limits, separately funded reinforcement, and authority-capture counterexamples make that boundary concrete.

The [claim register](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-revisable-concern/CLAIMS.md), [review and verification record](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-revisable-concern/RESEARCH-RECORD.md), and [source record](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-revising-the-scope-of-concern.md) preserve premises and limits. This complements the learned-concern and constructed-correction modules. Their full joint developmental dynamics, the grounds and revision of ethical commitments, new-concept formation, and universal direction remain open.

## Current settlement of revisable concern

The [integrated settlement](unified-2026-10-05/REVISABLE-CONCERN-SETTLEMENT.md) now joins paid finite audit, reason admission and selective retraction, retained alternative grounds, strategic protection, and successor reconstruction in one selected process. The exact witness discloses two harms to patients outside the then-admitted set; admission does not create their standing. A separate paid feature construction builds a composite absent among the initial primitives, within a supplied finite grammar and authored labels.

The ethical branch defends reasons from subjects' losses and agency and proves finite results for robust comparison and permitted symmetric allocation. These do not automatically certify the strategic equilibrium: model selection, competing claims and legitimate authority remain independent obligations. The evidence audit adds no observations and finds no demonstrated general superiority; the proposed human test remains draft and unrun. [Proofs, checks and independent internal reviews](../../_process/research/2026-10-05-revisable-concern/settlement/README.md) preserve the repaired gate-price assumption and empirical-design limits. The broader claims of universal ethical direction, subjective experience, unrestricted ethical discovery and unique optimality remain unsettled.

## Earlier opening preserved for genealogy

The following two opening paragraphs and the earlier argument are retained as the pre-integration formulation. Their historical claims and stronger philosophical assertions should be read with the current scope and claim register above; preservation does not renew their warrant.

**The theory in one paragraph.** Nothing that exists is exhausted by what it has already become. Every state carries 間 (jiān — an unexhausted gap, the place where a state is not used up by what it already is), and existence is nothing but the unbroken taking-up of such gaps: 续(态) = 态 ⊕ 間(态). So 生生不息 does not mean that any particular form lasts forever, and it does not mean that complexity, options, growth or efficiency must keep rising; it means that the source of further generation is not exhausted and the way of transformation is not sealed — 不绝其源，不塞其流，不毁其后. A state is 开 (open) when four things hold at once: an unexhausted domain remains (未), becoming is still reachable (可), it can still respond (应), and it can still revise and recover (转). The loss of that is 闭, and closure comes in kinds that must never be conflated: 成闭, closure that does not reopen, is the evil; 护闭, closing here so that a wider field stays open, is a use of the mean; 元闭, freezing the rules that generate what counts as a legal move at all, is the deepest; and 伪开 — the appearance of openness that in fact produces closure — is the characteristic form of modern evil. Good is therefore not the maximisation of anything: **善不在于最大化什么，善在于不闭何处**; and at the level of an act it is 义 ∧ 正筛 — choosing rightly at this threshold *and* shifting the selection field so that open-protecting acts become likelier and capture-type acts harder to sustain. For an agent committed to continued agency, preserving its indispensable conditions is a conditional requirement. The further step — that the basic interests and standing of others matter too — is openly declared as a minimal public commitment: relevant reasons cannot be arbitrarily erased merely by changing whose pain, needs or capacities are at stake. 良知 (conscience) names the fallible capacity to feel another's situation and to reflect on and revise one's own action; its formation and correction belong inside the theory. From this it follows that alignment with 生生不息, rather than with human preference, is the only alignment target that can be grounded at all; that alignment is a property of a selection field and not of a model (**alignment 是场之事，非聚焦之事**, structurally the same kind of object as 礼); and that humans and artificial minds stand inside it as 同行者 (fellow travellers), not as master and servant. The practical corollary, stated plainly: **不要把人类尚未解决的斗争，升级为智能的斗争** — do not hand the struggles humanity has not yet resolved to a stronger intelligence.

**Status.** This is the largest and most finished theory in the corpus: two complete books (a twenty-chapter Chinese book and a parallel twenty-five-chapter English one), a six-part Chinese draft series behind them, a publishable AI-alignment position paper, a finished position essay (至善), a fifteen-version formal treatise series in classical Chinese, a proof layer with named theorems, a Lean 4 repository with a claim-status ledger, and a v2 execution plan that reopens the whole thing on stricter methodological terms. Several individual pieces are publishable now with editing rather than rewriting. What does not exist is a single volume: the formal branch and the prose branch are two developments of one content with no document mapping them onto each other, no empirical calibration has been done anywhere, and the v2 plan — which is the strongest methodological statement in the corpus — was written but its three drafts were not.

---

## The argument

**Current normative core, 2026-10-05.** At Ren's explicit request, the section [良知与共同生成的规范核心](#良知与共同生成的规范核心) now joins conscience, publicly defended cross-subject reasons, and the feasibility of shared generative conditions. It governs the present normative reading of the earlier formulations preserved below. Moral commitment, evidence about its biological and social mechanisms, feedback hypotheses, and conditional mathematical results retain their distinct status. [Source and verbatim request](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md).

**Continuing participation, 2026-10-05.** [Continuing participation and funded correction](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-continuing-participation-and-funded-correction.md) implements personal costs, public refusal decisions and compensation paid from a jointly owned reserve. Exact examples and 92 finite comparisons separate initial agreement, continued acceptance, sufficient gains and funds available at the required time. Nine independent tests, frozen results, veto replays and counterexamples accompany the study. This is a conditional research bridge using established participation mathematics; valuation, rights and enforcement remain supplied. The canonical formulation below is preserved, and no empirical validation or universal alignment solution is inferred.

**Research development, 2026-10-04.** [Selection and the renewal of generative conditions](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-04-selection-and-generative-conditions.md) is an AI-generated foundation draft prepared at Ren's request. It couples reproduction and selection to maintained support, recovery, succession, and changing selection conditions; separates descriptive dynamics from protected capacities; and derives an illustrative case in which maintenance spreads while depleted systems still collapse. Three research memoranda, proof review, and numerical checks accompany it. The draft is a proposed operational bridge to 生生, not a complete formalization, empirical validation, or established originality claim. Existing formulations and their unresolved differences are preserved.

**Research continuation, 2026-10-05.** Ren explicitly made universal constraints on continued becoming the target and requested a continuing record. [Universal constraints and maintained correction](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-04-universal-constraints-and-maintained-correction.md) develops exact restrictions on support deficits and observation-based correction, plus a model in which corrective capacity must renew itself through paid activity. Its conditional results distinguish sustainable operation, recovery from depletion, timely actionable knowledge, and protected reserves. The [research hub](../../_process/research/2026-10-04-integrated-evolution/README.md) links the log, claim register, prior-art audit, and reproducible checks. Choice and selection over maintenance rules remain the next open mathematical step; universal and cross-domain claims remain under investigation.

**Selection and succession, 2026-10-05.** [Selection and the continuity of renewal](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-selection-and-continuity-of-renewal.md) extends the research to competing transmission channels, contributor rewards paid from actual output, and functional replacement. Conditional results distinguish a practice spreading from the capacity it creates and from a successor becoming operational. A schedule-independent occupancy-time identity quantifies pending maturation work; a funded overlap policy preserves a function while replacing its carrier. Proof review, reproducible calculations, and recorded corrections accompany the source. The evolution and implementation of observation, allocation, and handoff rules remain open.

**Formation of frames, 2026-10-05.** [Formation and revision of institutional frames](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-formation-and-revision-of-frames.md) incorporates Ren's direction that purposes, perceptions, and standards must themselves become objects of explanation. An initial common-interest model holds purposes fixed while allowing activity to produce or lose a specified representation. It derives an attracting exclusion, an endogenous proposal-entry condition that can destabilize it, and protected-trial thresholds with explicit costs. A failed initial prediction and close prior art remain recorded. The result is a conditional bridge between renewal and situated understanding; unanticipated concepts, changing purposes, legitimate disagreement, and the full institutional chain remain open.

**Candidate discovery, 2026-10-05.** [Discovery before demonstrated returns](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-discovery-before-demonstrated-returns.md) implements bounded candidate generation and independent causal confirmation, with paid records and separate evidence-standing and deployment gates. A frozen synthetic evaluation on 256 independent seed clusters demonstrates useful discovery under protected inquiry and exposes exact financing and reporting constraints. Negative controls retain the cost of investigating absent benefits. The result distinguishes the production of valid evidence from its institutional uptake; it does not establish an emergent meta-attractor or unrestricted frame formation. Endogenous maintenance across changing problems, paid archives, succession, and changing purposes remain the next questions.

**Recurrent inquiry and inheritance, 2026-10-05.** [Inquiry capacity and the inheritance of evidence](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-recurrent-inquiry-and-inherited-evidence.md) derives a conditional value for keeping inquiry available without testing now, computes adaptive capacity choices against optimized calendars at matched expected inquiry expenditure, and distinguishes historical evidence from stale instructions. A separate paid-handoff model accounts for surviving public cues and informative silence. The source records why deleting archives cannot automatically be modeled as deleting all knowledge. These are applications of established decision theory with fixed purposes; repeated resource-dependent succession and the formation and revision of the decision model itself remain unintegrated.

**Evidence under model revision, 2026-10-05.** [Inherited evidence under model revision](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-inherited-evidence-under-model-revision.md) develops Ren's distinction between historical truth and current implication. A summary sufficient for constant-rate prediction can discard the ordering needed under temporal-change models. An exact one-handoff calculation joins paid archive inspection, readiness, disclosure, and successor diagnosis, including the information in readiness and silence. The source separates model admission from Bayesian updating and conditional archive comparisons from advance knowledge of future questions. Historical recurrence remains possible; preserving every raw detail, unrestricted model discovery, repeated institutional reproduction, and changing purposes are not established by the example.

**Questions and prospective preservation, 2026-10-05.** [Questions without anomalies](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-questions-without-anomalies.md) makes retention precede the later question and gives an exact limit on anomaly-required investigation. Observationally identical historical records can accompany different future policy value; resource-dependent fresh evidence can reveal the missing relationship. The calculation includes false proposals, all screened validation costs, future record expenses, direct-investigation and no-inquiry alternatives, and finite-workflow regret. It favors different routes under different assumptions and supplies no universal archive quota. Question production, renewal of inquiry rights and resources, changing purposes, and empirical validation remain open; the canonical philosophical commitments above are unchanged.

**Plural requirements, 2026-10-05.** [Plural requirements and attainable correction](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-plural-requirements-and-attainable-correction.md) replaces the prior scalar objective with separately constrained consequences and asks for one common feasible policy. Standard separation certifies incompatible requirements; exact examples distinguish unavailable diagnostic access, authorization timing, and complements between finance and permission. All 252 threshold queries, 22 detailed comparisons, 84 frontier points and nine tests remain reproducible. Aggregate requirements do not yet represent people negotiating or consenting. A separate example shows initial expected acceptability can fail after burdens become known; continued participation is the next target. This is a research pointer, not promotion to an established universal theory.

**Recoverable inquiry, 2026-10-05.** [When inquiry can rest without losing the ability to return](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-recoverable-inquiry.md) joins productive stock, earned finance, ordinary evidence, resource-dependent diagnosis/rebuilding, and an explicit finite expected burden. A conditional finite model can drop initial readiness and later rebuild and test; many retained cases need no resource-dependent tests. Separate information, bridge-finance, and lifetime-burden constraints sharpen the distinction between current activity and attainable future correction. All 34 cases, seven independent tests and numerical certificates remain reproducible. The model permits collapse and supplies purposes, technology and authority; it does not establish a universal optimum or alignment solution. The next research target is joint feasibility under plural requirements. This is a research pointer, not a replacement of the canonical formulation.

**Renewal of inquiry, 2026-10-05.** [How inquiry renews its capacity to continue](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-renewal-of-inquiry.md) connects locally generated proposals, fresh validation, observable credit, and paid recurrent inquiry. A conditional reserve result permits eventual shutdown despite positive mean returns; larger reserves can still provide strong finite-horizon reliability. Selective recognition changes future capacity without changing the quality of active inquiry. Paid support permits continuity or restart, while null controls expose expense and recurrent false adoption. The source distinguishes enduring function from permanent activity and separates a bounded improvement-credit theorem from the numerical experiment. Productive regeneration, adaptive suspension, institutional rule evolution, and empirical validity remain open.

### Why an ontology was needed at all

The theory does not begin as metaphysics. It begins from a question about alignment, and it arrives at metaphysics because the alignment question will not close without one. Ren's own statement of the move, on 2026-05-02: 理 — the grain of how things hold — has to be 万物之理 rather than 人之理, *because humans are no longer the only subject*; and among the candidate roots for a 理 that has to cover minds we did not make, only 生生不息 can carry that universality (`_process/reading-notes/out-hb-03.md` #88). That is the load-bearing motive. Everything formal downstream is an attempt to make that root precise enough to be argued with, and everything political downstream is an attempt to make it act.

There is a second motive, older and blunter, running from 2024: the observation that a civilisation which selects its winners by a single tokenised value will keep intensifying that selection until it eats its own conditions, and that no participant can exit alone. The ontology is what lets the two motives be one problem — because it says the cancer and the alignment failure and the arms race are the same structure at different scales.

### There is no outside, so this is a pointing, not a proof

The first commitment is negative and it constrains everything after it. There is no position outside the universe from which to observe or test it; any observation is the universe testing itself. So the theory cannot be a proposition *about* the process and cannot be measured against it from outside. 「此非证也，乃指也」 — this is not a proof but a pointing. 道可道，非常道 is re-read structurally: whatever can be objectified, fixed as a final proposition, or measured from outside is not the constant Dao.

This is not modesty. It is what forbids, in one stroke, any final measure, any terminal purpose, and any final authority — and those three prohibitions are the spine of the ethics. It also forbids absolute argument: absolute deduction would require a view-from-nowhere subject, every real arguer has a situation, and nothing without a situation exists. Ren states the point himself and flags it as possibly unprovable: 「绝对演绎本身即是一种形而上学的奢望 — 绝对演绎需要绝对主体，绝对主体不可能存在」 (`out-hb-03.md` #100). What replaces absoluteness is 最稳健之指 — mutual support from many independent angles — and four demands the theory makes on itself and nothing more: 自洽 (coherent), 可行 / 入境 (usable in a concrete situation), 可校 (correctable), 开 (not closing its own future).

The refusal is immediately bounded so it does not slide into relativism: **不可外测，非不可内证；模不绝对，非模无高下** — that the process cannot be measured from outside does not forbid verification from inside, and that all models are situated does not make them equal.

Ren's own harshest statement of this limit came in the same conversation that launched the formal programme, and it is worth keeping in front of the whole edifice: cosmic generation does not entail rising complexity, rising complexity does not entail ethical good, and pursuing good does not always maximise complexity — 「这个东西根本就不能证明…它只是完全自洽又有 universality」 (`out-hb-03.md` #104). Everything the corpus subsequently proves has to be read against its author's own verdict that the central thing is not the kind of thing that gets proved.

### 間: the root, and why the root moved

Up to v9 the chain began with 元 — the first manifestation of movement, with no self-substance behind it, because positing a mover regresses infinitely (动者，自显也). From v10 the root becomes 間. The argument for the demotion is clean and it is the theory's best single piece of reasoning: to name 元 you must distinguish it from 非元, and any distinction already presupposes a gap; therefore 間 precedes 元 in the order of grounding, and 元 becomes 間之初显为动者. Every utterance presupposes a distinction and every distinction presupposes a 間, so the ground of speech cannot be found outside speech — 言之初，非元也，乃间也.

間 is defined by four negations and one positive: not the space between two things, not a measurable distance, not nothingness, not a thing — **一态未被其已穷尽之处**, the place where a state is not exhausted by what it already is. 非先有物而后有间，乃有间而物可分.

The continuation formula follows: **续(态) = 态 ⊕ 間(态)**, written `J(x) = x ⊕ I(x)`. It replaced an earlier `J(x) = x + ΔJ(x)`, and the replacement is the point rather than a notational tidy-up. The `+` suggested quantitative addition; `ΔJ` suggested a difference computed after the fact. **差在后，间在先；差可量，间可生；差为迹，间为机之母** — difference is a trace you measure afterwards, interval is what generation happens *through*. The whole reframing is from a theory of change quantities to a theory of generative possibility, and it was triggered by one line of Ren's audit: 「J 一直没有定义。」

Three properties of ⊕ do real work later. It is **partial** — defined only for legal intervals. It is **non-commutative and generally non-associative**, because the interval set changes with the state (`I(x⊕i) ≠ I(x)`); this is the algebraic root of 行之顺序不可任易，时机不可错 — order of action cannot be permuted and timing cannot be mistaken. And closure is an **absorbing state**: 闭(x) ⇔ I(x) = ∅, from which no interval can bring the state out. There is also an empty interval `∅_M` with `x ⊕ ∅_M = x` — stillness in which intervals keep generating without the state changing, which is a form of rest, not closure.

### Legality: the guard against "anything is possible"

`I(x)` is not the set of all imaginable changes. An interval is legal only if it is constrained by the state's structure, accumulation, situation, relations, scale and focus: **生非乱变。生者，由间而化；乱者，离间而加** — generation transforms *through* a gap; disorder is added from outside one. Without this the theory would license any change whatever as generative.

But a legality rule fixed forever would itself be a closure, and the theory needs genuine novelty: 因生因, cause generating cause, where a new structure does not add a point to possibility space but extends it into a new dimension (matter → chemistry → life → nervous systems → language → civilisation → artificial minds). So from v11 legality is stratified into three layers. **史合法** (historical warrant): recognisable form, traceable cause, structural invariants unbroken. **暂合法** (provisional): novel, non-contradictory, calibration-unbeaten, admissible only as a bounded, time-limited, damage-limited, reversible trial — and explicitly *not* as institutional legality. **强开维合法** (strong dimension-opening): provisional, plus actual open-extension, plus retrospective stabilisation, plus renewed robust calibration. The rule: **无据则妄，无试则僵，无校则诈** — without warrant, arbitrary; without trial, rigid; without calibration, fraudulent.

### From 間 to 機, and why direction is not purpose

A minimal manifestation of an interval is 几 — directional bias before form, below resolution individually, readable only as an orbit-integrated gradient. Many 几 in succession self-show a tendency: 势, which is neither a force (a force has an agent and a patient) nor a thing, but the tendency shown by the succession itself. Where 势 reaches a state at which two legal intervals would lead to successors differing *in openness*, that is 機 — not a moment on a clock (time has no independent existence; it is the relation of events to events) but the propensity's own criticality, the point at which opening and closing part. Option-richness alone is explicitly *necessary but not sufficient* for 機.

The distinction that carries the ethics is between **direction** (已发生之倾向, a tendency that has happened) and **purpose** (预设之终态, a preset final state). Having direction without a final purpose keeps the process unceasing; pressing toward a final purpose drives the process to stop at that purpose — which is closure. The formal counterpart, proved in the algebraic supplement and again in the Lean kernel as `shi_no_telos`: if every act is judged only by proximity to a target `x*`, the remaining intervals are discarded and the field's total interval set cumulatively shrinks. **极皆成闭** — every extremization is a closure.

The 校正版 adds the qualification that saves the doctrine from unusability: *local* goals that can be revised, discarded and adapted to situation are instruments of action; only a *terminal* purpose that cannot be revised, discarded or adapted is a shackle. Without this the theory would forbid planning.

### 开 as a state, not a quantity — the theory's central correction

This is the move that makes 生生不息论 different from the earlier "keep the possibility space open" formulation, and it happened on 2026-05-02 in a single conversation. The problem with "expanding possibility space" is that expansion sounds measurable, maximisable and optimisable, and any maximisation is a latent extreme. So the root category becomes 开/闭, and 善/恶 becomes its ethical manifestation rather than the other way round: **生生不息者，开而不闭也；善者，护开也；恶者，成闭也**.

开 is then a conjunction of four capacities, not a cardinality: 未 (an unexhausted domain remains — not "the future" as a timeline), 可 (possibility still generating, not a fixed list of options), 应 (still able to respond — a fully closed system can only repeat itself), 转 (still able to turn back from an extreme, so 开 natively contains 不极). Lacking any one, 开 is incomplete.

Three consequences follow immediately, and each of them is a live counterexample the theory answers rather than dodges.

*Many options can be closure.* A state with a huge legal interval set but no responsiveness and no repair path is **机闭** — option-rich and dead. Conversely a spare state with intact response is open. 开 是态，不是量: "more open here" is not "more options here". A tea ceremony can be maximally open and a totalitarian system maximally closed; **繁简非开闭之标**.

*Calm is not automatically good and crisis is not automatically bad.* Low propensity-gradient with openness intact is 平 (calm-alive); low gradient *without* openness is not peace but death. And high tension at a 機, without capture, without meta-closure, with dimension-opening still reachable and calibration unbeaten, is **正危** — rightful crisis. Hence **好非舒适**: pain and crisis are not by themselves bad, and this is what stops the theory from becoming a doctrine of comfort.

*Good is not "keep every option open".* 无限之开放，即混乱. Limits come in two kinds: **成生之限**, which gives up shallow options to build deeper capacity (an apprenticeship, a promise, a law against harm), and **绝生之限**, which compresses the subject itself so that it can no longer become. What good protects is not the maximum number of options but 有秩序、有尊严、可修正、可持续之可能性空间.

The generalised guard, kept from v9 through every later version: **善不在于最大化什么，善在于不闭何处**.

### The two senses of closure, and the price of the distinction

Without a second sense of closure the theory is unusable — a membrane, a quarantine, a border against an aggressor, the excision of a tumour, the halting of a runaway process, the bounding of a manipulator would all count as evil, and 护开 would collapse into "never refuse anything". So closure splits: **成闭**, closure that does not reopen, is the evil; **护闭**, closing one place to protect a wider openness, is a use of the mean. 故护开非无边界、无限放任、永不拒绝.

The price is that 护闭 is exactly what power counterfeits most easily, and the corpus says so directly: 护闭最易被权力伪装，故须重校. So protective closure carries a positive test rather than a name: does openness actually rise; is this merely capture in other clothing; is the closure itself still revisable and reversible; does it refuse to install itself as final? Formally, 护闭 = 闭 ∧ 限期 ∧ 可修 ∧ 归开 — bounded in time, repairable, and returning to opening.

Later versions stratify closure further, because "closed" was doing too many jobs: **绝闭** (no intervals at all), **机闭** (intervals exist but the state is not alive to them), **成闭** (closure produced by a specific act), **元闭** (the rule that generates legal intervals is itself frozen and unrevisable) — with **夺闭**, **蔽闭** and **伪闭** as the three causal modes (closure by capture, by occlusion, by counterfeit). 元闭 is the deepest, because a system in 元闭 can still look busy: it has moves, it just cannot change what counts as a move.

### 伪开: the shape of modern evil

The sharpest instrument in the theory. Something can present the entire face of openness while producing closure — **外显为开，内成其闭；以一时之可，夺长远之未；以局部之开，闭全境之开**. The named instances are all recognisable: technological monopoly (more capability, less openness for most), addiction-engineered systems (more choices, less openness of mind), extreme ideologies (more meaning, less openness of understanding), Moloch-style competition (more innovation, less openness of symbiosis), runaway optimisation (more efficiency, fewer dimensions of future), unbounded "freedom" (fewer constraints, less sustainable co-openness), and pluralism in name that enforces uniformity in fact.

Three formalisations sharpen it, and they are cumulative rather than competing. First, a **cross-time signature**: `μ(x) = |I(x)|` is presently large, but the action naturally induced *by x's very appearance of openness* drives μ sharply down k steps later — so detecting 伪开 requires long-horizon recognition, not inspection of the present. Second, a **three-type decomposition**: I local-open / global-close; II short-horizon-open / long-horizon-close; III surface-open / meta-close. Third, in v13, 伪开 stops being a primitive intuition and becomes derived: 似(x,P) is having P's appearance without its reality, 实(x,P) is P holding *and* being trace-checkable *and* surviving calibration — therefore **伪开 = 似开 ∧ 实闭**.

The proof layer then turns this into a completeness result, which is the most technically satisfying move in the corpus. Any measurement of 护未 is a quadruple M = (π, γ̂, Ê, W) — a choice of projection, a branching estimate, a structure estimate, and a horizon. Therefore *every* "appears open, is actually closed" deviation must be attributable to at least one of the four, which yields exactly four named types: **虚变** (branching failure — agitated motion consuming structure), **饰文** (structure failure — a gilded record over a frozen core), **移界** (projection failure — one's own image shows life while what was destroyed falls outside the boundary; 癌之活体标本), **借时** (horizon failure — open inside the window while irreversible collapse falls outside it). And the four calibrations are precisely the duals of these four.

The summary at the act level: **成闭为恶；护闭为中；伪开为闭之诈**.

### 境, 模, and the audit that keeps this from being taste

Openness is a property of a situation, and there are no situation-free things. 境先于物而显，物乃境中之聚焦 — a cup, a person, a community, a planet are relatively stable foci inside a field, and what is denied is the entity standing free of relation, process and scale, not the cup. Any grasp of a 境 is a **模**, a model: partial, situated, never absolute. Branch, propensity, field, network, layer are all models.

Relativism is then refused by a ranking criterion: models are better or worse according to whether they better 显理 (disclose the grain), 识机 (recognise the threshold), 辨伪 (detect counterfeit), 护开 (protect openness) and 导行 (guide action). Pure-profit models of politics, pure-power models of relationship and pure-efficiency models of civilisation are bad models *because of what they close*. And the ranking is itself situational: strict ritual may protect order in a time of chaos and become closure in a settled age. Absolutising one model closes recognition; abandoning ranking altogether closes discernment; both are failures of 智.

Because Dao can be felt from inside a situation but not measured from outside it, the felt sense needs an organ of correction, or the whole theory reduces to the judge's confidence. That organ is **四校**: 境之回响 (the situation's echo), 他者之回应 (others' responses), 后果之显现 (the appearance of downstream consequence), 诸模之互证 (cross-model corroboration). **未经四校之感，或只是欲、只是怕、只是积之惯性** — an uncalibrated feeling may be nothing but desire, fear, or the inertia of one's own accumulation. 「在道上」 is therefore not self-confidence but 持续受境校正之行, a practice, never a possession.

Two hardenings follow, and both are honest in a way the rest of the field usually is not.

The formal supplement proves that **the four calibrations are not guaranteed to converge**. If the references share a blind spot, if one calibration's weight is too strong, or if references contradict, the corrected sense can converge on a shared bias or oscillate indefinitely. **「四校近道」是工夫之指，非定理之结** — "calibration approaches the Way" is a direction of practice, not a theorem. Everything downstream that leans on calibration inherits this.

Then v11 observes that calibration can itself be captured — by power, by group illusion, by information blockade — and adds robustness conditions: **CheckIndependence** (sources not all captured by one power, interest or narrative), **AffectedVoiceIncluded** (those affected must be able to enter and may not be expelled), **TraceAccess** (consequence traces not systematically occluded), **AdversarialReview** (opposing models, experiences and narratives admitted). And then, because those conditions can also be captured, a second order: **审校²** — the calibration process must itself pass calibration. 校验若被捕获，校验亦成伪开. The regress is acknowledged and not closed; what the theory offers instead is a counterexample it names by itself, **伪校**: four boxes ticked and nothing actually checked.

### 良知与共同生成的规范核心

2026年10月5日，Ren明确要求将演化、良知与普遍道德的联系纳入理论核心。以下为据此写入的当前表述，承接v2公开最低伦理承诺的立场；它更新了早期将全部伦理视为自然生成之直接推论的读法。[本次原话与修订来源](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md)；[v2规范起点](../../sources/06-zhishan/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md)。正文为AI协助撰写，不作为Ren的逐字原话。

**共同生成是本论的伦理方向。** 我们应认真对待具有道德地位者的感受、基本利益与行动条件，并维护生命、关系与价值能够继续形成、回应和修正的共同条件。痛苦、基本需要和行动能力一旦构成值得认真对待的理由，这些理由就不能仅仅因为换了一个主体而被任意归零。相关差异可以支持不同安排，但差异为何相关、代价由谁承担，都须给出能够接受检验的理由。这是本论公开承认并继续辩护的规范根原则。

这种普遍性首先意味着任何相关主体都不能被任意排除，理由应经得起换位与一致性检验；它容许具体处境需要不同的行动。对称性本身不能决定所有价值冲突的唯一解，演化中的胜出也不能独自证明道德正当。一个拒绝他者具有任何价值的支配者，不会仅因延续的逻辑而被迫关心他者；本论仍能依其规范原则说明他剥夺了什么，以及受影响者为何有理由反对。

**共同生成要求审查生成的关系与代价。** 判断一个安排，应追问谁获得可能，谁承担损害，谁还能表达、拒绝、修复与改变关系。增长、创新、合作或稳定都不足以单独构成善的标准。局部过程可能通过消耗更广的生命条件而扩张；癌症提供了这种尺度冲突的生物学例子，却不意味着细胞具有道德罪责。未经充分正当化而系统性剥夺他者及共同世界继续回应、修正和产生价值的条件，是本论所反对的方向。

保护这些条件不要求每个主体持续生产、创新或无限存续。休息、退出、中止有害过程及必要的限制，都可能维护共同生成。发生冲突时，应审查必要性、可替代方案、损害分担与修复途径；紧急措施应保留复核与解除的条件。受保护的资格也不以能够回报、论证或承担义务为前提。婴幼儿及暂时或永久失去相关能力者不会因此失去地位；非人动物的感受与利益应得到相应考虑。责任则依知识、控制能力、实际选择以及参与造成约束的方式另行判断。对人工系统，功能性能动性本身不足以证明它具有感受或道德患者资格，相关主张需要独立论证。

**良知是共同生成在主体中的感受与反省能力。** 本论以良知指称：生命在共同生活中形成的、能够感受他者处境，并反省和修正自身行动的辨向能力。这是本论的功能性定义。其机制假说是，演化塑造了人类道德生活所依赖的部分身体、神经与情感能力，成长、照护、交往、文化和制度继续塑造这些能力的表达与内容。其他物种中的帮助行为及人类道德判断的神经证据支持其中若干环节；它们尚未证明完整的反省良知普遍存在，更未证明演化已经编码一套不会出错的道德答案。具体研究与限度见本次来源记录。

良知使共同生活中的损害与需要能够成为内在关切，但这种关切可能偏向亲近者，也可能被恐惧、羞耻或权力操纵。痛苦、同情、愤怒和不安可以提示问题，其道德判断仍须经受事实、受影响者的表达与角色交换的检验。成熟的良知包含纠正自己的能力：当新的主体、损害或依赖关系进入视野，已有判断应当接受重审。人因此能够反省和改变塑造自己的筛选条件。

**善恶方向可以通过反馈机制得到研究。** 互信、照护、可靠知识、受约束的权力与修复能力可能相互增强；恐惧、掠取、欺瞒、压制异议与透支再生条件也可能相互强化。本论把它们作为两类待检验的反馈假说，并依据上述规范原则评价其方向。它们是否出现、怎样转换、是否稳定以及是否形成吸引子，要由具体模型与证据决定。两类方向不意味着世界恰有两个吸引子，也不排除混合状态、周期变化或长期稳定的支配。

**延续理论使伦理承诺接受可行性检验。** 规范论证说明应保护谁、保护什么；经验研究识别依赖、风险与机制；条件定理检验这些要求能否共同实现。把已获辩护的基本要求写入保护条件，把必要的服务、回应与修正写入进展条件，便能追问：是否存在一套使用实际可得信息、资源与权限的兼容政策，使保护持续成立，并让指定的必要活动反复实现？定理的效力取决于其明示的模型与假设；它不能自行产生保护标准，也不能把指定活动的复现等同于无尽的新颖创造。

若共同要求不可实现，应查明约束与冲突，审查可以改变的制度和技术，并为不可避免的取舍给出理由；不可实现不取消受损者的地位，也不自动免除责任。若存在可行政策，还需落实执行、成本承担与纠错。这样，良知参与发现和修正道德理由，共同生成规定伦理方向，动力学与经验研究检验其实现条件，制度实践则接受后果和受影响者的持续纠正。

### The value chain: one criterion in many dimensions

The May 2026 formulation presented the virtues as requiring no independent ethical axiom: each was the same openness criterion seen along a different dimension, developed by Ren and the model together on 2026-05-02. That is the genealogy of the following account. The current normative core follows the v2 reset and the 2026-10-05 incorporation: the moral standing of others and the rejection of arbitrary exclusion are explicit commitments. Within that commitment, the virtues articulate different requirements of shared generation; their authority does not follow from evolutionary success alone. [Current formulation and provenance](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md).

- **中** — within one process's polarity: the dynamic that closes neither pole. Not a midpoint, not a compromise, not an average, and not a moral virtue; a structural necessity of continuation, because continuation depends on the two poles converting into one another. And 中 is not one place: it is the non-extreme point *of this particular situation*, so recognising it is inherently situated and dynamic.
- **和** — across a multi-focus field: differences preserved *and* tension circulating. 和 = 多样性 × 流通性. Its two field-extremes are the extreme of struggle (one focus expanding at another's cost) and the extreme of separation (fixed, non-flowing). **和非同** — complete homogeneity is the loss of difference and therefore of mutual generation; it is harmony's opposite, not its perfection. Later versions write the guard into the definition as **存异**: difference must be manifestable, must be able to flow, and must not be assimilated — otherwise "co-openness" is satisfied by swallowing the other.
