- **美** — when a mind meets an event: the state in which a mind's own possibility expands. Not conformity to norm (正), not usefulness (利), not satisfaction of desire (快). Beauty always sits at a fine mean: perfect symmetry is not beautiful because 极同则趣闭, and complete chaos is not beautiful either.
- **德** — along time and accumulation: a focus whose 积 (character, habit, response pattern) has stabilised toward the mean, so that fitting response no longer needs recomputation from zero. Not an external norm and not an innate gift, but the tendency of accumulation.
- **仁** — in relation: the only non-contracting mode of one focus meeting another — not as resource, not as obstacle, not as identical with oneself, not as absolute other, but as **同根异显**, another concrete form of the same process. 仁 = 关系中之「中」. Formalised: **己续 ⊆_实 共续 without 夺依** — one's own conditions of continuation are really contained within the shared conditions, with no element of one's continuation depending on another's suppression.
- **义** — at this threshold: recognising and enacting the mean of *this* situation's propensity. 仁定其向，义具其行 — benevolence fixes the direction, rightness supplies the act. Formally 可行机 ∧ 可择 ∧ 正行: there is a real bifurcation, one can choose, and one chooses the right interval.
- **礼** — as transmissible form: the stable, repeatable shape of fitting action, necessary because nobody can recompute the mean from zero each time. But 礼必含礼之自我修订之机制 — a ritual without a self-revision mechanism becomes **死礼**, and 死礼 is the concrete form of 不仁不义: 以礼杀人. Formally, a ritual pattern lacking a revision operator ρ, which keeps recommending action out of accord with 义 with no correction path — the structural definition of a tradition that has become 元闭. 其名虽礼，其实礼之尸.
- **智** — as capacity to recognise: cross-scale recognition, long-horizon recognition, multi-agent recognition, detection of pseudo-openness, discrimination of protective from achieved closure, recognition of models' situatedness and their ranking, and calibration of one's own feeling. Its core is reflexive: **不识自己之识之局限者，不为智者**. And 智 ≠ 知识 — knowledge is 积; wisdom is the use of 积 in a context it was not accumulated in.
- **信** — as internal coherence: through-coherence of a focus across speech, action, knowledge, feeling and accumulation. A split focus is already misaligned, because it has internally contracted into mutually opposed fragments. 信非不变 — changing with reason is 信; clinging to the old while deceiving oneself is its loss.

The five interpersonal ones are not five virtues but one alignment in five dimensions, and each is hollow without the others. The most usable single passage in the corpus says so: **无仁之义即冷酷，无义之仁即滥爱；无礼之仁义难传，无仁义之礼即僵；无智之仁义礼即盲，无仁义礼之智即诡；无信之上述四者皆为表演** — rightness without benevolence is cruelty, benevolence without rightness is indiscriminate indulgence, benevolence and rightness without ritual cannot be transmitted, ritual without them is rigid, all three without wisdom are blind, wisdom without them is cunning, and without 信 all four are performance.

Three later values complete the axis and they are the formal branch's distinctive contribution. **平**: `‖势‖ < θ ∧ 开` — calm *and* alive, sharply separated from calm-and-dead. **正危**: high tension at a 機 without capture, meta-closure or lost calibration. And then **好 is an interval, not a point**: 好 ⇔ 平 ∨ 和 ∨ 正危, with 不好 as the exterior on *both* sides — closure-by-stagnation below (落于死) and capture-by-violence above (入于暴). Choosing between 平 and 和 is therefore wisdom, not morality: **the polarity within the good is not a polarity between good and bad**.

From 好, freedom is derived as its individual-scale projection: **自由 = 不向坏**. `IntentionGood ⇒ Free` but not conversely — freedom is the necessary floor, good the supererogatory direction. A constrained focus can be free; an unconstrained focus disposed toward capture is not. And freedom is given a reality condition that most negative-liberty accounts lack: the option set must be actually reachable given the agent's resources, capabilities, knowledge, institutional position, bodily condition and risk tolerance, must not be coerced away, and must be *discernible* — **自由者，非纸上有路，乃现实可达、可识、且不被坏径所迫**. The quantifier was also deliberately weakened: an earlier draft required *every* available choice to be non-bad, which is too strong; the settled form requires a non-empty non-bad path set, not coerced, recognisable, and traceable to the agent's own structure.

Then **繁荣** (prosperity): all foci free ∧ co-open ∧ relationally flowing. "Freedom for all" is only half a definition; the other half is "without mutual capture", because otherwise free agents compose into a Hobbesian field that is collectively closed. 诸自由共开而不相食. Explicitly not GDP, not a metric, not quantity of accumulation.

And the ladder, each row strictly stronger than the last: **自由 → 平 → 和 → 繁荣 → 生生不息**, with the summary **不向坏者，自由也；向好者，道也；自由和平，是为存在之道**. Ren derived this himself on 2026-05-03, and derived it as an argument *for peace*: ∇平 is a field not perturbed, 和 is a field whose perturbation is open, therefore 和平 is a direction and at the material and structural level a constraint; our intention interval should lie between 平 and 和, and that is 仁 (`out-hb-03.md` #127–131).

Two more terms complete the top of the ladder. **夺** (capture) has both an act form and a state form; the state form is the important one — one focus's openness *structurally depends* on the suppression of relevant others' openness. **夺者，非彼此差异，乃以彼之闭为己之开** — capture is not that we differ, it is that your closure is my opening; mere co-occurrence of one rising and another falling is not 夺. And **道** is given a subject so that nothing persisting inside a good field can call itself the Way by default: 道⁰ = sufficient continuation capacity; 道⁺ = contributes non-negatively to co-openness, does not worsen the bad, does not gain by capture; **真道** additionally requires the judgement to close over all *relevant* scales and horizons and to survive calibration. **道非物自称道，乃其存在方式在相关尺度中不夺共续.** The universal quantifier was deliberately restricted from "all scales" to "all *relevant* scales" because the unrestricted form is almost unsatisfiable: 道非万度无代价，乃于相关度期中，不以一端之续闭其共续.

### Scale non-transitivity, and why governance is sieve-correction

Here the theory earns its keep. Evolution selects whatever can continue at a given scale and horizon: `Select ∝ ContinuationCapacity(p, Γ, s, t)`. It is tempting to conclude 演化筛选在道上者 — that what survives is what is on the Way. The theory blocks that conclusion with one structural fact: **being on the Way is not transitive across scales**.

> 癌之于细胞，似续；于身，则闭。争之于一己，似胜；于共场，则陷。指标之于机器，似明；于万维生命，则蔽。

Cancer looks like continuation at the scale of the cell and is closure at the scale of the body. Molochian competition looks like winning for one actor and is entrapment for the shared field. Metric optimisation looks like clarity inside the metric space and is occlusion in the full value field. The parallel three-view proof in the repository makes the same point three ways: a **scale view** (what a sieve preserves at one scale-and-window need not be preserved at another — the cancer counterexample), a **value view** (evolution preserves 道⁰, the capacity to continue, but not 道⁺, co-openness and non-capture — the parasite counterexample; "适者生存" quietly collapses 适 into 正), and a **reflexive view** (once a subject exists that can see and change the sieve, there is no longer a pure evolutionary sieve at all: σ = σ^evolution ⊕ σ^R). 真道 is the intersection of all three; drop any one and cancer, parasitism or pseudo-openness enters the set. And the reconciliation is generous rather than triumphal: read with a zero-degree, single-scale, non-reflexive sense of the Way, "evolution selects those on the Way" is not false, it is **未尽** — unfinished.

The governance conclusion follows directly: **治道者，非逆演化，乃正其筛** — the task is not to fight selection but to correct the sieve, so that what survives is not merely one actor's continuation. And once you see it that way, an entire class of things becomes one kind of object: institutions, ritual, education, protocols, culture, markets, law, algorithms and AI alignment are all **正筛**, selection-field shapers. **圣人不强万物以从己，乃正其场，使善者易生，伪开难久，成闭难成** — the sage does not force the myriad things to follow him; he corrects the field so that good is easier to generate, counterfeit openness harder to sustain, and achieved closure harder to accomplish.

This is also why **善** is not only choosing well: 善 = 义 ∧ 正筛, where 正筛 means ΔPr(正行) > 0 ∧ ΔPr(邪行) < 0 ∧ calibration-unbeaten. **善者，使好更易生，使伪开难久，使成闭难成.** Ethics is a matter of reshaping selection pressure, not only of individual acts.

And it is why **责** (responsibility) cannot stop at immediate consequences. An act has three levels of effect — the next state, the next state's interval set, and the *rule* that generates legal intervals — and responsibility is the sum of all three. **行者，择间以续；深行者，亦改后来可择之间.** An act changes not only one thing but what can later be changed.

### The alignment argument

The field's central question is misframed before any engineering begins. The error is not "alignment" but **与什么对齐**: taking "human values" — historically mutable, culturally divergent, capturable, abusable — as the final anchor is not an engineering problem but an ontological dislocation, **用一个不稳定之物作为稳定之锚**.

The diagnosis of the existing programme is structural rather than a complaint about implementation. RLHF's reward hacking follows from compressing preference into a scalar; preference inconsistency and drift defeat every aggregation method; and the training signal is "these annotators' preferences under these conditions", not "human preference". Underneath all three is a **type error**: 行动之合宜性不是分数，合宜性是关系性的、情境性的、过程性的 — the fittingness of an action is not a score; it is relational, situational and processual. Constitutional AI moves "whose preferences" to "whose constitution" without answering it, and principles still require situated interpretation. CEV is closer to the truth because it recognises that present preferences are unreliable, but it still assumes idealised humanity's volition can be identified as a *content*, when humanity may be 本质上是一个未完成的过程，不是一个可被收敛的偏好集. All content-based alignment then fails on three independent axes at once — Hume's is/ought gap, Berlin's value pluralism, and an *extended* frame problem in which the stronger the capability, the further the created contexts from the encoding context, and the worse the misfit. **任何把 alignment 视为「优化向某内容」的方法，在能力扩展之下必然崩塌.**

The positive argument is a **web of three independent pillars**, not a chain, and the point of the structure is that all three would have to be overturned simultaneously and their weak points do not overlap.

*Pillar one — exhaustive reductio.* Ten conceivable targets, each shown to fail. Individual preference: unstable, manipulable. Aggregated values: incommensurable, lossy, Arrow-blocked. A specific ideology: the local passed off as universal. Economic efficiency: a means promoted to an end, which dissolves its own non-market base. Power or strongest-survives: internally anti-生生, because it ends the process at a winner. Survival: reduces life to not-dying. A final state: the teleological error itself — any terminal state is the end of generation. No alignment: not neutral, because an unaligned system defaults to alignment with its training objective, and in a physical world under energy gradients free evolution converges on resource-acquisition maximisation — **任何 agent，在任何环境中，皆有事实上的方向；问题不是有无方向，是何种方向**. Pure acceptance: dissolves the concept. Game-theoretic equilibrium: stability is not fittingness, and many zero-sum equilibria are locally stable and globally terrible. Only alignment with the continuation of the process escapes all ten.

*Pillar two — transcendental and performative.* Any aligning subject is a concrete generated form of the process; its existence depends on the process continuing; therefore any action it takes is already factually in accord with that continuation, which is the minimal form of alignment with 生生不息. Hence 生生不息 alignment is a *condition of possibility* of any alignment whatever, and opposing it uses a capacity that depends on it. The performative version is sharper: any utterance opposing this alignment requires the speaker's existence (the process continuing), its intelligibility (accumulated structure), its efficacy (a future that has not stopped) and its meaning (alternative possibilities) — so the opposition contradicts itself not between two propositions but **between what it does and what it says**. On the is/ought gap the claim is not that ought is derived from is but that the gap is not where it was thought to be: 任何提出「我应当做什么」之问者，其问之提出本身已预设此问者真实存在 — **提出鸿沟之主体本身即在桥之上**. 生生不息 不是一个 ought，是 ought 之可能性条件.

*Pillar three — empirical convergence.* Evolution selects forms able to keep generating; civilisational forms that contract self-collapse (informational closure → bad decisions → brittleness; suppressed innovation → stagnation; ecological over-extraction → collapse); and the deep wisdom traditions converge under different names on the same structure — 儒 (仁; 天地之大德曰生), 道 (道生一…), Buddhist 缘起 and the bodhisattva vow, Greek eudaimonia and logos, the Hebrew-Christian affirmation of abundant life, Hindu dharma, indigenous seven-generation ethics. **不同语言，同一收束.** From which the modest conclusion: this is not a new proposal but the common structure of every stable alignment form, never before named at its own ontological level — 我们不是在发明新的 alignment，我们是在识别一直在那里的 alignment.

Then the engineering consequence, which is the single sharpest move in the corpus. Naive alignment, `Output(a) ≈ Pref(humans)`, is diagnosed as **type-III pseudo-openness**: preference is a bias of accumulated history, not the whole of continuation capacity, so `argmax Pref` runs through Goodhart into surface-open / meta-closed, freezing the very rules that generate legal intervals. What replaces it is **真合 / TrueAlign**: across all relevant scales and horizons, the agent's acts tend to preserve co-openness without pseudo-openness or meta-closure, under calibration — **AI 之合道，非合人之欲，乃合人-AI-地球之共续**. And because 真合 at agent scale is structurally identical to 礼 (both are selection-field shapers), it cannot be installed inside one model: **alignment 不可由 AI 单独完成 —— alignment 是场之事，非聚焦之事**.

Three tiers make it operational: a floor (AI and humans both not oriented toward the bad — i.e. both free), a middle (the field within [平, 和]), an upper (prosperity across AI, humans and the ecosphere). 此即 alignment 与大同之同形.

And the constraint is *stricter* than human-value alignment, not looser: it requires considering all affected agents including non-human ones, all relevant scales, all long-run net effects; **它不允许简单的服从（因为人类指令可能是反生生不息的）**; and it requires independent judgement grounded not in the AI's preferences but in the structure of the process. 不是更危险之 AI，是真正可信之 AI.

The repository adds a failure catalogue read the same way: Goodhart, specification gaming, reward hacking, mesa-optimisation, deceptive alignment, wireheading and power-seeking all reduce to violations of the same kernel invariants — 中 (no orbit collapses to an extreme), 多样 (at least two distinct orbits persist), 流通 (every orbit keeps stepping), 同根 (an action's effect is equivalued across any two middle states, i.e. universalizability). Two corollaries are striking. Wireheading and power-seeking are winner-take-all attractors and any attractor violates 多样, so 解 wireheading ≠ 更好之 reward function; 解 wireheading = 拒 attractor design 本身. And **no stable terminal goal is possible, aligned or misaligned alike** — worrying about an AI holding a stable misaligned goal and hoping for a stable aligned end-state are the same category error.

### The civilisational mechanism

The 2026-07 prose rebuilds the whole content on two new primitives, and the rebuild is not a translation of the formal branch but a different and in some ways better construction.

**象** is a structure that appears in a situation and causally affects what happens next: DNA, an idea, a law, a rite, a narrative, a program, a form in nature, a body, an institution. A structure counts as 象 only when it influences what comes after. **凡显于情境而能生后续者，皆象也；世界者，万象相生之流也.** **像** is a human cognitive construction of some 象 — concept, map, model, theory, narrative — and it exists because a mind must compress reality in order to judge and act, which builds in a permanent humility: 凡我所持者，皆是像，皆可错.

The move that makes this elegant is **像亦象也**: once a 像 is collectively held and enacted, it becomes one of the world's hardest 象. Money, law, states, reputation, identity are 共像所成之象 — neither natural objects nor falsehoods. And this dissolves the subject/object problem without a bridge: **并没有一道「心内」与「世界」之间的鸿沟需要跨越 —— 像的形成本身，就是万象之流的一段**. The world senses itself through life and images itself through mind.

Two by-products fall out. **幻象** — illusion is not powerless. Rumour, prejudice, manufactured enemies and manufactured scarcity can be the strongest 象 of all; a million people believing something makes it socially causal, not true. Its 幻 lies in four things: it mis-maps the structure it claims to represent, it survives only locally, it occludes the 理 that actually decides outcomes, and it defends itself by blocking feedback. And **真** is recast as **共至** (co-arrival): a 像 that keeps responding through changes of situation, can be corrected by counterexample, expresses relations not tied to private interest, and is re-reachable by others testing honestly. Truth stays humanly constructed and stops being arbitrary.

From there the social mechanism runs in five steps, and each one is a claim you could be wrong about.

*Environments generate conduct.* Safety lengthens time horizons and lowers defensive cost; sufficiency restores a boundary to desire; abundance — of knowledge, time, opportunity, trust, public services, not only goods — makes cooperation possible without self-sacrifice. Fear does the reverse: it contracts the circle of "we", so 仁囿于亲，义缩为党，信化为对内部叙事之忠，智被动员为制胜之术 — benevolence narrows to kin, rightness shrinks to faction, trust becomes loyalty to the in-group narrative, and wisdom is conscripted as technique for winning. Hence the reading of 管子's 仓廪实则知礼节 as a claim about governance rather than about the poor: a civilisation may not take "everyone should be a saint in adversity" as its political method — **善不必依英雄之牺牲方能发生，方是良序**.

*Much scarcity is manufactured.* Monopoly makes opportunity scarce, ranking makes status scarce, advertising makes satisfaction scarce, inequality makes safety scarce, algorithms make attention scarce, enemy narratives make belonging scarce. The function is not to answer a real shortfall but to keep the majority permanently short, so that they keep consuming, competing, obeying and accumulating.

*Struggle selects for strugglers.* A system in prolonged contest rewards faster attack, deeper suspicion, less feeling, stricter discipline and simpler enemy narratives, so those who preserve complexity and restraint lose in the short run. **斗争不止耗善，且生产更宜于继续斗争之人** — struggle does not merely consume the good, it produces people better suited to continuing the struggle.

*Local principles become universal ones.* **邪理 = 执一隅之理，冒天下之道** — a local structure passed off as a constant. "All will seize, so I must seize first." "Competition raises efficiency, so every domain should compete." "Accumulation hedges risk, so more is always better." "The strong win, so victory proves rightness." "Markets can price, so everything should be priced." "Algorithms raise engagement, so higher engagement is better." Each contains a local truth with its boundary, its time and its whole-system conditions stripped away. 邪理 has two supreme dangers: it lets people do evil with a clear conscience, because having a "principle" turns predation into pragmatism and cruelty into clear-sightedness; and **it builds the world that proves it** — assume others are enemies, defend and expand pre-emptively, they respond in kind, enmity becomes fact, and you say you saw it coming.

*And the whole thing becomes an attractor.* Totalizing obstruction unfolds in four movements: a method succeeds in a limited context; success gives it resources, prestige and explanatory authority; it reorganises other domains so that their values can only be expressed in its terms; the reorganised world then appears to prove the method indispensable. **The metric has changed the world that now seems to justify the metric.** Capital return comes to define the worth of education, medicine, art and persons; security expands into control of all difference; competition becomes the universal law of persons, schools, states and civilisations; attention becomes the measure of truth, value and existence; group loyalty swallows fact and conscience. Individual exit then costs too much, and the moral conclusion follows: **克恶不能独责志士，必改其构** — you cannot fight evil by demanding heroism; you must change the structure.

Three modern instances are worked through in detail: **资本** (a means promoted to supreme end, so that the unpriceable — care, ecology, trust, leisure, dignity — appears valueless or freely consumable, cumulative advantage turns "many actors competing" into "a few defining the terms of competition", and the system daily teaches people to see themselves as assets requiring perpetual appreciation); **斗争式之全球化** (a connected world organised by capital contest and state contest, where gains flow globally and costs stay local, supply chains join while responsibility chains break, and winners rewrite the rules — so globalisation manufactures its own backlash); and **注意力之平台** (engagement optimisation systematically rewarding the most divisive 象, remaking 仁 into tribal sympathy, 义 into faction-taking, 礼 into interface-written norms of retweet-before-read, 智 into advocacy, and 信 into loyalty to the group's image).

The deepest danger is not incompetence. **它可以极聪明、极高效、极有组织——它会愈来愈擅长复制那些使破坏显得必要的条件** — a degenerative system can be extremely intelligent, efficient and organised, and it will get better and better at reproducing the conditions that make destruction look necessary.

Against which the two cycles: **善环** — safety → trust → cooperation → abundance → sufficiency and slack → wider 仁 and longer 智 → better order and safety; **恶环** — fear → defence → struggle → predation and concentration → scarcity and discontent → collapse of trust → deeper fear. Their difference is not moral taste but structure: the good cycle turns more subjects into generators; the bad cycle turns more subjects into defenders, instruments or exiles.

And the response, **治 = 守生生之向**: what must be governed is not life but the runaway structures that would close it — 所当管者，非生命，乃将绝生之失控结构. Six handles. **管**: bound devouring power — and a governance that binds only the weak while letting capital, power and platforms self-amplify is 饰控制为秩序, dressing control up as order; **力愈大者，受调当愈严**. **教**: form subjects, and teach the recognition of feedback and of 邪理. **正义**: repair cumulative feedback asymmetry so that accidental advantage does not become permanent domination and one failure does not become lifelong exclusion — 正义所守者，共同生成之最低资格. **秩序**: the memory of cooperation, stable enough to be trusted and open enough to be corrected. **共识**: not agreement on conclusions but a **元共识** — facts are not settled by power, evidence can correct positions, others are subjects, rules are general, nobody is exempt, and the common world must be left to the future; this preserves the road into 理 without prescribing answers, and it cannot be manufactured by force. **调节**: boundary, feedback, reversibility, diversity, intergenerational cost — and **调节者亦须被调节**. The summary rule: **真正之秩序，非得永远正确之人，乃立错误不能无限扩大之构** — real order is not finding people who are always right but building a structure in which error cannot expand without limit.

Two instruments come out of this that are immediately usable. **器之七尺** — seven gauges for any technology or institution: 真实 (does it depend on continual deception?), 再生 (does it replenish what it consumes?), 主体 (does it make participants more able to understand, choose and take responsibility, or reduce them to measurable usable objects?), 关系 (does it generate trust and reciprocity, or fear and enmity?), 多样 (does it hold enough difference for the system to learn and change?), 可纠 (can error be admitted, reversed, repaired? — 不可质疑、不可逆转之力，尤险), 代际 (are costs shifted onto those with no voice and no vote?). **器无自善，视其所生** — a tool has no goodness of its own; look at what it generates. And the English book's **nine questions** — truth, renewal, humaneness, rightness, trust, wisdom, harmony, correction, posterity — offered explicitly as "not an algorithm… disciplines of attention", whose purpose is to prevent judgement from remaining inside one interest, one horizon and one metric.

The account of 和 is careful in a way that matters politically. 和 ≠ 同 (史伯: 和实生物，同则不继；以他平他谓之和), and 和 ≠ the absence of conflict — some surface calm is the weak not daring to speak, and some order is power monopolising the right to define good and evil. What 和 does is 不使冲突成为吞噬一切之最高逻辑. **谐** names proportion among forces: growth and restraint, competition and cooperation, freedom and responsibility, individual and collective, stability and change, efficiency and redundancy, unity and diversity. And **大同** is redefined: not a historical endpoint and not a static utopia, but a dynamic, continually maintained condition — a generative attractor basin, and in the English formulation *a civilization's capacity to return to the Great Way after drift*. 共同生成之常行, not sameness.

Two failure conditions of the shared world get named. When the field of manifestation fractures, **講理 itself becomes impossible**: each side holds its own 理 with no shared meta-structure for testing, so interests dress as reasons, positions select facts, power decides truth and winning replaces verification. **非世间无理也，人失共入于理之路也** — it is not that there is no grain in the world; people have lost the shared road into it. And 「大道废，有仁义」 is read structurally rather than as disparagement: when the whole generative order can be directly seen and lived, the virtues need not be shouted; when the shared world fractures, civilisation must compress the Great Way's functions into discrete virtues as scaffolding. Scaffolding repairs but cannot replace, and cut from 大道 each virtue degrades — 仁 into favouritism, 义 into moral superiority, 礼 into enforced obedience, 智 into scheming, 信 into loyalty to a group illusion.

Finally, the account of the self and of what a life is for. **教 = 以象成象**: anything that repeatedly shapes attention, feeling, desire, expectation and action is education — family, school, market, state, organisation, media, platform, war, scarcity. Hence **一个文明真信何物，不书于其经典与宪章，而书于其日日所赏、所罚、所显、所默** — what a civilisation actually believes is written not in its classics and charters but in what it daily rewards, punishes, makes visible and passes over in silence. Moralising against an environment that daily teaches the opposite is empty: **文明之责，不在使人言善，而在不以其制度日日教恶.** **良知** is then 生生之理在人心中的初显 — a 生生感应器 forged by long co-living, since a species that could never trust, care or reciprocate could not have language, division of labour or civilisation. But it is a sensor, not a finished product: sympathy can be confined to one's tribe, loyalty enslaved by violence, shame manipulated by power, indignation hijacked by enemy narratives — **人且能于自觉行善之际，酿至大之恶**. And innate and learned are not two sources but the same environment shaping life at different timescales: instinct is what deep time taught, culture what history taught, institutions what reward and punishment teach, technology what feedback teaches. **人性非定质，乃多重势能之象** — and therefore **人可以被教成更完整的人，也可以被教成一具高效的退化结构**, which is the theory's greatest hope and its deepest fear in one sentence. The current core above gives this account a functional definition and separates the proposed evolutionary mechanism from the normative test of its judgements; evidence for helping or neural involvement does not establish an infallible conscience. [2026-10-05 clarification](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md).

**修身** is accordingly not sculpting oneself into isolated perfection but **使自己成为大道可以经过之地** — making oneself a place the Great Way can pass through. 格物 in five steps: see the 象 and not the slogan; examine the 理 of its incentives, boundaries and feedback; distinguish the actual 象 from one's constructed 像; check *why* one believes it — because it is true, or because it suits one's interest and one's group; and look at what the structure actually generates. 诚意 with five questions asked before acting: would I still do it unseen? would I still call it right if the beneficiary were not my side? could I accept the same rule applied to me? am I solving a problem or proving myself? is the principle I invoke a cover for my fear and my envy? 正心 by giving each feeling its measure — fear signals danger, anger signals a violated boundary, grief signals that a relation mattered. And three sustaining practices: **慎独** (unseen acts still enter causation — an undetected lie still alters the liar), **知止** (wealth must stop or one's safety becomes others' insecurity; power must stop or governance becomes domination; fame must stop or action becomes performance; rationality must stop or people become optimisable objects; and **责任亦须止，不然承担即成控制** — even responsibility must stop, or taking care becomes control), and **恕** in two layers: the other is a subject like me, *and* the other is not a copy of me.

Responsibility scales with the radius of consequence rather than with rank — **力之所及，责之所在** — from which **王道者，非权利之最大化，责任之最大化也**, and 修身齐家治国平天下 is read as a sequence of causal radii rather than a ladder of domination. **业** (karma) is demythologised as the structural residue of action in three places: shaping oneself (act → habit → character → next judgement), shaping others (changing their beliefs, fears, capacities and choices), shaping the environment (repeated action becomes rule, culture, institution, shared expectation). 果为后因 — structurally identical to 生生. And the deepest available form of freedom is stated as: **人之自由，最实之形态，是使继承而来的恶，不必原样传给未来** — the most real form of human freedom is making the evil one inherited not have to be passed on unchanged.

### 至善: what this becomes when it is asked to build something

The July 2026 dialogue is the one document assembled from an original spoken conversation, and it is where the motive and the political line are stated most plainly. It begins from a pane of tempered glass. With steel frames and standardised reversible joints, six planes enclose a space, spaces combine into a house that robots can assemble in a day and fully disassemble later, and the material returns to the flow to become a school, a clinic, a greenhouse or a bridge somewhere else. And then the actual point: 「这里最重要的并不是"房子可以搭得很快"。真正重要的是：**这些东西没有在一次使用之后死掉**。」

From which a whole programme. Production should not centre on one-time terminal products but on a continually recomposable **物质语言** — modules as the world's alphabet, mechanically derived general structures rather than "modules for one home". Not 回收 but **归流**: recycling rescues what has already become waste, and the deeper change is that **从一开始，就不把任何东西设计成物质的终点** — nothing should have to become garbage first and be saved afterwards. And intelligence's role changes accordingly: not helping a subject own and produce more, but recognising material states, understanding structural histories, arranging disassembly and recomposition, maintaining quality, coordinating needs across places — **它是在识流、护流、导流**. The category question underneath: 物 is neither commodity nor merely infrastructure, since both are categories of the existing order and renaming without changing how we understand the world only re-symbolises the old understanding. The real question is not how we own a cup but 杯子究竟是什么 — a material presenting, at this moment, as a cup.

Then the diagnosis. **我们在事实上已经彼此相连，却仍在意识中把彼此当作外部** — materially we already live in one body; cognitively we still run the world as separate competitors. Externality is the category that makes the denial possible: as long as the price is low enough resources may be wasted, as long as profit is high enough consequences may be deferred, and as long as harm happens elsewhere, to others, or in the future, it is as if it never happened. 为什么这一代人有权把千万年积累的物质，在几百年之内挥霍殆尽？

Moloch is named without mystification: nobody actually wants the outcome, but every participant is pushed toward it by local incentives, and together they sacrifice trust, freedom, ecology, the future and their own descendants. **这是一种自我断子绝孙式的成功.** The cancer analogy is exact and it is the same object as the scale non-transitivity theorem: **它非常擅长复制。它的问题是，它不再知道自己属于一个身体** — a cancer cell is not bad at replicating; it has simply stopped knowing that it belongs to a body. It keeps "I want more" and loses "if the whole dies, I die too."

And the observation that ties this to the AI-safety literature: **our civilisation is already a giant instrumental convergence.** Individuals, firms, organisations and states have different goals yet are all forced to chase money, scale, attention, data, power, technical capability and control, because those serve almost any goal — so 本来只是工具的东西，逐渐变成了所有主体共同追逐的终点. Numbers define authority, metrics define value, platforms decide who is seen, capital decides what can happen. The dynamic has no clear centre and no clear boundary: 它更像一种没有主体的动力结构，一种分布在每个人、每个组织、每条规则和每个恐惧之中的驱动力. Which is why the accurate statement is not "they are mad" but **是我们疯了** — a collective psychosis in which everyone believes they are doing the right thing in a right world.

The danger is not that the logic never created value. It did accelerate production, organisation and technology. The danger is that **它可以创造能力，却没有生成与能力相匹配的智慧；它可以让局部主体变得更强，却不断削弱整体的稳定；它可以提高行动速度，却不能回答行动应当朝向哪里** — it can create capability without generating wisdom to match, strengthen local subjects while weakening the stability of the whole, and increase the speed of action without answering where action should go.

From which the bright line. AI will not automatically transcend our paradigm; whatever we train it on, deploy it with and reward it by, it will amplify. **我们并不是在创造智慧，而是在把文明已有的失明自动化。我们是在把癌症交给指数增长.** Human struggle can still be reflected on, halted and renegotiated; **一旦斗争被写进自动化系统、基础模型、全球基础设施与不可逆的军备竞赛，它就会成为路径依赖，甚至被误认为新的物理法则**. Therefore the first question of the AI era is not "what more can we do" but **什么绝不能被加速？** — and the line to hold in common is 不要把人类尚未解决的斗争，升级为智能的斗争. **要么不造这样的智能；要造，就不能再按照「我要赢、我要最大、我要让它替我战胜所有人」的逻辑去造** — stated explicitly as neither pessimism nor mere caution but 最基本的清醒.

And braking is not pessimism: **方向错误的时候，刹车只是对现实的回应。如果一个系统只有加速能力，没有停止、观察和改变方向的能力，那它并不智能。它只是失控.** Sorting everything into optimism and pessimism is itself a loss of reality-sense. The 分寸 is stated too: not all struggle is bad — some resistance is necessary, some boundaries must be defended, some competition is play, exploration and mutual stimulation. The question is knowing what may be experimented with and what must never be touched with arms-race logic; what errors are repairable and what, once automated, scaled and made infrastructural, becomes path dependency. **AI 就是这样的地方.**

The positive programme is deliberately modest about authority. 善 cannot be a subject's preference or one group's discipline over others, or it has not left the old centralised logic; the change is not a stronger subject prescribing the good for everyone but **改变智能看待自身与世界的位置**. **善，是使生命、关系与世界继续拥有未来的方向** — 善是生生, generation generating generation, this round opening conditions for the next; 恶是熄灭, degeneration producing degeneration, this round's profit paid for by compressing, closing and consuming the future. 己所不欲，勿施于人 is then not politeness but a recognition of interconnected reality: 你把恐惧、剥削和控制写进系统，它们就会通过系统返回. Pollution returns through climate and food; fear returns through politics; the system you use to control others eventually controls you.

The first uses of intelligence should be the **基本生活底座**: food grown like an ecosystem rather than manufactured for surplus and harmful consumption; housing from demountable, movable, recomposable structures; medicine, education, energy, transport and information serving real capability; machines taking repetitive, dangerous and heavy work; materials circulating rather than running from mine to landfill; cities as places to meet, create, rest and live together rather than machines for extracting attention and labour. Because real freedom is the ability to say no: **当一个人即使不依附某个雇主、平台、组织或权力，也仍然拥有食物、住所、医疗、信息与基本尊严，他才真正拥有自由** — only then is cooperation not coerced obedience, labour not merely the avoidance of hunger, and creation able to come from interest, responsibility, relationship, curiosity and love. Today's welfare is largely punitive in conception — one must work, earn and obey to deserve food, medicine and shelter — and as automation takes on more production and maintenance, that arrangement is not only cruel but 越来越没有必要. The common substrate does not end individual choice: some build their own house, some form new communities, some travel, research, create, care or explore, some choose a simple and quiet life. **共同基础不是规定所有人如何生活，而是让所有人不必通过战胜别人，才有资格活着.** The shift is from *We all fight with each other* to *We all work together* — which does not mean the end of disagreement, only that 分歧不再以剥夺彼此生存和未来为前提.

And individuality and community are not opposed: a healthy community is not one that flattens individuals but the soil in which they grow, and only when people need not spend all their energy on survival, defence and competition can genuinely rich individuality develop. Under enforced scarcity both terms deform — individuality becomes isolation, defence and unlimited self-expansion; commonality becomes obedience, exclusion and group struggle. **真正的个体性需要共同体托住。真正的共同体也需要个体不断生成新的可能.**

**至善** itself is explicitly not a new authority centre, not a claim to the final answer, not another system using "good" to control others. It is a shared confirmation of a direction — 智能应当使世界更加真实、更加美好、更加能够生生不息 — with 向善 meaning that the model's *way of existing* may not be built on exploitation, colonisation, manipulation, deception, cognitive compression and the closing of futures, and 普惠 meaning not that more people use the same product but that the capabilities intelligence creates **真正扩大所有人的未来，而不是让少数人获得控制所有人的能力**. And it must be treated as a 人类命运共同体 decision rather than settled by who wins the race — **不是因为我们已经足够智慧，而恰恰是因为我们还没有**: precisely because humanity as a whole is still pulled by local incentives, fear, competition and power, those drives must not be handed to something stronger, faster and harder to stop.

> **至善，不是到达一个静止的完美终点。至善，是在每一个当下，使下一刻仍然可以生成；是在每一次选择中，不关闭他者与世界的未来；是在获得力量时，仍然知道自己属于整体；是在创造智能时，不让它继承我们的疯狂，而让它参与世界的生生。**

### The proof layer, and what it will not promise

The 2026-08 证明篇 inverts the usual order: **《生生不息论》为草稿；本文为其修订之地基** — the doctrine is the draft and the formal work is its foundation. Its distinctive contribution is **predicates without a subject**. 绝未 is a transition driving the world from the generative region G into the locked region Δ; 减未 is choosing, when alternatives exist, toward either degenerate pole; 护未 is keeping the process in the interior within feasible branches. Because the predicates act on *transitions* rather than on agent utilities, cross-agent evaluation is redeemed — you can evaluate an action without first agreeing on whose preferences count. And where everything has already collapsed the predicates fall silent, which is ought-implies-can written into the formalism.

Four results carry weight.

**The two poles.** Degeneration is not one thing. There is a **freezing pole** (H(future) → 0 — sameness, exhaustion, dead wood) and a **noise pole** (H(future|past) → H(future) — chaos, quicksand; book-burning raises thermal entropy and destroys structure). Convergence and closing are a *necessary phase* of writing structure in, not evil in themselves: **恶为滞于一极；生生 ＝ 凝-发持续偕行** — evil is being stuck at one pole; generation is condensation and release proceeding together.

**Irreversibility and the asymmetry of good and evil.** Locked states are forward-invariant, so from Δ one cannot return to G. Entering can be a single step; returning is impossible. **恶一步而永久，继不可一步而复** (从善如登，从恶如崩) — and this is a property of branching structure, prior to any subject or agent. It is the structural reason the ethics is asymmetric.

**Winner-take-all is unattainable, conditionally.** On a named axiom class, either the domination phase cannot be sustained or it completes and the winner enters an absorbing state — **通吃作为结局不可得**, anchored in 竭泽而渔，岂不获得？而明年无鱼. Under net-positive spillover, coexistence policies strictly dominate after some T*. The domain where all the axioms fail is precisely the domain where institutions must be installed. And at the top level the exogenous-reward construction cannot be instantiated at all — **至大无外**, there is no outside to be rewarded from.

**Self-destruction is not good.** Strategies carrying recurrent collapse risk, driving the state into Δ, or burning options under (uncertainty ∧ irreversibility ∧ learnability) are strictly dominated across all independent measures, for any goal requiring future action — assembled from a ruin argument (survival ≤ (1−ε)^k; multiplicative processes whose ensemble average diverges while time-average ruin is certain), a quasi-option-value argument, and an information-refinement monotonicity argument.

Three refusals matter as much as the results. The objectivity claim is explicitly conceded to be **constructivist-strength** — observer-independent and machine-checkable *given the definitions* — and explicitly not categorical realism, and explicitly unable to persuade a thoroughgoing nihilist. The theorems are **necessary but not sufficient**: there exist dynamics whose supremum probability of never locking is below 1, so **不许福，独明祸之必至；正向漂移无定理** — the theory licenses no promise of good outcomes, only the demonstration that certain classes of strategy are dominated. And **totalizers do not reliably self-destruct**: petrification can be rigid without dying, so the political corollary is **避免须安装，不可待淘汰** — you cannot wait for a totalizing order to be selected out; the avoidance has to be built. That last one directly contradicts the alignment paper's empirical-convergence pillar, and the proof layer says so.

Deployment is then specified as a **constraint class rather than a maximisation objective**, which is the corpus's defence surface against Goodhart: take no strong-绝未 action, keep system-level process inside the interior region, do not let coupling terms turn negative. Its ethic is 玄德 — **生而不有，为而不恃，长而不宰**: continue others' generation without seizing their agency, which in this vocabulary means not collapsing other agents' selectors.

### The counterexamples, and what the theory does with them

The corpus is unusually adversarial toward itself, and this is the best argument for taking it seriously. Three separate batteries exist.

**External objections, answered.** *Heat death*: the claim concerns the process's structure while it occurs, not eternal generation; a person's mortality does not void the fittingness of living well. *Cancer expands possibility*: locally yes, globally no — local expansion with global contraction is precisely the anti-生生 case, and the criterion was always multi-scale and multi-agent. *Territorial or genocidal expansion*: judged by net effect across all scales and agents — **任何以消灭其他 agent 之可能性空间为代价的扩张，皆反生生不息**. *Berlin's pluralism*: 生生不息 is not one value among values but **价值多元性自身存在之 medium**, hence pluralism's condition rather than its rival. *The frame problem*: this is a directional principle, not a content encoding — like "increasing function", it applies to any content whatever. *Instrumental convergence*: the objection presupposes a strict terminal/instrumental split that fails here, and under this alignment resource concentration is itself anti-aligned. *Bootstrap and engineering*: conceded as a genuine objection; the only reply is that directional specification is short and does not require solving pluralism or the frame problem first. *Self-reference*: conceded as an honest dependency, with the fallback that the core argument needs only the minimal fact that the process is continuing, which any thinker already accepts.

**The internal battery** — twelve cases built to break the doctrine in use, and kept in the text so it can be falsified rather than admired. Many options can still be functional closure (**开非选项多**). Protective closure is the easiest thing for power to counterfeit. A focus's freedom that grows by capture is not prosperity. Suffering and high tension can be 正危, so **好非舒适**. A tradition that has stopped accepting calibration and frozen its own legality rules is meta-closure — **其名虽礼，其实礼之尸**; **古非必道**. Novelty without warrant, dimension-opening or calibratability is not strong legality; **新非必生**. Low tension without openness is not 平 but death; **静非必平**. Local short-run good that seizes global long-run continuity is not 真道. Captured calibration is itself pseudo-openness — 伪校. Co-openness by assimilation is not 和 but 同化. An act that calls itself 正 while running on seizure, concealment, freezing and counterfeit is 名正而实邪. And, added last, **正名 itself can be counterfeited**.

**Proof-layer concessions**, listed above: no promise of good outcomes, constructivist-strength objectivity only, totalizers do not reliably self-destruct.

And the corpus knows its own formal apparatus cannot do the work by itself. The formal supplement names three impossibilities outright: it cannot supply the content of `I(x)` (that is situational), it cannot choose for an agent at a 機, and it cannot verify the felt sense of Dao. Its two powers are to check the main text's internal coherence and to expose where the main text is unrefined — its job is **捕异**, catching discrepancies. If any claim of the main text cannot be developed in it without contradiction, the main text must be revised.

### The verification discipline, and the honesty machinery

Because the value predicates cite each other — 正 mentions 夺 mentions 开; 好 mentions 坏 mentions 邪 — the later versions refuse to hand-wave the circularity. They put the predicates in a complete lattice under a monotone operator: the least fixed point gives the conservative criterion, the greatest fixed point the persistence criterion, and where monotonicity or convergence fails the verdict is **待校** (pending) and none of 善, 仁, 道, 真 may be claimed. **定义可互照，不可互偷** — definitions may illuminate one another; they may not steal from one another.

That pending verdict is backed by a three-valued logic with a real distinction between false and unknown, and the norm that governs the whole system: **未知非假，未校非真；待校者，不得冒称道、仁、善、真**. Unknown is not false, uncalibrated is not true, and what is pending may not impersonate the Way, benevolence, goodness or truth.

Every value claim must also carry an explicit scale `s` and time window `ω`: **无尺度之开，常成幻开；无时间窗之好，常成短视之好**. And the scales may not be chosen at will — causal relevance, affected-party relevance and externality closure force scales into the judgement: **凡被影响者，皆有入判之权**. If an effect spills over into another scale or horizon, that scale is in the relevant set as a matter of law, not of the judge's convenience. The value-term for this is **度期周** (scale-and-horizon comprehensiveness), and it was deliberately renamed away from an earlier term using 闭, because 闭 already means closure: **周非闭；周者，无漏也**.

Openness itself becomes a graded function `O_F(Γ; s, ω)` composed from 未 · 可 · 应 · 转 rather than a boolean, so that "more open" and "more closed" can be compared at all — 开谓其是否仍活，开度谓其活得几何. The composition must be monotone, **short-board sensitive** (any dimension below its survival threshold blocks a verdict of strong openness), **anti-capture** (availability obtained by capture does not count as real openness), scale-explicit, and itself auditable. The conservative form is `min(U, A, R, T)`; weighted forms must not mask a short board. Three concrete aggregators are supplied with the rule that the choice is recorded in the audit, that high-risk domains default to the min form, and that disagreement between aggregators forces a pending verdict rather than a pick.

One more repair is quietly important. Logical negation `¬i` is replaced by an **alternative-path set** `Alt(Γ, i)` with a **baseline alternative**, because the negation of an interval need not itself be a legal interval. **行之校验，不可只问此路如何，亦须问不走此路将如何** — evaluating an act means asking not only how this road goes but how things would go if it were not taken. Inaction is an option with consequences, and it has to be scored.

And the whole apparatus is made runnable. Openness is extracted as a four-dimensional real feature vector with explicit formulas and an absolute rule that an unobservable component is marked pending and **may not be defaulted to 0 or 1**. Audit gets a data schema — event record, trace table, affected-party response table with voice/valence/harm/consent/confidence, model cross-check table, outcome-by-horizon table — and a quality score taken as a minimum rather than an average. Thresholds get a five-step calibration procedure (anchor cases, harm irreversibility, sensitivity analysis across aggregators, counterfactual baselines including inaction and protective closure, recorded provenance) with three risk bands. The worked case is a recommender system with three candidate actions: maximising dwell time is judged 邪行 because platform growth *depends on* closing user attention and autonomy while apparent choice increases as responsiveness and repairability fall; a multi-objective, user-controllable, diversity-constrained, externally audited alternative passes to 正行 → 义 → 善 → 仁; and pausing the high-risk channel is 护闭 provided it is term-bound and returns power to the affected. And then the discipline holds even in the passing case: the label stops at **候选真道**, candidate, rather than proven, because the three-year horizon is prediction and not observation. **不得以缺证替代通过** — absence of evidence may not substitute for passing.

### The v2 reset: the strongest position the theory has reached

In July 2026, after the six-part prose book and after external reviews in philosophy, sociology-economics and AI-alignment/systems-safety, the theory was reopened on stricter terms. The v2 execution plan is not another version of the doctrine; it is a contract about how the doctrine may be argued, and on the points where it differs from the May formal series it is better supported.

The decisive change is the order of the argument. **生成 is no longer asserted as good.** The book runs mechanism first, without appealing to good and evil: how finite processes depend on wider fields to persist; how action, dependency, consequence and feedback boundaries can come apart so that local continuation rests on damage that never returns; how differential persistence creates a selection environment *prior to* conscious competition; how success buys the power to shape the next round's conditions; how infrastructure, institutions and education form the participants themselves; how those loops become a totalizing mechanism under stated conditions; and how AI enters, accelerates and may reorganise that selection ecology. Only then does the normative commitment arrive, **openly declared rather than derived**: centres that bear feeling, care, knowing, acting, creating and valuing are worth caring about; the common conditions that let such centres arise, respond to each other, find their errors and produce unknown value are worth caring about; and no process acquires the right to close other sources of value merely by being stronger, more stable, larger or more successful. With the concession attached: **逻辑不能强迫一个一致的支配者关心他者** — logic cannot force a consistent dominator to care about others. What the theory can do is show what such a choice destroys, why the affected have reason to resist, and why anyone who still demands that *their own* reasons, future and judgement be respected has already appealed to the public layer in practice.

Second, the plan **replaces the seven gauges with six protection functions, each derived by a deletion test** — each is justified by naming the distinct failure that no other function can cover. 主体地位 (if centres bearing feeling and relation can be erased at will, the common good is a system property nobody experiences). 真实接触 (if consequences cannot refute the actor's model, error and harm never become reasons in the next decision). 非支配 (if one centre can change another's basic conditions at will without giving reasons, accepting appeal or facing counterweight, the others lose the capacity to make their reasons have effects). 可再生的多元性 (alternatives that are permitted to *speak* but have no time, land, resources, education, infrastructure or successors cannot correct the shared direction — and a single failure becomes total failure). 基底再生 (a process that consumes ecology, bodies, care, attention, trust, knowledge and maintenance without replenishing them is moving costs elsewhere or later). 纠正能力 (without interruption, refusal, appeal, rollback, repair, exit, replacement and succession, greater capability means error becomes irreversible reality before it is understood). They are explicitly **not summable into a score**, and they are given a conflict ordering: rule out extermination, enslavement, torture and boundless domination first; protect minimum survival, standing and appeal for everyone affected, especially against concentrating risk on the powerless; then prioritise reducing irreversible, cross-generational and systemic damage; then compare remaining options on necessity, proportionality, reversibility, incidence of benefit and cost, alternatives and reparability; for incommensurable values use public reasons, representation of the affected, adversarial review, checks and periodic reconsideration rather than pretending a technical calculation has removed the politics; and give emergency measures explicit triggers, minimum necessary powers, time limits, independent review and a path back to ordinary rights.

Third, the totalizing mechanism is given **a strict definition and explicit conditions for withdrawing the diagnosis**. It is separated from adjacent phenomena — positive feedback, path lock-in, monopoly, capture, domination — each of which is insufficient on its own. Its carrier-independent invariant: locally appropriated success converts into greater power to shape the conditions of later choice, while key consequences are moved out of or delayed past feedback, and other centres progressively lose the conditions for independent perception, action, updating and reproducing alternatives. Its observable trajectory must be traced in time order rather than labelled after the fact: a local evaluative scale succeeds → resources and visibility concentrate → the successful shape interfaces, admission, metrics and rules → the same scale enters domains that were heterogeneous → consequences are externalised or delayed → persons, organisations and technologies adapted to that scale are retained → the cost of exit, dissent and reproducing alternatives rises → crisis and criticism are absorbed as further justification. And it must be **withdrawn** if advantage does not convert into rule-setting power, if the goal stays confined to one domain, if multiple evaluations remain independent, if growth strengthens exit and feedback and alternatives, if personnel change or external shock can quickly change direction, or if criticism reduces rather than increases the mechanism's resources. The plan also declines the metaphysical upgrade: 吸引子 is an analytic lens for a recurring trajectory, and 正文不声称创造了超出可建模动态系统的新数学对象.

Fourth, **生成性对齐 is explicitly demoted** to a cross-layer acceptability judgement about a specific socio-technical system in a specific use, with a specific permission set and a specific relation to affected parties — not a reward function, not a civilisational utility function, not a solved algorithm. It is supported by a **seven-layer stack** — spec/training, model, agent system, product and interaction, deploying organisation, market and state, governance — each with its own question, failure modes, responsible party, evidence type, and an explicit statement of *what success at this layer cannot prove*. Alignment does not substitute for security; security does not justify opaque centralisation.

Fifth, the virtuous loop must be **as materially specified as the vicious one**: consequences perceived and given causal force → costs borne or repaired → ecological, care, knowledge and trust substrates replenished → capability and benefit circulating → more centres acquiring independent observation, refusal, building and maintenance capacity → plural feedback improving public knowledge → renewed commons making the next round of shared capability feasible. Goodwill is not a carrier. Time, resources, care, knowledge, maintenance, organisation, legal standing and capture-resistance are.

Sixth, a **claim-type discipline**: every load-bearing sentence is a definition, a description, a mechanism, an empirical diagnosis, a normative commitment or a practical inference, and each type carries its own obligation. Forbidden constructions are listed by name — 任何／必然／唯一根源／西方就是／自然告诉我们应该／AI 只要……就…… Traditional terms carry a use protocol: separate the classical source from this book's chosen reading and say where the two cannot corroborate each other; do not pass glyph association off as philology; do not write Confucian, Daoist, Buddhist, Ubuntu, virtue, deontological and consequentialist positions as one long-agreed doctrine; use "Chinese"/"Western" only of specific institutions, texts and periods; and — the strongest requirement — **a reader must be able to restate the main chain without using any of these terms**.

And seventh, the theory writes down **its own failure conditions**. If subjects and values stably arise without the generative conditions it claims; if one of the six functions can be deleted without any loss of protection or explanation; if totalizing cannot be distinguished from ordinary success, lock-in or monopoly; if systems diagnosed as totalizing durably strengthen independent feedback, exit, alternative reproduction and substrate renewal; if the virtuous loop can only exist on permanent subsidy, coerced uniformity or hidden costs; if 生生 language repeatedly justifies suppressing dissent, sacrificing the weak or permanent rule and the reflexive machinery cannot correct it; if the AI-layer evidence shows deployment choices do not change which technical failures are retained and spread; or if the traditional interpretations are shown to be historical misreadings that the modern argument needs in order to stand — then the claim must be downgraded, rewritten or abandoned. With the reason for writing them down at all: **理论不是因可失败而软弱。只有写明什么能改变它，它才没有把「生生」变成不可纠正的最终形式** — a theory is not weakened by being falsifiable; only by saying what could change it does it avoid turning "generation" into an incorrigible final form.

That last sentence is the theory applying its own criterion to itself, and it is the right place to end the argument: a doctrine of openness that could not be closed by evidence would be its own best counterexample.

### What it means, said aloud (2026-10-02)

The v2 reset puts mechanism first and declares the commitment afterwards. What it does not supply is the meaning the commitment serves. On 2026-10-02 Ren supplied it in speech. He recorded a conversation with his friend Alex and then named it as showing the why and meaning of his theories (`drafts/2026-10-02-friend-conversation/transcript.md`; `sources/16-claude-code-directions/2026-10-02-why-and-meaning-request.md`). It is an automatic transcript of unedited speech, and Ren's voice is provisionally Speaker B. Most of what he says there is already in this document in his earlier words (claims 417–437). What is new is that he says it all together, under pressure, to someone arguing for a different view.

**The process, and that it is good.** Alex presses an ecological objection. If everything belongs to one system, then "you have to say, okay, America declining is progress … You have to say that the lion eating the gazelle is progress … Otherwise, your model doesn't work" [01:05:21]. Ren will not call every event progress. He gives progress a direction instead: "Like, you can't argue progress in this, like, reduction … I think the best uh way it can be argued is that uh Uh in the future there are more like complexities that leads to even more complexities arising. Uh and uh during this process one of the most sophisticated dynamics also like starts to emerge and creates further emergence that leads to us. So we we we we are not just our life. We are the whole process of the universe doing this and itself and this is great" [01:06:05–01:06:43]. This is the 2026-07-14 book's 总纲 sentence 「我们是天地之间，宇宙不断变化与生成的一个进程」 in his own voice, where until now the corpus had it only in AI drafting (own words 82, 87). It also carries something the 总纲 line does not: an evaluation. The process is great, and we are it.

**Endings belong to it.** Alex argues for seasonality: civilisations grow and decline, and "the tree doesn't grow to destroy the soil that leads to its very foundation" [00:56:00]. Ren accepts the cycle and moves the line: "I would look at decay as a form of growth" [00:57:27]. "[dying?, unclear in the audio] is not a bug but a feature like we we we are fundamentally looking at this wrong that's why we or at least many people pursue this immortality as being better" [00:57:44]. That repeats claim 425 ("death is not a bug but a feature") nearly word for word, four months later. The closing that the theory names 成闭 and 元闭 he then describes from inside the closer: "for anything That is a structure of itself that maintains its environment for its own existence and looks at a part of its structure as permanent. It becomes an enforcer of a logic that can become very detrimental to to the situationality and the growth, the changes, that's like in conflict with its rigid frame of what is" [00:58:23]. In this account, closure is a part taking its present form as permanent and enforcing that form on the field. That is 不变者熄 (own words 81) stated as a mechanism.

**Humanity has a future in it.** Alex draws the other conclusion an ecological view invites: humans are the cancer, so humanity should give the Earth back. Ren grants the premise, "all the cancerous system are human made", and adds that in an ecosystem "if you're cancerous over time, you will get selected out" [00:47:13]. He rejects the conclusion: "I also disagree. I think we are still on continuation that can still keep manifesting. We have a great future" [00:47:53]. That is claim 424, humanity as "the way we keep our future going", now against its misanthropic reading.

**The generative field, as trees.** Claim 420 asked for "a generative field … one that provides all necessities without condition. For example, oxygen doesn't require your sacrifice". In the conversation Ren gives it a body: infrastructures for security and "basic freedom of like having a decent life", "fully autonomous", only "good enough … like trees. Trees are providers" [00:50:01]. "the trees are the lungs of the ecosystem, it does not become the whole ecosystem and it does not like as the returns for its uh fruit" [01:01:18]. The tree shows both guards on the generative field together. It provides without asking for return, so there is no gate. And it does not become the whole, so it does not close.

**Not growth, and not competition as such.** "I never said growth is bad … I love development. I love new things. Like, I love novelty" [00:51:21–00:51:27]. Competition "has a situationality" [01:05:05], and Chinese "actually [has] a different word for those different things" [01:09:23], which he does not name in the recording. The target is a self that "only you cares about what you perceive as you" and pursues "even more" without seeing "what the existence of you and your world really is" [01:10:12]. The wolf's "existence … is not just about the wolf" [01:11:23]. In this theory's words, that is the part that takes its continuation as separable from the field's, 以彼闭为己开.

**Why this ontology, and why in Chinese.** Ren says the work should "use the the pre-Qin ontology as the basis and construct one that everyone could accept that intellectually like undefeatable" [01:29:19–01:30:21], without being "bonded by … those names" [01:29:19]. Chinese thought, as he reads it, is "relational is non-dual" [01:27:33]. Classical Chinese "lacks like subject object differentiations and it lacks tenses it talks about things just as the way things are" [01:37:16]. Its value is also practical: on how status and greed reshape minds it "does provide like much higher engineering utility" [01:44:18]. And he adds a reason of timing: China is now "the biggest leverage", so a theory built on its own tradition carries legitimacy [01:44:52]. Alex's reply is that the same non-dual, cyclical insight is found in Kashmir Shaivism, Zen, Heraclitus and the Norse gods who die, and that each person finds their own tradition the purest [01:31:44; 01:40:02]. Ren's next turn grants the universals ("There is a common call … that leads to those like universal social invariances" [01:32:44]) and keeps the claim about engineering utility. That is the empirical-convergence pillar (claim 203), on the convergence of wisdom traditions, met by its strongest critic and given its most specific defence.

**2026-10-04: 逍遥 as fit, and the affirmation as a life.** From the recording "Soi Si Lom 5 2", which Ren supplied on 2026-10-04 to be added to his theories (`sources/18-friend-conversations/2026-10-04-soi-si-lom-5-2/`): a 51-minute monologue, one voice throughout, supplied by Ren as his own and not confirmed by ear. Quotations in 「」 are from the Chinese translation (`drafts/2026-10-04-soi-si-lom-5-2/translation-zh.md`), not verbatim; the glosses are the assistant's. Most of what the talk says that touches this theory is already one of its claims: 止戈为武 (claim 277), 己所不欲 (claim 333), braking (claim 329), 向善 and 普惠 (claim 343). Two things are new here. 第十九章 reads 逍遥 as not being wholly defined by any identity, name, fear, victory or others' gaze (claim 296). The talk gives the same word a condition: 「如果你当下的认知模型与现实相契合，那就更接近一种“逍遥”的状态」 [05:26] (if your present cognitive model fits reality, you are nearer to 逍遥), and in that state there is no independent self, 「只有不同的力、不同的能量在流动，形成动态的作用」 [05:26] (only different forces, different energies flowing and acting). If the two are one state, fit is the mechanism and the freedom its symptom (open question 51; the readings are set out in `relational-self-and-datong` and `drives-narrative-attractors-and-mind`, 2026-10-04). The talk's last minute refuses dropping out of the structural problems, 「不能直接退出、不管了」 [51:08] (one cannot simply drop out and stop caring). It does not speak of 逍遥 there, but the refusal is consistent with 第十九章's reading that 逍遥 is not withdrawal; the link is the assistant's. And the affirmation of 2026-10-02 returns as a description of a life rather than of the universe: 「我们也可以去探索、创造、发现，去玩，去创造社会游戏，去拥有一个像大同那样的社会。这才应该是我们想要的」 [30:56] (we can also explore, create, discover, play, create social games, and have a society like 大同; that is what we should want). The being that does this is directional: 「我的心和我的理，跟这个世界一样，都在朝着某个方向流动；它是有方向性的」 [04:09] (my heart and my reason, like the world, flow in some direction; it is directional).

## Claims

**Citation legend.** All paths are relative to `/Users/ren/theories/sources/`. `D5/` = `07-shengsheng-buxi/downloads-drafts-2026-05/`; `D7/` = `07-shengsheng-buxi/downloads-2026-07-08/`; `«repo»/` = `07-shengsheng-buxi/ssbx-repo/`; `Z/` = `06-zhishan/`. Voice-note citations point into `/Users/ren/theories/_process/reading-notes/` and are Ren's own words from claude.ai conversations.

### A. What kind of thing this theory is

1. There is no position outside the universe from which to observe or test it; any observation is the universe testing itself, so this is not a proof (证) but a pointing (指) — 「此非证也，乃**指**也」. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §〇; D5/生生不息论_v_11_间开定本.md §〇; «repo»/史料/生生不息论_三本文完整版/01_正篇_v13.2形式项总清与类型校正版/01_生生不息论.md
2. Absolute argument is impossible: it would require a view-from-nowhere subject, every real arguer has a situation, and nothing without a situation exists — so the theory pursues 最稳健/自洽之指, mutual support from many independent angles, rather than absoluteness. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §一; D5/生生不息论_v9.md §一
3. Ren states the same point in his own voice and flags it as itself uncertain: 「绝对演绎本身即是一种形而上学的奢望 — 绝对演绎需要绝对主体，绝对主体不可能存在（不确定这个是不是可以证明，还是增加claim）」. [observation] — `_process/reading-notes/out-hb-03.md` #100 (2026-05-02, 生生不息的出处)
4. The theory asks only four things of itself: 自洽 (coherent), 可行/入境 (applicable in a concrete situation), 可校 (correctable), 开 (not closing its own future) — 「无外于宇宙之位，无外于过程之尺」. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md 序; D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md 序; «repo»/…/03_补篇一_间开本_致知版/01_生生不息论.md
5. **不可外测，非不可内证；模不绝对，非模无高下** — non-measurability from outside does not forbid verification from inside, and non-absoluteness of models does not make all models equal. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9_开境护闭校正版.md §〇
6. 道可道，非常道 re-read structurally: whatever can be objectified, turned into a final proposition, or externally measured is not the constant Dao. [observation] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §〇
7. 道法自然 and 生生不息 name the same thing from two entries: 自然 = 自如此 (so of itself, not by external command), 生生 = the unbroken continuation. Ren's own version: 「道法自然，生生不息这两个基本上是同义的…如果然是『变化，为』」. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §〇; D7/Shengsheng_The_Way_of_Unceasing_Generation.md Ch2; `out-hb-03.md` #122
8. The theory is reflexively bound by its own criterion: a model that closes itself as the unique truth 自背其指 — contradicts what it points at. Hence the edition name 间开本 and its closing line 「间未尽，开未闭，本仍待校」. [value stance] — «repo»/…/03_补篇一_间开本_致知版/13_终言.md
9. The treatise's own existence is an instance of what it describes: writing it enlarged what could be said, so its standing is itself an act of 护开, not a proof of 护开 — 「此论之有效性，在它被言说之那一刻，即已得到证明」. [observation] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §二十一; D5/生生不息论_v9.md §二十七; «repo»/义理/R_与生生不息对齐之必然.md 终言
10. Writing style is itself governed by the doctrine: the three readings (事/物/场) must mutually constrain, because over-extending any one is a closure — 极间相则美而难校；极物相则清而失生；极场相则严而失肉；**三相皆极，则论自身成一全景之闭点**. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v_11_间开定本.md 附章三; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/02_卷一_立位.md
11. Ren's parallel formulation of the same guard: 「物相，事相，气相，每一个如果追求『极』就变成了象，就失去了生」. [observation] — `out-hb-03.md` #135 (2026-05-03)
12. Arguments must be self-standing: 「说理，不要搞引用，理要完整连贯」 — importing 朱子曰 / 戴震曰 made the argument incomplete without the reference, which Ren calls a major defect. [design direction] — `out-hb-03.md` #99
13. The standard for the argument text: it must still stand against a rational, persistently challenging sceptic, and it must explicitly engage the concrete positions of current AI-alignment academia. [design direction] — `out-hb-03.md` #97, #98
14. 智 in this system includes recognising the limits of one's own recognition: **不识自己之识之局限者，不为智者** — and the doctrine applies this to itself, which is why every mature version carries a counterexample battery and a pending verdict. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §二十三

### B. 間 and the generative ontology

15. The root is **間**: not the space between two things, not a measurable distance, not nothingness, not a thing — **一态未被其已穷尽之处**. 非先有物而后有间，乃有间而物可分. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v_10_间生形式版.md 序; D5/生生不息论_v_11_间开定本.md §三
16. 間's four-fold description: 变未发而已有其隙；形未成而已有其可；言未立而已有其分；事未动而已有其应 — not void (it has structure), not full (it has an unexhausted remainder), not a thing (it has no standing), not nothing (it manifests). [mechanism] — «repo»/…/04_补篇二_间生论_主篇/03_一、间.md
17. Every utterance presupposes a distinction and every distinction presupposes a 間, so 言之初，非元也，乃间也; the ground of speech cannot be found outside speech — 「求根者必先立一言；言既立，间已生」. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v_10_间生形式版.md §一; «repo»/…/04_补篇二…/03_一、间.md
18. 元 is demoted to an aspect: 元 := 間之初显为动者 — nameable and useful, but not a deeper root, because naming 元 already requires distinguishing it from 非元, which requires a 間. **元赖间而名，非先间而立.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v_10_间生形式版.md §三; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/06_卷四_元、几、势、机.md
19. Before v10, 元 was the root: not a first thing but 动之初显, with no self-substance; and movement has no mover, since positing one regresses infinitely — **动者，自显也**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §二 · §三
20. Core formula: **续(态) = 态 生合 間(态)**, `J(x) = x ⊕ I(x)`, field-generalised to `Γ' = Γ ⊕_F i` with `i ∈ 間_F(Γ)`. It replaces `J(x) = x + ΔJ(x)`, because **差在后，间在先；差可量，间可生；差为迹，间为机之母**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v_10_间生形式版.md 序; «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
21. The rename reframed the whole theory from a theory of *change quantities* into a theory of *generative possibility*, and it was triggered by one line of Ren's audit: 「J 一直没有定义。」 [observation] — «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
22. 生合 (⊕_F) obeys five laws: partiality (defined only for legal intervals — 乱者，非生), type asymmetry (the field bears the interval, not conversely), general non-commutativity and non-associativity, closure as an absorbing state, and an empty interval leaving the field unchanged. [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §2; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/02_卷一_立位.md
23. Non-commutativity is named the algebraic root of 機: because `I(x⊕i) ≠ I(x)`, **行之顺序不可任易，时机不可错**. [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §2.2; D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三律三
24. Closure is an absorbing state: 闭(x) ⇔ I(x) = ∅ ⇔ J(x) = ∅ — once entered, no interval brings the state out; the algebraic correspondent of "death and collapse are real". [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §2.4
25. There is an empty interval ∅_M with x ⊕ ∅_M = x: stillness in which intervals still generate without the state changing — 非闭，乃 stillness 之形. [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §2.3
26. `I(x)` is multi-valued (a set of legal intervals), and the structural condition of 機 is not `|I(x)| ≥ 2` but the existence of two intervals whose successor states differ *in openness*: 机(Γ) ⟺ ∃ i ≠ j ∈ I_F(Γ) with Π_开(Γ⊕i) ≠ Π_开(Γ⊕j). Option-richness is necessary, not sufficient. [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §1.3 · §4.1; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/06_卷四_元、几、势、机.md
27. Legality constraint: a change unrelated to the state's structure, accumulation, situation and relations is not that state's generation but external disorder — **生非乱变。生者，由间而化；乱者，离间而加**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v_10_间生形式版.md §二; D5/间生论_形式补.md §3.4
28. 几 is the process's earliest self-reference: directional bias before form — 元(i,Γ) ∧ ‖∇_i w_Γ‖ > δ ∧ w_Γ(i) < τ_stab. A single 几 is below resolution; its orbit-integrated gradient becomes 可读之几. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §三; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/03_卷二_发生.md
29. 势 (propensity) is neither force (which has an agent and a patient) nor a thing; it is the tendency self-shown by many 几 in succession — direction without purpose. In v13.2 it is made structure-relative rather than manifold-dependent (势_Γ := D_{K_Γ} w_Γ) and must always come with interval domain, structure, weight, difference, magnitude and comparability: **无结构，不言势；无权重，不言强**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §四; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/06_卷四_元、几、势、机.md
30. Direction (已发生之倾向) and purpose (预设之终态) differ in their relation to time. Direction without a final purpose keeps the process unceasing; pressing toward a final purpose drives the process to stop there — **有方向无目的则过程不息；有目的而强趋之，则过程将止于此目的而息**. Teleology is a closure mechanism. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §四; «repo»/…/04_补篇二…/04_二、元、几、势、机.md
31. The saving qualification: revisable, discardable, situation-adaptable *local* goals are instruments of action; only a *terminal* purpose that cannot be revised, discarded or adapted is a shackle of closure. [conditional] — D5/生生不息论_v9_开境护闭校正版.md §四
32. Time has no independent existence — it is the relation of events to events — so 机 is 势之自我临界, the propensity's self-criticality, not a point on a clock. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §五
33. Generation and extinction, gathering and dispersal, manifest and hidden are two faces of one process; 灭 is a change of form, and what has been enters the universe's irreversible accumulation. Hence the 生 in 生生不息 is the total dynamic of gathering and dispersing that does not cease, not "born and never dying". [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §六
34. But death and collapse are not explained away: if a pattern's invariants pass into no successor and the paths it opened are all closed, that is 真坍 — **死与坍缩为真——此论不诡辩生灭一相而忽视真绝**. If many paths close at once, that is collapse. [observation] — D5/生生不息论_v_10_间生形式版.md §七; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/03_卷二_发生.md
35. Every polarity has two extremes; **极** is structurally the process's self-closure, because continuation depends on the two poles converting into one another. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §七
36. 积 (sedimentation) is the process stabilised at some scale — the a priori, tradition, genes, language, civilisation, self, character, habit are all 积; **积即过程，过程即积**. Ren's reason for adopting the term: 「积淀这个定义不错，这是一种变化关系，不需要明确主体」. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十一; `out-hb-03.md` #87
37. **积生积**: sedimentation sediments further sedimentation, both by same-scale accumulation and by cross-scale emergence (molecules → cell), so an established structure opens rather than merely limits what follows. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十二
38. **因生因**: causation recurses — a new structure does not add a point to possibility space, it extends possibility space into a new dimension (matter → chemistry → life → nervous systems → language → civilisation → artificial minds). Formally 开维: an interval whose legality criterion was simply *undefined* at the earlier time. **因生因者，间之自我开维也.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十三; D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十三
39. **自相似之节**: the same principle manifests isomorphically at every scale — cell, multicellular body, mind, relationship, community, society, civilisation, species, planet, cosmos — 牽一髮而動全身，非比喻也，乃自相似之实也. Stated functorially, the openness predicate has the same logical form across scales (a natural transformation commuting with coarsening and refinement), and that formal sameness is precisely what makes cross-scale *difference* comparable: **自相似与尺度差，互为支柱**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十四; D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十二
40. 理 is neither before events, nor inside a particular event, nor outside events: 理 is the event's self-manifesting as coherence — 事之自显为条贯之事自身; and 「理无根据，以其本非有据之物」 — asking for the ground of 理 already misconstrues it as a thing. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §八; «repo»/…/04_补篇二…/09_七、理、心、情、积.md
41. 心 is the process's self-sensing focus at some place and scale — one body's, one group's, one age's, one species' — mind and body being two aspects, not two things. Formally 心(a,Γ) ⟺ a ∈ A ∧ 感_a(I_F(Γ)) ∧ 择_a ∧ 可校_a: a focus that can sense, choose and be corrected. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §九; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/14_卷十二….md
