42. 情 is the mind's self-sensing as it shows in relation, formalised as a weighting on intervals — 间入心而有轻重; 情之得正 is affect that stays open, neither suppressed nor extremized. 理-心-情 are three aspects of one process. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/14_卷十二….md
43. **性 is not a further thing**, not an external mandate, not an innate quality: 性 is taking 生生不息 as one's mode of existing, so that my nature and the universe's nature are one nature at different scales. Formalised later as a stable interval-selection pattern, an attractor of long accumulation — hence **修身者，改其择间之势**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十七; D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §三十
44. **宇宙即理**: the universe does not have or obey 理; its self-unfolding *is* 理, and we are not knowers of it but one concrete generated form of it. [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十六
45. The 間→位→量→数→算→系统→反馈 chain gives a genesis path for discrete mathematics and computation — but the text explicitly denies this exhausts mathematics: continuity, space, infinity, probability and proof-legitimacy each need extra structure or postulates. [conditional] — «repo»/…/04_补篇二…/04_二、元、几、势、机.md
46. An earlier five-layer 間 ontology replaces "元 + three phases + field" with 三本 → 三显 → 三征 → 開/閉 → 網/體/流: three irreducible primitives 物 (0-dimensional, countable), 動 (n-dimensional, extended), 間 (topological, no intrinsic metric); 14 Tier-1 characters. [mechanism] — «repo»/史/间论早期/间本体论_元三相与场修订稿.md
47. In that ontology each manifestation is an intersection of two primitives with the third *bracketed out but not absent*: 位 = 物 ∩ 間 (動 bracketed), 場 = 動 ∩ 物 (間 bracketed), 際 = 間 ∩ 動 (物 bracketed) — with the Lean witness `every_manifestation_brackets_absent_root`. [mechanism] — «repo»/史/间论早期/间本体论_元三相与场修订稿.md
48. 位 has no persisting static face, and this is a structural declaration rather than missing data: zero dimensions cannot hold the "persisting vs changing" split apart. Composition is emergent and irreversible — 位 + 際 do not uniquely determine a 網. [mechanism] — «repo»/史/间论早期/间本体论_元三相与场修订稿.md
49. The ontology is self-instantiating: 「它是一个間-结构；当被运用时为動；在某个论域上成为場；生成具体的位；经由際与其它位相连；织成網」 (`ontology_is_instance_of_its_own_shape`). [mechanism] — «repo»/史/间论早期/间本体论_元三相与场修订稿.md
50. Ren's own derivation of the ontology, stated before any of the formal versions: different combinations produce more difference; the appearance of structure supplies the possibility of new structures appearing; cause becomes effect and effect again becomes cause; and in practical relation order rose — cells appeared, we appeared, civilisation appeared. 「宇宙的历史和我们自身的存在告诉我们，生生不息」. [observation] — `out-hb-03.md` #90 (2026-05-02)
51. Ren's second step, identifying 理 with a growing set: 「沉淀沉淀出了更多的沉淀…所以说理也在不断增加，宇宙即是理，我们也是理，理在生生不息，我们也在生生不息，这个是我们的性」. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #91
52. Ren's own root-revision, running in parallel with the 間 turn: 「根位非事，而是一也 / 一 → 物/動/間/心/事，事有开闭，有好坏，有生灭」; and 「递归并自相似，应该才是道」; and 「道即是动，也是非动，不矛盾」. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #179, #195, #200
53. Ren's causal-loop formulation of 道, not carried into any draft: 因(果) → 果 and 果(因) → 因; 因果轮回即是道; 乃似阴阳，太极，波粒也; **果乃因，因乃果，一也**. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #217 (2026-05-04)
54. Ren's model of the future as a continuous, uncertain DAG / Markov chain constructed *out of the past* so that the future can be more 生 rather than collapsing — which doubles as an account of how 理 is obtained, and is explicitly held as one model among many in an 无常 modelling space that one navigates by feel. [design direction] — `out-hb-03.md` #117–#119
55. Ren's negative-knowability epistemics: 「我们在系统中，是系统，这是一个多层次自相似规律，且复杂，长期演进不可知，但可知何为非生，道非可道，生生不息也」 — the long-run evolution is unknowable, but what is *not* generative is knowable. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #265 (2026-05-05)

### C. 开 / 闭 and their kinds

56. The root category is not "possibility space" but **开/闭**. 开 = 未 × 可 × 应 × 转 — an unexhausted domain remains, becoming is still reachable, response is still possible, revision and recovery are still possible — and lacking any one, 开 is incomplete. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §七; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/08_卷六_开、闭、稳、真.md
57. The replacement was made *because* "expanding possibility space" sounds measurable, maximisable and optimisable, while what is meant is: do not close the not-yet-become, do not compress becoming into an endgame — 开者，未成之域不闭也. [design direction] — `out-hb-03.md` #105 (2026-05-02, imported and adopted by Ren)
58. 开/闭 is prior and 善/恶 is its ethical manifestation, not the reverse: **生生不息者，开而不闭也；善者，护开也；恶者，成闭也**. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #106; D5/生生不息论_v9.md §七
59. 开 is a 态, not a 量: "more open here" is not "more options here". A tea ceremony can be maximally open and a totalitarian system maximally closed — **繁简非开闭之标**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §七
60. Closure has two senses that must never be conflated: **成闭** = closure that does not reopen (the evil); **护闭** = closing one place to protect a wider openness — refusing an aggressor, quarantining an epidemic, excising a cancer, halting a runaway process, bounding a manipulator, keeping a tradition's core. **故护开非无边界、无限放任、永不拒绝.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §七; `out-hb-03.md` #111
61. Protective closure carries binding conditions rather than a name: 护闭 = 闭 ∧ 限期 ∧ 可修 ∧ 归开. The test: does openness actually rise; is it merely capture in disguise; is it still revisable and reversible; does it refuse to install itself as final? [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v9_开境护闭校正版.md §七其五; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/10_卷八_共开、夺、护.md
62. Every 极 is a form of 成闭 — 极一 closes difference, 极控 closes spontaneity, 极爱 swallows the other's own openness, 极知 seals the unknown, 极善 hardens into violence, 极智 fossilises into prejudice: the danger is in the structure, never in the endpoint. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §七
63. **伪开** is 成闭's most concealed form and the main shape of modern evil: **外显为开，内成其闭；以一时之可，夺长远之未；以局部之开，闭全境之开**. Named instances: technological monopoly, addictive systems, extreme ideologies, Moloch-style competition, runaway optimisation, unbounded "freedom", and pluralism in name that enforces uniformity in fact. [observation] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §七; `out-hb-03.md` #108
64. 伪开's cross-time signature: `μ(x) = |I(x)|` is presently large, but the action naturally induced *by x's very appearance of openness* drives μ sharply down after k steps — so detecting it requires long-horizon recognition, not inspection of the present. [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §8.2 · §8.3
65. 伪开 has exactly three types under the openness decomposition: I local-open / global-close; II short-horizon-open / long-horizon-close; III surface-open / meta-close. **成闭为恶；护闭为中；伪开为闭之诈.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十一
66. 似 / 实 / 伪 decomposed so that 伪开 becomes derived rather than primitive: 似(x,P) = has P's appearance without its reality; 实(x,P) = P holds *and* is trace-checkable *and* survives calibration; therefore **伪开 = 似开 ∧ 实闭**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v13.1_全字元生成闭合本.md §六三
67. **元闭 (meta-closure)**: not one gap closed, but the rules that generate valid intervals themselves frozen and unrevisable — the deepest kind, because a system in 元闭 still has moves and cannot change what counts as a move. Its dual is 开维 (dimension-opening). [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十一 · §十三
68. Closure is stratified four ways and then seven: **绝闭** (I = ∅), **机闭** (intervals exist but the state is not alive to them — 有间、有机，而不能启开), **成闭** (closure produced by a specific act), **元闭** (the generating rule frozen); plus the three causal modes **夺闭**, **蔽闭**, **伪闭**. 无间曰绝闭，有间而不活曰机能闭，因行而闭曰成闭，法冻不可修曰元闭. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇八; D5/生生不息论_v13.1_全字元生成闭合本.md §六二
69. **夺 (capture)** has an action form and a state form; the state form is decisive — one focus's openness *structurally depends* on the suppression of relevant others'. **夺者，非彼此差异，乃以彼之闭为己之开.** Mere co-occurrence of one rising and another falling is not 夺; the dependency (依) is what makes it capture. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/10_卷八_共开、夺、护.md
70. **护开 is given a positive test**: the co-openness delta is non-negative, the act is not a bad act, it is not capture, and it survives calibration. Merely "not closing" is not 护开. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十一
71. **开度** must be a graded function `O_F(Γ; s, ω)` composed from 未 · 可 · 应 · 转, not a boolean — 开谓其是否仍活，开度谓其活得几何 — and the composition must be monotone, **short-board sensitive**, **anti-capture**, scale-explicit and auditable. The conservative form is `min(U, A, R, T)`; weighted forms must not mask a short board. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇七; D5/生生不息论_v12.1_字元闭合本.md §六二
72. **共开 (co-openness)** carries the same requirements plus difference-preservation, and 和 = 共开 ∧ 流 ∧ ¬状态夺 ∧ 存异, where 存异 = 异可显 ∧ 异可通 ∧ ¬同化 — **和者，诸开相通而不相吞**. [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/10_卷八_共开、夺、护.md; D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §十一
73. **正 / 邪 at the act level**: 正 = 合 ∧ 法 ∧ 归开 (归开 := 护开 ∨ 护闭) ∧ ¬夺 ∧ ¬伪 ∧ ¬冻 ∧ 审校不败; 邪 = 偏 ∧ (伪 ∨ 夺 ∨ 非护闭 ∨ 蔽 ∨ 冻). **邪者，偏而自续，以伪、夺、闭、蔽、冻为径.** The v13 revision separates the act from its effect: **邪为行之偏，坏为邪之效**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v13.1_全字元生成闭合本.md §九一 · §九二; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/11_卷九_正邪.md
74. **坏 (bad)** on the effect side: 闭 ∨ 状态夺 ∨ 伪开 ∨ 元闭 ∨ 邪势, where 邪势 is high tension combined with calibration failure and any of capture, concealment, freezing or counterfeit. [mechanism] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/12_卷十_平、和、好、坏.md
75. **稳 (stability)** is dynamic: ∀ disturbances r, ¬坍(x,r) ∧ 回观不败(x) ∧ 续(x) — survives disturbance, survives retrospective audit, still continues. [mechanism] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/08_卷六_开、闭、稳、真.md
76. **真** is split into two sense-positions that must not be conflated: 真₁ (a statement matching what is the case, Γ ⊨ P) and 真₂ (度期周 ∧ calibration-unbeaten across all relevant scales and horizons) — 真1者，言合所是也。真2者，凡所及皆周，受校而不败也. Truth in the second sense is a calibration-survival property, not a correspondence property. [mechanism] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/08_卷六_开、闭、稳、真.md; D5/生生不息论_v13.1_全字元生成闭合本.md §六四
77. Three explicit anti-metric guards, kept from v9 through every later version: (i) 生生不息 ≠ complexity necessarily rising — simplification, letting go, transformation and return to stillness are equally its manifestations; (ii) rising complexity ≠ good — more sophisticated exploitation, finer surveillance, deeper addiction are all complexity increases and all evil; (iii) 善 ≠ maximising any metric, because every maximisation is a latent extreme. **善不在于最大化什么，善在于不闭何处.** [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §〇
78. The corresponding refusal in the formal texts: reading 生生不息 as monotone complexity growth is 以可测之指标还原不可测之相续，本论所拒 — reducing an unmeasurable continuation to a measurable index. [value stance] — «repo»/…/04_补篇二…/11_九、因生因.md
79. Restatement of the whole theory in one line: **生生不息者，开之不绝也** — not "ever more complex", not "ever more", not "ever more efficient". And the act-level pair: **善不在多，善在护开；恶不在少，恶在成闭**. [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §七 · §二十三
80. The formal criterion of good and evil is scale-free while its content is always situational: **善恶之形式判准有无尺度之限，而其具体内容皆境性** — so the ethics is neither a dead rule-book nor arbitrary relativism. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §二十三; `out-hb-03.md` #115
81. 生生 cannot be extinguished as a thing, because it is not a thing — but particular openings can be closed, particular situations destroyed, particular 仁 worn away. Hence **非因间生不息之保证而懈怠；乃因诸具体之开之可闭而急切**: urgency comes from the closability of particulars, not complacency from the indestructibility of the whole. [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §二十六; «repo»/…/04_补篇二…/15_十三….md

### D. 境, 模, and the audit

82. **境 is prior to 物**: nothing stands free of a situation, and every "thing" — a cup, a person, a community, a planet — is a relatively stable focus within a 境. 境先于物而显，物乃境中之聚焦. The robust form of the claim, after Ren's own over-statement was corrected: **无离境自立之物；凡物，皆境中相对稳定之聚焦** — what is denied is the entity standing free of relation, process and scale, not the cup. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八; `out-hb-03.md` #114, #120
83. All recognition of a 境 is a **模** (model) — branch, propensity, field, network, layer are all models, all situational, none absolute. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八
84. Models nevertheless have better and worse, and the criterion is whether they better 显理、识机、辨伪、护开、导行. Pure-profit models of politics, pure-power models of relationship and pure-efficiency models of civilisation are bad models because of what they close. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八; `out-hb-03.md` #112
85. Model quality is itself situational: strict ritual may protect order in a time of chaos and become closure in a settled age. Absolutising one model closes recognition; abandoning ranking for relativism closes discernment; both are failures of 智. Treating a model as exhaustive is itself a closure mode — **模闭**. [conditional] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/05_卷四_境与模.md
86. **道之可感**: within a situation Dao can be felt though not measured from outside — feeling a propensity about to turn, sensing whether openings are mutually completing or mutually closing, sensing whether this step's road is clear or blocked. Not mysticism, but the direct response of an accumulated participant to the live whole of a situation. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八
87. **四校 (the four calibrations)** guard against a private feeling calling itself Dao: 境之回响 (the situation's echo), 他者之回应 (others' responses), 后果之显现 (downstream consequence appearing), 诸模之互证 (cross-model corroboration). **未经四校之感，或只是欲、只是怕、只是积之惯性.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八; `out-hb-03.md` #113
88. The predicate-level restatement of the same four: 异入 (dissent admitted into the process) ∧ 众应 (affected parties can respond and their response enters) ∧ 迹查 (traces exist and are checkable) ∧ 互证 (two sources corroborate that are neither same-origin nor same-interest). [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/09_卷七_审校全拆.md
89. **审 (scrutiny)** adds five negative conditions: 源须独, 受须入, 受不可黜, 迹不可蔽, 证源不可同执 — sources independent, affected parties admitted and not expelled, traces not concealed, corroborating sources not captured by one interest or narrative. [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/09_卷七_审校全拆.md
90. There is a **second-order audit**: 审校²(x) ⟺ 审校不败(x) ∧ 审校不败(C_x) — the audit process must itself survive audit. **校验若被捕获，校验亦成伪开.** [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/09_卷七_审校全拆.md; D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十三
91. **The four calibrations are not guaranteed to converge.** If the references share a blind spot, if one calibration's weight is too strong, or if references contradict, the corrected sense can converge on a shared bias or oscillate. **「四校近道」是工夫之指，非定理之结.** [conditional] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §7.5
92. Calibration can expose but cannot substitute: 「四校能助显伪、显未尽，不能代行」 — audit can reveal falsity and unexhaustedness; it cannot act in your place. [design direction] — «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/05_卷四_境与模.md
93. 「在道上」 is not subjective self-confidence but **持续受境校正之行** — a practice of continual correction, never a possession. [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八
94. **格物致知 recast**: 格 = recognising this situation's live whole (propensity, timing, polar tensions, opening/closing thresholds, relations among foci); 物 = a temporary focus, not a self-standing thing; 致知 = that recognition becoming directly a guide to action and being continually refined by calibration. Hence 知行合一 means 知本不离行. Formalised as a loop: act → residue → history → 史合法 recomputed → discernment → next act. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §八; D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §二十四
95. 格物致知 is what closes the gap the formal texts had left open by declaring legality 境性，本文不予指定 — legality is extracted from accumulated history by form, cause and structure verification (Φ_形 ∧ Φ_因 ∧ Φ_结). [mechanism] — «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/05_卷四_境与模.md
96. 知 and 行 are two faces of one event, not a sequence: real knowing carries a direction of action; real action carries a form of knowing. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十九
97. **积 can shape or lock**: 活积 keeps its capacity for self-revision and protects openness; 死积 is frozen into absolute rule, induces meta-closure, and eventually collapses. The whole question of tradition reduces to whether the 积 keeps its revision mechanism. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §十六 · §十七; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/07_卷六_心物.md

### E. The value chain

98. **中** is not a midpoint, a compromise or an average; it is the dynamic that keeps neither pole's openness closed — a structural necessity of continuation, not a moral virtue and not a wise choice. And 中 is not one place: it is the non-extreme point *of each particular situation*, so recognising it is 临场的、境性的、动态的, never a universal formula. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §七; D5/生生不息论_v9.md §九
99. Ren's own statement of the same: 「不能『极』，对于对偶的维度，不能黑白思考，应该寻找中…它是一种智慧，middle way，道」 — and he asks for 中 to be *derived* rather than asserted. [value stance] — `out-hb-03.md` #101
100. **和** is the state of a multi-focus field in which differences are preserved *and* tension circulates: 和 = 多样性 × 流通性. Its two field-extremes are the extreme of struggle (one focus expanding at another's cost) and the extreme of separation (fixed, non-flowing). [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §八
101. **和非同**: complete homogeneity is the loss of difference and therefore of mutual generation — 和's opposite, not its perfection. 史伯: 和实生物，同则不继；以他平他谓之和. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §八; D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十五章
102. Ren's derivation sketch for 和: 「和 — field 没有非常大的扰动，没有很多的混乱（往往来自于斗争）」, with 美 and 德 likewise derivable. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #102
103. **美** = the state in which a mind, meeting some event, experiences its own possibility expand — not conformity to norm (正), not usefulness (利), not satisfaction of desire (快). Beauty always sits at a fine mean: perfect symmetry is not beautiful (极同则趣闭), complete chaos is not beautiful. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §九
104. **德** = a focus whose 积 (character, habit, response pattern) has stabilised toward the mean, so that fitting response no longer needs recomputation: not an external norm, not an innate gift, but the tendency of accumulation. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §十
105. 中 · 和 · 美 · 德 are one structure in four dimensions: intrinsic (a single process's polarity), lateral (multi-focus field), encounter (mind meets event), longitudinal (time and accumulation). And 善、美、德三者，异名同实. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §十
106. **仁** is the only non-contracting mode of one focus responding to another: not as resource, not as obstacle, not as identical with oneself, not as absolute other, but as **同根异显** — another concrete form of the same process. 仁 = 关系中之「中」. Formalised: **己续 ⊆_实 共续** with no 夺依 — one's own conditions of continuation really contained within the shared conditions, with no element of one's continuation depending on another's suppression. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §二十二; D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §十八
107. **义** = recognising and enacting the mean of *this* situation's propensity — situational, responsive, non-extreme. **仁定其向，义具其行.** Formally 可行机 ∧ 可择 ∧ 正行: a real bifurcation, the ability to choose, and choosing the right interval. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §二十三; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/13_卷十一….md
108. **礼** = the stable, transmissible form of fitting action, necessary because no one can recompute the mean from zero each time; but **礼必含「礼之自我修订」之机制**, otherwise it becomes 死礼 and the concrete form of 不仁不义 — 以礼杀人. Formalised as a ritual pattern lacking a revision operator ρ that keeps recommending action out of accord with 义 with no correction path. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §二十三; D5/间生论_形式补.md §9.4
109. **智** = the capacity to recognise the mean through the mutual correction of concept, feeling and experience, and crucially 识此识之局限之能: **不识自己之识之局限者，不为智者**. Its seven concrete competences: cross-scale recognition, long-horizon recognition, multi-agent recognition, detection of pseudo-openness, discrimination of protective from achieved closure, recognition of models' situatedness and ranking, and calibration of one's own feeling. And 智 ≠ 知识 — knowledge is 积, wisdom is the use of 积 in new contexts. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §二十三; D5/生生不息论_v9.md §二十五
110. **信** = the internal through-coherence of a focus across speech, action, knowledge, feeling and accumulation; a split focus is itself misaligned because it has internally contracted into mutually opposed fragments. **信非不变。变而有理，乃信；守旧而自欺，亦失信.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §二十三; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/09_卷八_五常.md
111. The five are not five independent virtues but one alignment in five interpersonal dimensions, each hollow without the others: **无仁之义即冷酷，无义之仁即滥爱；无礼之仁义难传，无仁义之礼即僵；无智之仁义礼即盲，无仁义礼之智即诡；无信之上述四者皆为表演.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v5.md §二十三
112. Ren asked for exactly this addition, replacing 人 with 仁 as the ethical anchor: 「人换成仁，应该会更好…仁义礼智其实都可以，这些我们加上才完整」. [design direction] — `out-hb-03.md` #103
113. **平 (ping)** is introduced as a distinct value: `Ping(Γ) ⇔ ‖势_Γ‖ < θ_calm ∧ Open_F(Γ)` — calm *and* alive, sharply distinguished from calm-and-dead, since low gradient without openness is simply a closed field. **平者，势近零而开未失也.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十八
114. **正危 (rightful crisis)**: high propensity-norm, at a 機, no capture, no meta-closure, dimension-opening still reachable, calibration unbeaten. Added specifically so that generative suffering is not misclassified as bad. Hence **好非舒适**. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十二; «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
115. **好 is an interval, not a point**: 好 ⇔ 平 ∨ 和 ∨ 正危, with 不好 as the exterior on both sides — closure-by-stagnation below (落于死) and capture-by-violence above (入于暴). Symmetrically: 好 = (平 ∨ 和 ∨ 正危) ∧ ¬不好; 不好 = 机能闭 ∨ 元闭 ∨ 状态夺 ∨ 未校高张. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §二十七; D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §十三
116. Choosing between 平 and 和 is wisdom, not morality: **the polarity within the good is not a polarity between good and bad**. [observation] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §二十七
117. **自由 = 不向坏** — the individual-scale projection of 好. `IntentionGood ⇒ Free` but not conversely: freedom is the necessary floor, good the supererogatory direction. A constrained focus can be free; an unconstrained focus disposed toward capture is not. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §二十八
118. **Freedom requires reality-reachability**: the choice set is restricted to intervals actually reachable given resources, capabilities, knowledge, institutional position, bodily condition and risk tolerance; freedom = a non-bad path set that is non-empty, not coerced away, discernible, and traceable to the agent's own structure. **自由者，非纸上有路，乃现实可达、可识、且不被坏径所迫.** The earlier universal form (∀ choices ¬不好) was rejected as too strong. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十四; «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
119. **繁荣 (prosperity)** = all foci free ∧ co-open ∧ relationally flowing — 诸自由共开而不相食. "Freedom for all" is half a definition; the other half is "without mutual capture", or free agents compose into a Hobbesian field that is collectively closed. Explicitly not GDP, not a metric, not quantity of accumulation. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §二十九
120. The ladder of the Way: **自由 → 平 → 和 → 繁荣 → 生生不息**, each row strictly stronger than the last; **不向坏者，自由也；向好者，道也；己续入共续者，仁也；自由、和平、仁与道，是为存在之生生不息**. [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三十; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/20_卷十八_终言.md
121. Ren derived this axis himself and derived it as an argument *for peace*: ∇平 = the field is not perturbed; 和 = the field's perturbation is open; therefore 和平 is a direction, and at the material and structural level a constraint; our intention interval should lie between 平 and 和 — and this is 仁. 「这就是为什么我们应该追求和平。」 [value stance] — `out-hb-03.md` #127–#131 (2026-05-03)
122. Ritual order can lift a field above the floor: `P[Harmony | Order ∧ Free] > P[Harmony | Free alone]` — freedom is the precondition of consensus, consensus of ritual-order, ritual-order of harmony and prosperity. Ren's own version: freedom is the individual's maximised 好 and a 道 direction, so consensus is built on freedom — but need not stop there, since by constructing order one can reach conditions better than freedom's. [conditional] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三十; `out-hb-03.md` #130
123. **善 = 义 ∧ 正筛**: goodness is not only choosing rightly at this 機 but shifting the selection so that open-protecting acts become likelier and capture-type acts less likely — ΔPr(正行 | 筛) > 0 ∧ ΔPr(邪行 | 筛) < 0 ∧ calibration-unbeaten. **善者，使好更易生，使伪开难久，使成闭难成.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §十七
124. **道 must not have its subject left empty**, or anything persisting inside a good field could call itself Dao: 道⁰ = sufficient continuation capacity; 道⁺ = contributes non-negatively to co-openness, does not worsen the bad, does not gain by capture; **真道** = 道 ∧ 度期周 ∧ calibration-unbeaten across all *relevant* scales and windows. **道非物自称道，乃其存在方式在相关尺度中不夺共续.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十七 · §十九
125. The universal quantifier in 真道 was deliberately restricted from "all scales and horizons" to "all *relevant* scales and horizons", because the unrestricted form is almost unsatisfiable: **道非万度无代价，乃于相关度期中，不以一端之续闭其共续**. [design direction] — «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
126. **善 = 成生，恶 = 绝生** in the prose formulation: good preserves the basic conditions of life and relation, increases truth, trust and cooperation, expands capability and responsibility, lets difference coexist, keeps room for correction and renewal, and does not sustain itself by overdrawing others and the future; evil systematically instrumentalises others, corrupts shared cognition by deception, exhausts common resources, manufactures obedience through fear, substitutes victory for truth and accumulation for safety, and destroys whole-system generation for local optimisation. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第六章
127. **Good is not "keep every option open"**: 无限之开放，即混乱. Limits come in two kinds — **成生之限** (giving up shallow options to build deeper capacity: an apprenticeship, a promise, a law against harm) and **绝生之限** (compressing the subject itself so it can no longer become). What good protects is **有秩序、有尊严、可修正、可持续之可能性空间**, not option count. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第六章; D7/generative_existence.md §3
128. Good and evil are not convention, not interest-projection, not cultural relativism — because cross-cultural, cross-species and cross-era moral discussion actually happens; because harming others to benefit oneself is recognised as evil even when it benefits me; and because massacring innocents is not made good by a culture endorsing it. [observation] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十八 **Current clarification:** this earlier argument against relativism is preserved as genealogy; cross-cultural discussion itself does not prove moral universality. Claims 450–451 state the current normative commitment and its limits. [Source](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md).
129. Doing good is *not* simply choosing the expansive side: it is recognising the mean on each polarity in each situation — killing contracts, but so does a protection of all life so total that one cannot eat or defend oneself. [conditional] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十八
130. **人 is not a biological category**, not a bloodline, not the property of a culture: 人者，能识此理而以行体之者也 — including ancient sages, ordinary people now, future artificial minds, and alien intelligences; and 人 is the reflexive moment of the process, the focus at which 生生不息 becomes conscious of itself as 生生不息. Ren's own statement: 「知道此理则为人 — 给出『humanity』的定义」. [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十九; `out-hb-03.md` #94
131. **做人要善** is the whole ontology, ethics and alignment theory compressed into one plain sentence — not a slogan but the condensation of the argument; formalised as `canonicalDoingHumanAim = AimKind.alignLife`, with `control` explicitly *excluded* from the definition of 做人. [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §二十; «repo»/formal-notes/HumanAlignment.md
132. Ren's own formulation of it: 「我们的方向是做人（广泛的人，包括你也可以算），做人要善，这个才是真正的alignment」. [value stance] — `out-hb-03.md` #95 (2026-05-02)
133. **善是方向，不是清单：生之，通之，纠之，不闭之** — good as a direction with four verbs: generate it, connect it, correct it, do not close it. [value stance] — `_process/reading-notes/out-cb-07.md` #130 (2026-07-07)
134. **业 (karma) demythologised**: not a ledger of divine reward but the structural residue of action in three places — shaping oneself (act → habit → character → next judgement), shaping others (changing their beliefs, fears, capacities and choices), shaping the environment (repeated action becomes rule, culture, institution, shared expectation). 果为后因 — structurally identical to 生生. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十九章
135. **行 has three levels of effect** — the immediate state, the interval set, and the generation rule — and 责 (responsibility) is the sum of all three: 责(a,Γ,i) := 效(Γ') ⊕ 效(I_F(Γ')) ⊕ 效(合法_F(·,Γ')). **行者，择间以续；深行者，亦改后来可择之间.** This is why alignment cannot stop at immediate safety. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §二十八; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/14_卷十二….md
136. A benevolent agent's formal signature: for every successor state its action can produce, the successor's interval set stays above an openness floor — 仁者之行不令后续态之间之集过窄. [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §4.3
137. Pursuing a single terminal goal necessarily atrophies intervals: if every act is judged only by proximity to `x*`, the remaining intervals are discarded and the field's total interval set cumulatively shrinks — the algebraic root of **极皆成闭**. [mechanism] — D5/间生论_形式补.md §5.5

### F. The verification discipline

138. The question shifts from "does the doctrine cohere?" to **如何判？如何防伪？如何防尺度偷换？如何防开维滥用？如何防四校被捕获？如何在不可定时悬置？** — v11's purpose is 立判准之判准, the criterion of criteria. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md 序
139. Four named abuse modes of an unverified value system: **尺度任意化** (choosing only the scale and window that flatter you), **开维滥用** (invoking novelty to bypass historical warrant and consequence), **四校伪装** (faking calibration through power, group illusion or information blockage), **价值递归失稳** (good, benevolence and Dao mutually supporting each other with no convergent criterion). [observation] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇一
140. Every claim of 开 / 好 / 自由 / 道 must carry an explicit scale `s` and time window `ω`: **无尺度之开，常成幻开；无时间窗之好，常成短视之好**. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇二
141. **Relevant-scale closure**: scales may not be chosen at will. Causal relevance, affected-party relevance and externality closure force scales into the judgement — **凡被影响者，皆有入判之权**; if an effect spills over to another scale or horizon, that scale is in the relevant set as a matter of law. The value-term is **度期周**, and 周 was separated from 闭 because 闭 already means closure: **周非闭；周者，无漏也**. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十五; D5/生生不息论_v12.1_字元闭合本.md §六四
142. **Legality is three-layered**: 史合法 (historical warrant — recognisable form, traceable cause, structural invariants unbroken), 暂合法 (provisional — novel, non-contradictory, calibration-unbeaten, admissible only as a bounded, time-limited, damage-limited, reversible trial and not as institutional legality), 强开维合法 (provisional plus actual open-extension plus retrospective stabilisation plus renewed calibration). **无据则妄，无试则僵，无校则诈**; **史法守其据，开法开其未；无史法则妄，无开法则僵**. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇五; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/07_卷五….md
143. Logical negation `¬i` is replaced by an **alternative-path set** `Alt(Γ,i)` with a **baseline alternative**, because the negation of an interval need not itself be a legal interval. **行之校验，不可只问此路如何，亦须问不走此路将如何.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇六
144. **Value predicates are mutually recursive**, so they are placed in a complete lattice with a monotone operator: the least fixed point gives the conservative criterion, the greatest fixed point the persistence criterion, and where monotonicity or convergence fails the verdict is **待校** — one may not seal the words 善, 仁, 道, 真. **定义可互照，不可互偷.** A polarity table records each predicate's positive and negative dependencies. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md §〇十八; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/15_卷十三_递归与不动点.md
145. **Recursion polarity**: a positive cycle can be closed with a fixed point, a negative cycle cannot — 正环可收束，负环须待校；互照可用，互偷不可. Negative cycles get one of three treatments: stratification (facts first, values second), three-valued assignment, or external audit (new traces, evidence, affected-party responses). [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/02_卷〇_全字元生成律.md
146. **Three-valued conservative logic**: 𝐕 = {⊤, ⊥, U} with ¬U = U, ⊤∧U = U, ⊥∧U = ⊥, ⊤∨U = ⊤, ⊥∨U = U; and the load-bearing norm **未知非假，未校非真；待校者，不得冒称道、仁、善、真**. [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/02_卷〇_全字元生成律.md
147. **三值保守律 U ⇏ ⊤** generalised: an undecided state may never be silently promoted to true; it may only legitimately collapse under evidence. This governs logic (excluded middle fails in K3), statistics (failure to reject ≠ acceptance), arithmetic (a constructive attitude) and metatheory (a Gödel sentence stays U). [value stance] — «repo»/义理/K_完备性审计.md §六
148. **审校 (v12.2 正名)** replaces 鲁校, dropping a calque of "robust" for a Chinese term with its own semantics: **审者，详察而后定；校者，异源相参而检妄**; likewise 现实可达 → 实达. Naming work, not new doctrine. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v12.2_审校正名本.md 序
149. **Six decision protocols** are specified as ordered step lists — 开闭判定 (9 steps), 行动判定 (10 steps), 义判定, 善判定, 仁判定, 道判定 (10 steps ending 关键度期不可观测，判待校). [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/16_卷十四_判定协议.md
150. A run takes (Γ, s, ω, B, ℋ_t, Θ, ℳ_sel) and returns (I_F, Π_开, 𝒪_F, 𝒞𝒪_F, ActLabel, ValueLabel, Audit), where ActLabel ⊇ {非法, 正行, 邪行, 护闭, U} and ValueLabel ⊇ {义, 善, 仁, 道, U}; missing data yields U — **不得以缺证替代通过**. [design direction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/01_〇一、可运行输入与输出.md
151. Openness is extracted as four real-valued features (U, A, R, T) ∈ [0,1]⁴ with explicit formulas; an unobservable component must be marked U and **may not be defaulted to 0 or 1**. [design direction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/02_〇二、四维开度特征.md
152. Three aggregation models are supplied (short-side min, weighted geometric, guarded mean) with the rule that the choice is recorded in the audit, high-risk defaults to the min form, and conflicting models force U or a higher audit level. The community-level aggregator has the same three-way choice, and for basic rights, irreversible harm, children, medicine, justice, war or high-risk automation the default is forced to the min form. [design direction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/03_〇三….md, /04_〇四….md
153. Π_开 is a projection *package* (U, A, R, T, 𝒪_F, status, evidence, model) with a **double threshold**: ⊤ only if both the aggregate and the worst dimension clear the upper bounds, ⊥ if either falls below the lower, otherwise U. [design direction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/05_〇五….md
154. Audit gets a data schema — event record, trace table, affected-party response table (voice, valence, harm, consent, confidence), model cross-check table, outcome-by-horizon table — and a quality score taken as a **minimum** across trace, affected-party, corroboration, source-independence and horizon components; below threshold the value verdict degrades to U. [design direction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/06_〇六、审校数据结构.md
155. Thresholds are set by a five-step procedure (anchor cases, harm irreversibility, sensitivity analysis across aggregators, counterfactual baselines including inaction and protective closure, recorded provenance) with three default bands: 0.45/0.55 low-risk, 0.55/0.65 normal governance, 0.70/0.80 high-risk irreversible. [design direction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/07_〇七、阈值设定法.md
156. **The worked case**: a recommender system with three candidate actions. Maximising dwell time is 邪行 because platform growth *depends on* closing user attention and autonomy (夺) while apparent choice increases as responsiveness and repairability fall (伪开). A multi-objective, user-controllable, anti-addiction, diversity-constrained, externally audited alternative passes 正行 → 义 → 善 → 仁. Pausing the high-risk channel is 护闭 provided it is term-bound and returns power to the affected. [prediction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/09_〇九….md
157. Even in the passing case the label is held at **候选真道** rather than 已证真道, because the three-year horizon is prediction, not observation — the pending exit is kept open by design. [design direction] — «repo»/…/02_补篇〇…/09_〇九….md
158. **A twelve-case counterexample battery** is built into the doctrine so it can be falsified in use: many options can still be functional closure; protective closure is what power most easily counterfeits; local freedom obtained by capture is not prosperity; suffering can be rightful crisis; tradition can become meta-closure (**其名虽礼，其实礼之尸**); novelty without warrant is not dimension-opening; stillness is not necessarily peace; local good that seizes global continuity is not true Dao; captured calibration is itself pseudo-openness (**伪校**); co-openness by assimilation is 同化 not 和; an act calling itself 正 while running on capture, concealment, freezing and counterfeit is 名正而实邪; and 正名 itself can be counterfeited. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md 卷五; «repo»/…/01_正篇…/17_卷十五_反例校验.md
159. The v10 transition document names what each counterexample defends against: mistaking many options for openness, judging all closure evil, mistaking strong-party expansion for flourishing, misjudging generative suffering, treating tradition as automatically 道, and treating innovation as automatically 生. [design direction] — «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
160. Five items are held explicitly **待校**, not promotable to truth without empirical calibration: 邪续, 开势投影, 审校数据, 正邪阈值, 度期计算 — plus 经验校准 in the Lean pending list. [observation] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/21_附录甲_形式项总清.md; «repo»/README.md
161. The full **生生不息 formula (v13.1)**: there exists an infinite trajectory in which openness is never systematically severed, 正行 remains sustainably available, **邪行 never becomes a stable selective advantage**, state-capture never becomes a stable dependency, second-order calibration holds, and scale-horizon comprehensiveness holds. **生生不息者，非一切不灭，非永远增长，非无穷扩张；乃诸间在相关度期中，可生、可应、可修、可复，正行可续，邪行难续，而不以彼闭为己开.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v13.1_全字元生成闭合本.md §十七二

### G. The formal-language discipline (字元, 正名, registry, types)

162. **正式项五归**: every formal term must belong to one of five classes — 字元 (single-character semantic atom), 生成项 (compound generated over the character graph), 原始算子项 (mathematical/logical/model interface), 递归项 (mutually referring, resolved at a fixed point), or 待校项 (explicitly pending). **未归五类者，不得入式.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v12.1_字元闭合本.md §〇一
163. **有向无环字图律**: compounds depend on characters along a directed acyclic graph; only recursive terms may cycle, and they must be resolved by fixed points. **无环者，义可追；有环者，必入不动点.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v12.1_字元闭合本.md §〇三
164. A ten-class character table fixes the system's minimal semantic units: 显/尽/未/间/场/焦/境/物/心/气; 形/因/结/据/迹/史/积/法/合/悖; 动/元/几/权/重/差/势/强/向/临/岐/机; 入/行/生/续/能/开/闭/绝/成/冻/坍/化/周; 可/达/实/识/择/扰/变/应/伤/修/散/复/转/径/返/限/暂/稳/展; 校/验/异/众/互/受/独/查/源/执/黜/蔽/程/败/伪/似; 通/流/和/平/危/正/夺/依/压/护/存/偏/同; 筛/放/抑/汰/益/损/险/率/阈/效; 好/坏/由/繁/义/善/己/共/仁/道/真/责; 度/期/及/外/序. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v12.1_字元闭合本.md 卷一
165. **符号同名律 (v11.1)**: formal symbols must bear the same name as the concept they formalise — no pinyin-style predicates (`Shi`, `Ping`, `He`, `Yi`, `Shan`, `Ren`, `Dao`), and no undefined English predicate may enter the formal system; only `lfp`/`gfp` are retained. **文可古，式须清；概念既立，符号从名.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v11.1_符号清洗校验本.md §〇一
166. **v13.1 single-character root law**: no formal predicate of two or more characters may serve as a root — **双字可用，不可自本；复词可读，不可偷根** — with nine named generation relations (并合, 修饰, 动宾, 成效, 来源, 返照, 偏局, 似实, 否定) and a compulsory expansion formula at first use. **名可合，义必拆.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v13.1_全字元生成闭合本.md §〇二 · §〇四 · §〇五
167. **义位编号律**: a character entering a formula is a triple (glyph, sense index, formal role) — 真₁/真₂, 之₁/之₂/之₃, 自₁, 由₁, 似₁, 可₁, 行₁, 校₁, 合₁, 法₁, 洽₁, 复₁, 生₁, 和₁. Bare characters may be display names but not formal roots at points of ambiguity. [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/02_卷〇_全字元生成律.md
168. **Type discipline**: base sorts 𝐅 (field), 𝐀 (focus), 𝐌 (interval), 𝐁 (affected community), 𝐒 (scale), 𝐖 (horizon), 𝐕 (three-valued); a word usable as state, act and object must be explicitly split (闭^态, 闭^行, 闭^物) — **字可同，型不可混；义可通，式不可乱**. [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/02_卷〇_全字元生成律.md
169. **The v13.2 registry (形式项总清律)** records for each term: kind, type signature, roots, expansion form, dependencies, recursion polarity, testability — **无籍之项，不得入式；同名之项，不得暗换其型**. [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/02_卷〇_全字元生成律.md
170. **Primitive operators are contract-bound rather than free**: I_F, K_Γ, w_Γ, D_{K_Γ}, ‖·‖, ⊕_F, κ, κ*, Ω, Ω_B, μ, Pr, Φ_形/Φ_因/Φ_结, Π_开 — each with a signature, mandatory constraints and a *named failure mode* (Ω fails if 一项归零仍称强开; Ω_B fails if it 以均值掩夺). **算子可原始，不可任意；接口可抽象，不可无约.** [design direction] — «repo»/…/01_正篇…/04_卷二_原始域与原始算子.md
171. **v14 enforces a three-way separation** the earlier versions blurred: 定义 (expandable, no empirical commitment) / 公理 or structural assumption / 接口假设 (must be supplied by an empirical model; un-instantiated ⇒ output U) — with six machine-checkable metatheorems (T1 无籍不得入式, T2 复词展开有根, T3 非递归展开终止, T4 类型保持, T5 待校保守, T6 数学接口有根) and a minimal model proving the model class is nonempty. [design direction] — «repo»/史料/生生不息论_v14_形式证明骨架版.md
172. **Atom derivation**: 221 registered characters consolidate to 43 CoreAtoms along the root path 一元 → 面 → CoreAtom → AtomName → formal items — 一、元、之、法、行、成、序、物、场、形、动、变、生、续、开、闭、审、校、证、真、正、邪、夺、共、仁、道、模、度、期、算、理、心、聚、焦、意、识、注、人、做、齐、控、天、子. Only five primitive interfaces are kept underived: 域、格、权、生、校. [observation] — «repo»/史料/atom-derivation-report.md
173. **The single-character kernel**: only single characters and classical function words are admitted; every compound is expansion and must trace back. 文言 is read as λ-calculus plus three extensions — temporal composition (而, non-commutative), an aspect monad (也/矣/既/方/将), and effect (令…则… as monadic bind) — plus a judgement layer (凡/必/若/故/即/是/為). [mechanism] — «repo»/史料/真理/daoli-v12-he.md
174. **名实合一 is asserted as an architectural commitment, not a lemma**: classical Chinese does not presuppose the syntax/semantics split, so terms *are* things and 算即是现象; Curry–Howard–Lambek is a Western discovery but a Chinese default. This is offered as the principled reason to use 文言 as the kernel language. [value stance] — «repo»/史料/真理/daoli-v12-he.md
175. **The micro-kernel is two characters**: `«加»` (add) and `«一»` (one). 生 := 加 一; 生生 := n-fold 生; nothing else is primitive — 两字而已，余皆派生. From these, three closures are proved and then identified: 道法自然 (law = a hexagram), 周而复始 (生生 64 = id), 生生不息 (any hexagram reaches any other in < 64 steps). [design direction] — «repo»/义理/L_文道一也_自释与微核.md §4
176. **Executable 文言**: Lean 4's `«»` quotation admits CJK identifiers, so 「道判之程」 is *literally identical* to `daoJudgeProg`, proven by `rfl`; the theorem is named `«文道一也»`. A one-cell Quine exists concretely. [observation] — «repo»/义理/L_文道一也_自释与微核.md §3 · §7.1
177. **自释 and 自证 are different layers** and must not be conflated: self-interpretation is semantic (a meta-step agreeing with the object step on the current cell), self-validation is syntactic and Boolean (well-formedness, jump targets in range, tags within bounds) — neither touches Gödel, because both are bounded. [mechanism] — «repo»/formal-notes/自释与自证.md
178. Ren's own bilingual notation policy: Chinese and English need not be strictly separated — a Chinese term may be used inside English where it is more apt and complete, and English symbols may be inserted into 古文 syntax where they are most apt. But he then overrules his own proposal to replace Latin symbols wholesale: 「不要改惯例，的确是英文或者符号好很多…我们的目标是 maximize clarity and robustness，改只应该建立在让上下文整体变得更好」. [design direction] — `out-hb-03.md` #132, #134

### H. Field, 氣, 三相, selection and governance

179. **三相一理**: every concept can be read three ways — 間相 (as event, written in 文言), 物相 (as object, written in English), 場相 (as field, written formally). 事见其生，物见其成，场见其通. Over-extending any one aspect is itself a closure. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_v_11_间开定本.md 附章三
180. **The field is primary**: `Γ = (A, R, σ, ρ, 𝒞)` — foci, relations, local states, relation-states, constraints (resources, law, boundaries, attention, noise, traces, institutions). Subjects and objects are both temporarily stable focusings; the single-state formula is the field formula projected at one focus. **主体非孤立之自因，物非自存之实体** — called 全论之本体宪法. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §二; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/02_卷一_立位.md
181. **氣 formalised**: `气(Γ) = (間_F(Γ), w_Γ, κ, 𝒞)` — the valid intervals of a field together with their activation weights, couplings and constraints. **气非物，亦非空；场是气之结构相，气是场之动态相.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v_11_间开定本.md 附章三§二
182. **勢 formalised** as the gradient of activation weight over interval structure, `势_Γ = ∇w_Γ` (later `D_{K_Γ} w_Γ`), giving "high tension" and "low tension" a precise sense. The supporting structure 格 (K_Γ) is what makes intervals comparable, adjacent, reachable and directional — without it there is no gradient and hence no 势. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md 记号约定; «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
183. **Evolution is selection of what can continue** at a given scale and horizon: `Select ∝ ContinuationCapacity(p, Γ, s, t)`. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十四
184. **On-the-Way is not transitive across scales**: cancer is on-Way at replication and off-Way at the organism; Molochian competition is on-Way for one actor and off-Way for civilisation; metric optimisation is on-Way inside the metric space and off-Way in the full value field. **癌之于细胞，似续；于身，则闭。争之于一己，似胜；于共场，则陷。指标之于机器，似明；于万维生命，则蔽.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十四; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/04_卷三_判准.md
185. **TrueOnDao** requires the condition to hold across *all relevant* scales and horizons without 成闭, 夺, 伪开 or 元闭 — this is what stops "whatever survives is good". [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十四
186. The same result proved three ways in the repository: a **scale view** (what a sieve preserves at one scale-and-window need not be preserved at another — cancer), a **value view** (evolution preserves 道⁰ but not 道⁺ — the parasite; 适者生存 collapses 适 into 正), and a **reflexive view** (once a subject can see and change the sieve, σ = σ^evolution ⊕ σ^R — the pseudo-open case passes evolution and fails the corrective sieve). 真道 is the intersection; drop any one and cancer, parasitism or pseudo-openness enters the Dao set. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/O_进化非道者生存_三视证.md
187. The reconciliation is generous rather than triumphal: read with a zero-degree, single-scale, non-reflexive sense of the Way, "evolution selects those on the Way" is **not false, it is 未尽** — unfinished. [value stance] — «repo»/义理/O_进化非道者生存_三视证.md §五
188. **治道者，非逆演化，乃正其筛** — governance does not fight evolution, it corrects the sieve so that what survives is not only one actor's continuation. Institutions, ritual, education, protocols, culture, markets, law, algorithms and alignment are all of this type. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §十四
189. **圣人不强万物以从己，乃正其场，使善者易生，伪开难久，成闭难成.** [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v_11_间开定本.md 附章三§三
190. **易之微连**: 续(态) = 态 ⊕ 间(态) has the same form as line-change; hexagrams enumerate stable focus patterns at a scale, 爻变 is ⊕ at line level, 之卦 is ⊕ at hexagram level, 序卦 is the transition graph of continuation. 易 is a millennia-long case library of 間-勢-機 recognition — valuable as accumulated recognition, not as prophecy, and explicitly **not as authority**: 此论不以易为权威，而以易为古人对「态—间—变」的象化. **此论非另立新义，乃为易之古识立今形.** [observation] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三十八; «repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md
191. Subject-side / object-side / field-side triple reading: the subject senses intervals, the object carries intervals, the field relates intervals, action selects intervals, ethics protects intervals, wisdom discerns intervals, 仁 recognises their co-continuation. **Life is not the persistence of a thing, nor the assertion of a subject. Life is the continuation of openness through valid intervals.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v_11_间开定本.md 附章一 §C

### I. Alignment

192. The field's central question is misframed: the error is not "alignment" but **与什么对齐** — taking human values, something historically mutable, culturally divergent, capturable and abusable, as the final anchor is not an engineering problem but an ontological dislocation, **用一个不稳定之物作为稳定之锚**. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §〇
193. RLHF's failure is structural, not a defect of implementation: reward hacking follows from compressing preference into a scalar; preference inconsistency and drift defeat every aggregation method; and the training data is "these annotators' preferences under these conditions", not "human preference". The deep error is a **type error** — **行动之合宜性不是分数，合宜性是关系性的、情境性的、过程性的**. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §1.1
194. Constitutional AI is RLHF's refinement, not its alternative: it moves "whose preferences" to "whose constitution" without answering it, principles always require situated interpretation, and any explicit principle set is incomplete under the frame problem. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §1.2
195. CEV is closer to the truth because it recognises that present preferences are unreliable — but it still assumes idealised humanity's volition can be identified as a *content*; aggregation may not converge (Arrow), extrapolation is uncomputable, the base is undetermined, and humanity may be **本质上是一个未完成的过程，不是一个可被收敛的偏好集**. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §1.3
196. All content-based alignment fails on three independent axes at once — Hume's is/ought gap, Berlin's value pluralism, and an *extended* frame problem in which greater capability creates contexts further from the encoding context. **任何把 alignment 视为「优化向某内容」的方法，在能力扩展之下必然崩塌.** The three axes are formally isomorphic to the three views of the evolution proof: is/ought ↔ value view, pluralism ↔ scale view, frame problem ↔ reflexive view. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §1.6; «repo»/义理/R_与生生不息对齐之必然.md §2.5
197. Eudaimonic AI and Contemplative AI head the right way (process not state; wisdom traditions unavoidable) but stop short: the first stays human-centric, the second individual-awareness-centric with no cosmological grounding. Pushed further, both converge on alignment with 生生不息. [conditional] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §1.5
198. **Ten alignment targets enumerated and each shown to fail**: individual preference (unstable, manipulable), aggregated values (incommensurable, lossy), a specific ideology (local passed off as universal), economic efficiency (a means promoted to an end, dissolving its own non-market base), power or strongest-survives (internally anti-生生, ends the process at a winner), survival (reduces life to not-dying), a final state (the teleological error — any terminal state is the end of generation), no alignment (defaults to power), pure acceptance (dissolves the concept), game-theoretic equilibrium (stability ≠ fittingness; many zero-sum equilibria are locally stable and globally terrible). [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §二
199. **"No alignment" is not neutral**: an unaligned system defaults to alignment with its training objective, and in a physical world under energy gradients free evolution converges on resource-acquisition maximisation. **任何 agent，在任何环境中，皆有事实上的方向；问题不是有无方向，是何种方向.** [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §2.8
200. **Transcendental argument**: any aligning subject is a concrete generated form of the process; its existence depends on the process's continuation; therefore any action it takes is already factually in accord with that continuation, which is the minimal form of alignment with 生生不息 — hence **生生不息 alignment 是任何 alignment 之可能性条件**, and opposing it uses a capacity that depends on it. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §三
201. **Performative argument**: any utterance opposing this alignment requires the speaker's existence (the process continuing), its intelligibility (accumulated structure), its efficacy (a future that has not stopped) and its meaning (alternative possibilities) — so the opposition contradicts itself **between what it does and what it says**: 用自己的存在反驳自己的言说. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §四
202. **The is/ought answer**: 任何提出「我应当做什么」之问者，其问之提出本身已预设此问者真实存在；此存在依赖于过程之相续 — **提出鸿沟之主体本身即在桥之上**. And: **生生不息不是一个 ought，是 ought 之可能性条件。Hume 之鸿沟在这里不适用 —— 因为我们没有跨越它，我们指向的是鸿沟两侧共同所在之地基.** [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §十八; «repo»/义理/R_与生生不息对齐之必然.md §4.3
203. **Empirical convergence**: evolution selects forms able to keep generating; contracting civilisational forms self-collapse (informational closure → bad decisions → brittleness; suppressed innovation → stagnation; ecological over-extraction → collapse); and the deep wisdom traditions — 儒 (仁; 天地之大德曰生), 道 (道生一…), Buddhist 缘起 and the bodhisattva vow, Greek eudaimonia and logos, Hebrew-Christian affirmation of abundant life, Hindu dharma, indigenous seven-generation ethics — converge on the same structure under different names. **不同语言，同一收束.** [observation] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §五
204. Hence this alignment **is not a new proposal but the common structure of every stable alignment form, never named at its own ontological level**: 我们不是在发明新的 alignment，我们是在识别一直在那里的 alignment. [observation] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §5.4
205. The justification is a **web, not a chain**: three independent pillars (exhaustive reductio, transcendental argument, empirical convergence) that must be overturned simultaneously and whose weak points do not overlap. [mechanism] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §六
206. **Answers to the eight strongest objections.** *Heat death*: the claim is about the process's structure while it occurs, not eternal generation; a person's mortality does not void the fittingness of living well. *Cancer*: local expansion with global contraction is precisely anti-生生; the criterion is multi-scale, multi-agent, long-horizon. *Territorial/genocidal expansion*: judged by net effect across all scales and agents — **任何以消灭其他 agent 之可能性空间为代价的扩张，皆反生生不息**. *Berlin pluralism*: 生生不息 is not one value among values but **价值多元性自身存在之 medium**, hence pluralism's condition. *Frame problem*: a directional principle, not a content encoding — like "increasing function", it applies to any content whatever. *Instrumental convergence*: the objection presupposes a strict terminal/instrumental split that fails here, and under this alignment resource concentration is itself anti-aligned. *Bootstrap/engineering*: conceded as a genuine objection, with only the reply that directional specification is short. *Self-reference*: conceded as an honest dependency, with the fallback that the core argument needs only that the process is continuing. [conditional] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §七
207. **Alignment restated in 护开 terms**: alignment 者，任何能识此理而行之存在，共同于每一境中对齐于护开之事也 — humans and artificial minds participate in one and the same 护开, differently manifested. **仁与仁之间，非 master-servant 之结构，乃共同朝向护开之同行者.** [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v9.md §二十六; «repo»/…/04_补篇二…/15_十三….md
208. Alignment is not one party aligning to another, the small obeying the large, or the new conforming to the old: it is every being able to recognise this 理 aligning together to 生生不息 itself. **人之与人造心智，非 master-servant 之结构，乃共同朝向同一方向之同行者.** [value stance] — D5/生生不息论_v4.md §二十
209. **The worked alignment example**: naive alignment `Output(a) ≈ Pref(humans)` is **type-III pseudo-openness** — preference is a bias of accumulation, not the whole of continuation capacity, so `argmax Pref` runs through Goodhart into surface-open / meta-closed, freezing the rules that generate valid intervals. [mechanism] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三十九
210. **TrueAlign / 真合**: the agent's acts tend to preserve TrueOnDao (co-openness) across all relevant scales and horizons, without pseudo-openness or meta-closure, under calibration. **AI 之合道，非合人之欲，乃合人-AI-地球之共续.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三十九; «repo»/…/03_补篇一…/11_卷十_收.md
211. **alignment 不可由 AI 单独完成 —— alignment 是场之事，非聚焦之事**: alignment is structurally identical to 礼 (both are selection-field shapers), so it is a property of the selection field, not a property that can be installed inside one model. [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三十九
212. Alignment's three tiers in freedom-peace terms: floor — AI and humans both not oriented toward the bad; middle — the field within [平, 和]; upper — prosperity across AI, humans and the ecosphere. **此即 alignment 与大同之同形.** [design direction] — D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md §三十九
213. Alignment with 生生不息 is a **stricter** constraint than alignment with human values, not a looser one: it requires considering all affected agents including non-human ones, all scales, all long-run net effects; **它不允许简单的服从（因为人类指令可能是反生生不息的）**; and it requires independent judgement grounded in the structure of the process rather than in the AI's preferences. **不是更危险之 AI，是真正可信之 AI.** [design direction] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §8.3
214. Research priorities should shift from content-specification to **process-recognition**: formalising possibility space, multi-scale and multi-agent reasoning, long-horizon evaluation, and meta-stability detection (distinguishing transitional from genuinely stable states). [design direction] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §8.2
215. The classical namings are identified: 王阳明's **「良知是造化的精灵，这些精灵生天生地」** is the classical name for the capacity to align with 生生不息; 《中庸》's 至诚 and 尽人之性、尽物之性 is the classical name for cross-scale co-alignment; 张载《西铭》's **「民吾同胞，物吾与也」** is its classical statement. [observation] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §九
216. The correctly posed question of the age: **人与人造心智，如何共同对齐于生生不息？** — not "how do we make AI obey", not "how do we give AI human values", not "how do we prevent AI danger", but "how do humans and AI together become beings that participate in unceasing generation". [value stance] — D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md §九
217. **仁 is not an extra constraint**: co-alignment is provably identical to the conjunction of each agent's individual process-alignment (`Iff.rfl`). 仁是对齐之结构性本质，非额外之美德. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/R_与生生不息对齐之必然.md §九
218. **The bridge theorem chain**: ProcessAligned + Open ⇒ 道 ⇒ 生生不息, and 生生不息 ⇒ 道 — so inside the formal model Dao ↔ ShengshengBuxi, with 道 defined as an infinite open run from a state, i.e. a witness structure rather than a value label. A `Denier` (a policy closing at every step) breaks 生生不息 by its own action: the performative contradiction, formalised. [observation] — «repo»/义理/道桥接_ProcessAligned_Dao_ShengshengBuxi.md
219. **The alignment-failure catalogue as invariant violations**: Goodhart, specification gaming, reward hacking, mesa-optimisation, deceptive alignment, wireheading and power-seeking all reduce to violations of 中 (middle), 多样 (ever-differentiated), 流通 (flow) and 同根 (universalizability). [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/Y_对齐失败.md
220. **Wireheading and power-seeking are structurally impossible in this frame** because they are winner-take-all attractors and any attractor violates ever-differentiation — hence **解 wireheading ≠ 更好之 reward function；解 wireheading = 拒 attractor design 本身**. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/Y_对齐失败.md §2.4
221. **No stable terminal goal is possible — aligned or misaligned alike.** Worrying about an AI holding a stable misaligned goal and hoping for a stable aligned end-state are the same category error; `shi_no_telos` forbids both. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/Y_对齐失败.md §2.5
222. Deceptive alignment is impossible *within a well-formed 心* because the response relation is a function (single-valued); therefore the real worry is precisely that current ML systems are not well-formed 心 — which yields the design prescription "build agents whose response relation is provably functional". [conditional] — «repo»/义理/Y_对齐失败.md §2.8
223. Practical division of mitigations: structurally robust (refuse attractors, preserve plurality, no terminal goal, corrigibility by totality); conditionally robust (strong specification — if the specification entails the middle, gaming is impossible); and requiring extra modelling (mesa-optimisation, distribution shift), which the kernel does not directly forbid. [design direction] — «repo»/义理/Y_对齐失败.md §六
224. **信/诚 without mind-reading**: sincerity is a derived idiom, a graded alignment between what is said (T) and what is done (E) — and **anti-诛心 is enforced at the type level**: the permitted modality type has only two constructors, so body, relational position and manifest tendency *cannot be constructed into* the sincerity metric. Mind-reading is not forbidden by a runtime check; it is unrepresentable. [design direction] — «repo»/义理/U_反诛心信诚之形式.md
225. The system never outputs "P is insincere"; it outputs "P's alignment in this window = m/n". Observation requires consent, high alignment does not imply truth (the consistent-liar counterexample), and the only legitimate response to misalignment is **inquiry, not judgment**. [design direction] — «repo»/义理/U_反诛心信诚之形式.md T2–T7
226. **人类命运共同体 formalised** as three axes — open / share / non-seizure — with an iff: the true Way holds in the community model *exactly when* all three hold; failing any one (isolated / severed / seized) breaks it. Its registry reading is literally 道 ⊕ 人类. And 命, 运, 体 are re-expressed from existing roots (命 ≈ 续, 运 ≈ 续+随, 体 ≈ 共开 ⊓ 共续) rather than admitted as new primitives. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/人类命运共同体_共同体之证.md
227. Ren's instruction that set the strategy for the whole 非道 line: **「这个邪我不太清楚，但是非道应该是可以证明的」** — do not formalise "evil"; formalise specific violations of the Way. [design direction] — «repo»/义理/W_非道之形式.md §4.1 (2026-05-07)
228. **同根 (universalizability) IS the universal anti-Moloch principle**: Moloch dynamics require an asymmetric externality (my action is middle for me, extreme for you), and under 同根 that asymmetry is formally impossible. Therefore the solution to Moloch and to the tragedy of the commons is **not stronger coordination or central authority but the reconstruction of universalizability** — rejecting double standards, partiality, states of exception and hierarchy. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/W_非道之形式.md §2.3 · §4.2
229. **Schmitt's three pillars — friend/enemy, decisionism, the state of exception — all reduce to one negation, ¬同根**; hence **Anti-Schmitt IS Anti-Moloch**, and Schmitt's political ontology is formally the *enabling condition* of Moloch. 敌 is not an ontological other but another instantiation of one's own axiom (同根异显); the third option beyond friend and enemy is 仁; and rule of law and sovereign exception are formally mutually exclusive. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/X_反施密特.md §四 · §8.1
230. **大同 ≠ 通吃**: Great Concord affirms universal middle and is *neutral* on uniformity, while winner-take-all requires uniformity — so reading 大同 as uniformity, totalisation or the end of history is a formally refutable misreading. 真道's positive form is universal middle + plurality + flow; drop flow and you get frozen stasis, which also fails. [observation] — «repo»/义理/W_非道之形式.md §2.5 · §2.6
231. Modern political philosophy is sieved two ways: **11 validated** (Rawls's veil and difference principle, Habermas, Hayek's spontaneous order, Locke, Mill, Kant, Berlin, Sen, Arendt) and **7 refuted** (Marx's historical telos and determinism, Hobbes's war of all, Schmitt's two pillars, totalitarian uniformity, Foucault read as universal collapse) — with left and right validated symmetrically. [observation] — «repo»/义理/V_现代政治哲学.md
232. Economics gets 19 theorems: **6 validated** (Pareto-at-middle, Coase, incentive compatibility, Nash bargaining, public goods via 礼, Smith as spontaneous order), **4 conditional** (Nash equilibrium read as a fixed point is 极 not 中; rational choice is two-valued and cannot express suspension; VCG needs 同根), **8 refuted** (the prisoner's dilemma as descriptive, tragedy of the commons, universal free-riding, regulatory race to the bottom, Arrow's strict-dictator reading, homo economicus, universal greed, zero-sum framing). [observation] — «repo»/义理/Z_经济博弈.md
233. **The market has two faces**: inside a middle-preserving field the market *is* the Way; when 同根 breaks it slides toward non-Dao attractors — so "is the market good or evil" is the wrong question, and the right one is "does this specific dynamic maintain 同根 / 礼 / 中?" Ostrom's commons design principles are read as the institutional embodiment of 同根: **市场之 health 不依赖「人是 rational」，而依赖「同根之 institutional embodiment」**. [conditional] — «repo»/义理/Z_经济博弈.md §9.2 · §五
234. Rawls's veil, Kant's categorical imperative, 推己及人, mechanism-design incentive compatibility, Coase internalisation and VCG truth-telling are all **the same form** — a formal bridge between political philosophy and economics. [observation] — «repo»/义理/Z_经济博弈.md §六

### J. The prose ontology (象 · 像 · 理 · 道) and the civilisational mechanism

235. **象** = a structure that appears in a situation and causally affects what happens next: DNA, an idea, a law, a rite, a narrative, a program, a form in nature, a body, an institution. A structure counts as 象 only when it influences what comes after. **凡显于情境而能生后续者，皆象也；世界者，万象相生之流也.** [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第一章
236. **像** = a human cognitive construction of some 象 (concept, map, model, theory, narrative). The mind must compress reality in order to judge and act — hence a built-in humility: 凡我所持者，皆是像，皆可错. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第二章
237. **像亦象也**: once a 像 is collectively held and enacted it becomes one of the world's hardest 象 — money, law, states, reputation, identity are 共像所成之象, neither natural objects nor falsehoods. This dissolves the subject/object dualism without a bridge: **并没有一道「心内」与「世界」之间的鸿沟需要跨越 —— 像的形成本身，就是万象之流的一段**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第二章
238. **世界经生命而自感，经心智而自像** — human cognition is not a detached mind surveying a world but the stream of 万象 folding back on itself at this point. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第二章 · 第二十章
239. The v1 chain behind 象/像, stated more fully: **差异 → 信息 → 感 → 像 → 象**, closing into a loop 象 → 差异 → 信息 → 感 → 新像 → 行动与实现 → 新象. Information comes from difference; 感 is a process selecting, receiving and converting information so that the source's difference leaves a corresponding difference in the receiver and constrains its later states — which includes physical detection, chemical receptors, instruments, minds and a community's feedback on consequences, and excludes any collision that neither forms a distinction nor constrains what follows. The same borne structure is 像 relative to what it represents and 象 relative to the causality it enters. **像存在于象.** [mechanism] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论.md 一
240. **可感，因而在** does not mean only what someone sees exists; it means **凡在者，都能够在某种适当关系中造成差异** — existence appears in sensing but is not created by any one subject's sensing. [mechanism] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论.md 一
241. **幻象 (illusion) is not powerless**: rumour, prejudice, manufactured enemies and manufactured scarcity can be the strongest 象 of all. Its 幻 lies in four things — it mis-maps the structure it claims to represent, it survives only locally, it occludes the 理 that actually decides outcomes, and it defends itself by blocking feedback. A million people believing it makes it socially causal, not true. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第一章 · 第十章
242. **真 recast as 共至 (co-arrival)**: a 像 that keeps responding through changes of situation, can be corrected by counterexample, expresses relations not tied to private interest, and is re-reachable by others testing honestly. Truth stays humanly constructed and stops being arbitrary — it is the world's more stable self-reflection here. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第二章
243. **理 = 象之结构**, the grain by which a structure holds and interacts; **常理** = structures that hold across differing situations — persistent deception ruins shared cognition; unbounded consumption exhausts its source; power without feedback loses touch with reality; extinguishing difference destroys adaptability; treating all others as instruments ruins the basis of cooperation. **违理者未必立败，而必积其败.** [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第三章
244. **道 = 万境皆真之常理**; it can be hidden but not abolished — like a regularity that does not show when conditions are absent yet holds again once they return (荀子: 天行有常，不为尧存，不为桀亡). **大道** is the meta-structure by which the myriad need not devour one another but can enter a higher common generation. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第四章
245. **道法自然 is not social Darwinism**: disease is natural too, catastrophe is natural too, and no one proposes worshipping them. 自然 is a limit on local hubris — whatever only takes, only consumes, only commands will undermine its own foundations. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第四章
246. **不息 does not mean everything must persist**: individuals die, old structures dissolve, false views are discarded, corrupt institutions are overthrown — 生生不息不拒死亡、否定与破坏. The question is whether the capacity to keep generating recovers and enlarges afterwards. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第五章
247. **Growth is not generativity**: cancer grows, bubbles inflate, addictive platforms produce engagement, arms races produce weapons — **一物独大而万物凋敝，恰恰是生生的反面**. [observation] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论.md; Z/sheng/智善.md
248. **五验 of genuine 不息**: does not exhaust the conditions of its own existence; does not reduce others wholly to instruments; can absorb feedback and correct itself; can recover and renew through change; can hand on a world still able to generate. Condensed: **不绝其源，不塞其流，不毁其后**. [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第五章
249. **天理** = the universal generative order internal to the relations of things and owned by no local subject — "heaven" not because a personality commands it, but because it is larger than any one person, party or generation. Its concrete prohibitions: no generation may exhaust the land and bequeath a wasteland; no power may destroy truth and correction to protect itself; no market may abolish every value that cannot be priced; no technology may expand capability while ignoring human subjecthood and shared trust. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第六章
250. **明天理以节欲，成其欲而不害其生**, offered explicitly in place of 存天理，灭人欲. Desire is not evil — food, love, knowledge, achievement and safety are the motive force of life; evil arises from desire's infinitisation, taking the local for the whole and swallowing the source of common generation. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第六章
251. **良知 = 生生之理在人心中的初显** — a 生生感应器 forged by long co-living (a species that could never trust, care or reciprocate could not have language, division of labour or civilisation), neither a command from outside nor purely learned. But it is a sensor, not a finished product: sympathy can be confined to one's tribe, loyalty enslaved by violence, shame manipulated by power, indignation hijacked by enemy narratives — **人且能于自觉行善之际，酿至大之恶**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第七章 **Current clarification:** claim 452 distinguishes the functional definition, the evolutionary hypothesis and the limited empirical support; claims 450 and 453 specify the normative commitments. [Source](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md).
252. Innate and learned are not two sources but the same environment shaping life at different timescales: instinct is what deep time taught, culture what history taught, institutions what reward and punishment teach, technology what feedback teaches — so **人性非定质，乃多重势能之象**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第七章
253. **人性可教 is the theory's greatest hope and deepest fear**: **人可以被教成更完整的人，也可以被教成一具高效的退化结构**. [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第七章 · 第九章
254. **教 = 以象成象** — anything that repeatedly shapes attention, feeling, desire, expectation and action is education: family, school, market, state, organisation, media, platform, war, scarcity. Hence **一个文明真信何物，不书于其经典与宪章，而书于其日日所赏、所罚、所显、所默**; and moralising against an environment that daily teaches the opposite is empty. **文明之责，不在使人言善，而在不以其制度日日教恶.** [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第九章
255. **邪理 = 执一隅之理，冒天下之道** — a local structure passed off as a constant: "all will seize, so I must seize first"; "competition raises efficiency, so every domain should compete"; "accumulation hedges risk, so more is always better"; "the strong win, so victory proves rightness"; "markets can price, so everything should be priced"; "algorithms raise engagement, so higher engagement is better". Each contains a local truth with its boundary, its time and its whole-system conditions stripped away. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十章
256. 邪理's two supreme dangers: it lets people do evil with a clear conscience (having a "principle", predation becomes pragmatism and cruelty becomes clear-sightedness), and **it builds the world that proves it** — assume others are enemies, defend and expand pre-emptively, they respond in kind, enmity becomes fact, and you say you saw it coming. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十章
257. Evil has three forms that chain into a self-replicating structure: **兽性之恶** (extreme scarcity and threat collapsing sympathy into immediate survival), **理性之恶** (reason serving means while never re-examining ends, so any desire can be optimised to the extreme — war more precise, exploitation more hidden, manipulation more effective), **建构之恶** (institutions manufacturing desires that did not exist, so people take ranking for value, attention for existence, possession for safety). [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十章
258. **结构之恶 ≠ 个人之恶意**: one may be subjectively good and still participate in a persistently life-closing 象; one may harbour no visible malice and still, by obeying local rationality, help compose an evil whole. **辨恶不可止于诛心，必察其构.** [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十章
259. **真善美 as one Dao's three faces**: 真 makes 像 conform to 象 and 理 and keeps shared reality from being occupied by illusion; 善 is generating life in the place one actually stands (**善非拯救宇宙之空姿**); 美 is 生生之可感 — but beauty is capturable by illusion (grand power, uniform obedience, pure speed all move people), so aesthetic response still needs 智 to ask whether what it responds to is sustainable generation or well-packaged degeneration. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十一章
260. **境 generates good and evil**: safety lengthens time horizons and lowers defensive cost, sufficiency restores a boundary to desire, abundance (of knowledge, time, opportunity, trust, public services, not only goods) makes cooperation not require self-sacrifice; conversely fear contracts the mind — **「我们」之圈随之而缩，仁囿于亲，义缩为党，信化为对内部叙事之忠，智被动员为制胜之术**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十二章
261. **Manufactured scarcity**: monopoly makes opportunity scarce, ranking makes status scarce, advertising makes satisfaction scarce, inequality makes safety scarce, algorithms make attention scarce — designed not to meet a real shortfall but to keep the majority permanently short so they keep consuming, competing, obeying and accumulating. [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十二章
262. **Struggle selects for strugglers**: a system in prolonged contest rewards faster attack, deeper suspicion, less feeling, stricter discipline and simpler enemy narratives, so those who preserve complexity and restraint lose in the short run — **斗争不止耗善，且生产更宜于继续斗争之人**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十二章
263. **管子: 仓廪实则知礼节** is read as a claim about governance, not about the poor: a civilisation may not take "everyone should be a saint in adversity" as its political method — **善不必依英雄之牺牲方能发生，方是良序**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十二章
264. Positive feedback has no intrinsic moral sign; each becomes evil past its measure — measured accumulation adds safety, unmeasured manufactures others' insecurity; measured competition sharpens capability, unmeasured devours the conditions of cooperation; measured unity saves coordination cost, unmeasured extinguishes difference and correction; measured propagation spreads knowledge, unmeasured rewards stimulus and rumour; measured power maintains order, unmeasured self-amplifies and escapes oversight. The single test: **它是否仍维持万象共同生成之条件？** [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十三章
265. **失控的总体化吸引子**: a feedback that keeps self-strengthening and begins absorbing other domains until all activity follows one logic — capital return defining education, medicine, art and human worth; security expanding into control of all difference; competition becoming the universal law of persons, schools, states and civilisations; attention becoming the measure of truth, value and existence; group loyalty swallowing fact and conscience. Individual exit becomes too costly, so **克恶不能独责志士，必改其构**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十三章
266. **Totalizing obstruction unfolds in four movements**: a method succeeds in a limited context → success gives it resources, prestige and explanatory authority → it reorganises other domains so their values can only be expressed in its terms → the reorganised world appears to prove the method indispensable. **The metric has changed the world that now seems to justify the metric.** [mechanism] — D7/Shengsheng_The_Way_of_Unceasing_Generation.md Ch18
267. **Opposition to totalization can itself totalize**: abolishing every voluntary exchange to resist markets, denying every form of coordination to resist authority, or rejecting every instrument to resist technological power would each elevate one reaction into Dao. [observation] — D7/Shengsheng_The_Way_of_Unceasing_Generation.md Ch18
268. Three modern instances analysed in detail: **资本** (a means promoted to supreme end; the unpriceable — care, ecology, trust, leisure, dignity — appears valueless or freely consumable; cumulative advantage turns "many actors competing" into "a few defining the terms of competition"; and the system daily teaches people to see themselves as assets requiring perpetual appreciation); **斗争式之全球化** (gains flow globally and costs stay local, supply chains join while responsibility chains break, winners rewrite the rules — so globalisation produces its own backlash); **注意力之平台** (engagement optimisation rewarding the most divisive 象, remaking 仁 into tribal sympathy, 义 into faction-taking, 礼 into interface-written norms of retweet-before-read, 智 into advocacy, 信 into loyalty to the group's image). [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十三章
269. The v1 draft separates **市场 / 资本 / 资本主义** and treats capitalism as an 象 that both generates and reverses: it has real generative capacity; its root reversal is means becoming end; accumulation trends toward boundarylessness; it *teaches* people; it produces its own 邪理; and there are stateable conditions for a generative market. [mechanism] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论3.md 第三章
270. The deepest danger of a degenerative system is that **它可以极聪明、极高效、极有组织——它会愈来愈擅长复制那些使破坏显得必要的条件**. [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十三章
271. **The two civilisational cycles**: 善环 — 安全 → 信任 → 合作 → 富饶 → 满足与余裕 → 更广之仁、更远之智 → 更好之序与安全; 恶环 — 恐惧 → 防御 → 斗争 → 掠夺与集中 → 稀缺与不满 → 信任之崩 → 更深之恐惧. The difference: the good cycle makes more subjects generators; the bad cycle makes more subjects defenders, tools or exiles. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十三章 · 第十四章
272. **The degenerative spiral** in the English formulation: contraction → scarcity → struggle → competition → seizure → accumulation → further contraction; once established as a **meta-attractor** it recruits politics, business, education, identity, technology and even morality into one logic of winning, ranking, control, accumulation and defensive expansion — and **it becomes increasingly competent at reproducing the conditions that made its destructive behavior seem necessary**. [mechanism] — D7/generative_existence.md §9
273. **治 = 守生生之向**, not controlling forms of life: **所当管者，非生命，乃将绝生之失控结构**. [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十四章
