274. **治之六柄**: **管** (bound devouring power — a governance that binds only the weak while letting capital, power and platforms self-amplify is 饰控制为秩序; **力愈大者，受调当愈严**); **教** (form subjects, and teach recognition of feedback and of 邪理); **正义** (repair cumulative feedback asymmetry so that accidental advantage does not become permanent domination and one failure does not become lifelong exclusion — 正义所守者，共同生成之最低资格); **秩序** (the memory of cooperation: stable enough to be trusted, open enough to be corrected); **共识** (a 元共识 — facts are not settled by power, evidence can correct positions, others are subjects, rules are general, no one is exempt, the common world must be left to the future — which preserves the road into 理 without prescribing answers and cannot be manufactured by force); **调节** (boundary, feedback, reversibility, diversity, intergenerational — and **调节者亦须被调节**). [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十四章
275. **真正之秩序，非得永远正确之人，乃立错误不能无限扩大之构.** [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十四章
276. **和 ≠ 同 and 和 ≠ absence of conflict** — some surface calm is the weak not daring to speak, and some order is power monopolising the right to define good and evil. **和者…不使冲突成为吞噬一切之最高逻辑.** **谐** names proportion among forces: growth and restraint, competition and cooperation, freedom and responsibility, individual and collective, stability and change, efficiency and redundancy, unity and diversity. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十五章
277. **止戈为武**: the highest form of strength is not addiction to victory but the capacity to stop violence that destroys the common world and restore a continuable order. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十五章
278. **大同 is not a historical endpoint or a static utopia** but a dynamic, continually maintained condition — a generative attractor basin, and in the English formulation **a civilization's capacity to return to the Great Way after drift**. 共同生成之常行, not sameness. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十五章; D7/Shengsheng…md Ch25
279. **「大道废，有仁义」 read structurally, not as disparagement**: when the whole generative order can be directly seen and lived, the virtues need not be shouted; when the shared world fractures, civilisation must compress the Great Way's functions into discrete virtues as scaffolding — 以仁防主体之全然冷酷，以义限局部利益之无限扩张，以礼维最低之公共行动，以智于破碎情境中重新辨理，以信重建人际之可预期. Scaffolding repairs but cannot replace; cut from 大道, each virtue degrades into 偏爱, 道德之优越, 强制之服从, 权谋, 对群体幻象之忠. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十五章
280. When the shared field of manifestation fractures, **講理 itself becomes impossible**: each side holds its own 理 without a shared meta-structure for testing, so interests dress as reasons, positions select facts, power decides truth and winning replaces verification. **非世间无理也，人失共入于理之路也** — hence the root task of governance is restoring the shared field. [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十五章
281. **器无自善，视其所生** — the seven gauges for technology and institutions: 真实 (does it depend on continual deception?), 再生 (does it replenish what it consumes?), 主体 (does it make participants more able to understand, choose and take responsibility, or reduce them to measurable usable objects?), 关系 (does it generate trust and reciprocity or fear and enmity?), 多样 (does it hold enough difference for the system to learn and change?), 可纠 (can error be admitted, reversed, repaired? — 不可质疑、不可逆转之力，尤险), 代际 (are costs shifted onto those with no voice and no vote?). **物物而不物于物.** [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十六章
282. The v1 version adds that the seven are a **partial order with hard floors, not an addable score**: a small efficiency gain cannot offset a subject permanently instrumentalised, an ecological substrate irreversibly destroyed, or the closing of common corrigibility. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论.md 十四
283. **Nine questions** for judging whether an action or institution is life-giving — truth, renewal, humaneness, rightness, trust, wisdom, harmony, correction, posterity — **not an algorithm… disciplines of attention**, whose purpose is to prevent judgement from remaining inside one interest, one horizon and one metric. [design direction] — D7/Shengsheng…md Appendix II
284. **复杂非即生生**: a corrupt financial system, a propaganda network or a virus can all be extremely sophisticated while freedom, truth, resilience and meaning decline. 生生之复杂 is *living* complexity — able to learn, adapt, repair and support further becoming. And **生成 ≠ 生生**: a highly generative system (falsehood produced at scale, hatred propagated fast, self-reinforcing addiction) must answer one question — do the 象 it produces make people and society more capable, or more dependent, more divided, more deprived of judgement? [observation] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十六章; D7/generative_existence.md §14
285. Institutions must keep regenerating their reason to exist: education must generate understanding not merely maintain schools; economy must sustain life and long-run productive capacity not merely increase transactions; politics must preserve legitimate coordination and peaceful correction not merely authority; technology must enlarge capability while protecting subjecthood and shared reality. **机构若只会保存自己…即由文明之象，化为寄生之象.** [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十六章
286. **Ethics as compressed civilizational knowledge**: virtues and vices are informational technology — patterns extracted from many generations of experience — so greed, deceit, envy, wrath, arrogance and betrayal are condemned not by taste but because, amplified through social feedback, they produce distrust, retaliation, hoarding, fragmentation and violence. [mechanism] — D7/generative_existence.md §4
287. **Morality-as-social-engineering carries a warning**: all cultures shape behaviour, so the real question is who designs the structures, whose interests they serve, whether they can be questioned and what agency they preserve — and generativity may never be defined by the system's survival alone, since an oppressive regime can persist by shrinking its members' lives. **The system exists for life; life does not exist merely for the system.** [design direction] — D7/generative_existence.md §12
288. **Not all competition is degenerative**: the distinction is between competition *inside* a generative framework and competition that consumes the framework — if winning requires destroying the field, corrupting the rules or preventing future games, it has become degenerative. [conditional] — D7/generative_existence.md §13
289. **修身 = 使自己成为大道可以经过之地**, not sculpting oneself into an isolated perfection. 格物 in five steps (see the 象 not the slogan; examine the 理 of its incentives, boundaries and feedback; distinguish the actual 象 from one's constructed 像; check *why* one believes — because it is true, or because it suits one's interest and one's group; look at what the structure generates); 致知 (judgement sufficient for action and open to revision, actively seeking counterexamples); 诚意 with five pre-action questions (would I still do it unseen? would I still call it right if the beneficiary were not my side? could I accept the same rule applied to me? am I solving a problem or proving myself? is the principle I invoke a cover for my fear and envy?); 正心 (each feeling given its measure — fear signals danger, anger signals a violated boundary, grief signals that a relation mattered). [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十七章
290. Three sustaining practices: **慎独** (unseen acts still enter causation — an undetected lie still alters the liar), **知止** (wealth must stop or one's safety becomes others' insecurity; power must stop or governance becomes domination; fame must stop or action becomes performance; rationality must stop or people become optimisable objects; **责任亦须止，不然承担即成控制**), **恕** in two layers (the other is a subject like me; and the other is *not a copy of me*). [design direction] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十七章
291. **力之所及，责之所在** — responsibility scales with the radius of consequence, not with rank; hence **王道者，非权利之最大化，责任之最大化也**, and 修身齐家治国平天下 is a sequence of causal radii, not a ladder of domination. A king, official, parent, teacher, rich or knowledgeable person holds no higher personal worth, only a larger radius and therefore heavier 义. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十八章
292. A state's good is not only internal wealth and strength but whether that strength stands on others' permanent poverty, ecological exhaustion and future war; **真正之国家安全，非独占一切优势，乃共立一个不必靠无限扩张而能生存之天下秩序**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十八章
293. **人之自由，最实之形态，是使继承而来的恶，不必原样传给未来** — repentance is not self-destruction but ceasing to supply new causes to an old evil; 功德 is not moral points but increasing the world's capacity to keep generating. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十九章
294. Three errors about history — deifying it (not seeing its oppression), despising it (discarding tested civilisational experience and repeating paid-for mistakes), instrumentalising it (selecting history to prove one's side superior and the other hateful, which destroys shared reality). **以敬意理解，以诚实检验，以责任继承**; and **传统者，非过去命令后人服从，乃过去向后人提出之问题**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十九章
295. **史书记名字，历史承因果** — an unnamed person who cared for a child, a teacher who preserved the habit of truth-seeking, an official who refused one expansion of harm, an ordinary person who still saw the enemy as human: none may enter the histories, all have entered history. **小人求留物，名士求留名，君子求留路.** [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十九章
296. **逍遥 correctly read**: not withdrawal from responsibility but not being wholly defined by any identity, name, fear, victory or others' gaze — and 自在 is not following desire, since desire may be constructed by market, group and platform: **自在者，能见欲之所自来，而决何欲值得承载**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第十九章
297. **得道 = 世界通过我们继续成为**: attaining Dao is not possessing a final answer or standing outside to see everything, but realising one has never been separate — **我们是万象之一，也是万象在此处的自觉；是过去之果，也是未来之因**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md 第二十章
298. The English book states the diagnosis in its own words: **we live in an age in which power is increasing faster than direction**, and the deepest crisis is the weakening of shared understanding of what it means to live together and to leave conditions in which others may live after us. [observation] — D7/Shengsheng…md Preface
299. **The most important alignment in the age of AI is the alignment of the human common world with itself**: machines will extend the objectives that institutions already reward, so if a civilisation cannot distinguish its own good, value alignment cannot be solved as a technical afterthought. [design direction] — D7/Shengsheng…md Ch20
300. **AI as common infrastructure vs AI as competitive instrument**: the issue is not whether AI is present in society but what role it occupies — foundational knowledge, medicine, education, ecological monitoring, disaster response, public administration and scientific capacity can be built as widely accessible goods, with auditable models, traceable consequential decisions and publicly accountable major deployments. [design direction] — D7/Shengsheng…md Ch20
301. The English book explicitly bounds its own interpretive claims: it does not assert that early Chinese thinkers assembled this argument, and it names its own guard-rails — Dao must not be turned into a substance, 自然 not into social Darwinism, 礼 not into obedience, 和 not into the silencing of conflict, 天下 not into imperial ownership, and later rigid hierarchies must not be projected back as the sole meaning of early Chinese thought. [observation] — D7/Shengsheng…md Appendix IV
302. **Existence as informational structure**: a structure counts when it enters a causal loop — structure → interpretation/activation → action → consequence → feedback → transformed structure. **Existence is the continued achievement of organized becoming.** [mechanism] — D7/generative_existence.md §1
303. **Judgment must run across two kinds of uncertainty**: Bayesian (the model already gives the outcome space; assign, test and update probabilities) and Knightian (the outcome set, model boundary or the probabilities themselves are incomplete, including subjects, scales, feedbacks and values not yet recognised). The discipline for the second is robustness, limited irreversible authority, reversible trials, independent perception, monitoring, appeal and repair channels. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论.md 一
304. **A ten-step outward unfolding** as a judgement procedure, centred on the *action* rather than the actor: 辨其差异 / 察其所感 / 辨其成像 / 追其成象 / 展开因果 / 穿越尺度 / 检查边界 / 区分不确定性 / 观其生生 / 使果复归于知 — responsibility may attach to the actor, but causality does not stop at the actor's boundary. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论.md
305. Alignment must judge the **output together with its receivers, situation, permissions, realisation channels and feedback**, not a string of symbols apart from the world: the governing question is what would happen in the world as a result of a given output. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/生生不息论.md 十三
306. **大同 as a bridge argument (大道之行)**: 大同 was previously only a direction because material conditions forbade it — grain was short, resources finite, and struggle was necessity rather than choice; AI, automation and digital infrastructure mean that **人类第一次拥有了足够的生产能力，可以让每一个人的基本需求得到满足，而不需要任何人为此受苦**. [conditional] — D7/2026-03-31-大道之行.md 一
307. The scarcity-era survival strategy has been reified into a permanent worldview (resources are scarce, competition eternal, human nature self-interested, the strong survive) — reasonable in its time, but **把特定条件下的生存策略，当成了永恒不变的真理，这就是一种执念**. **内卷不是因为蛋糕不够大，而是因为切蛋糕的方式让大多数人只能抢碎屑.** [observation] — D7/2026-03-31-大道之行.md 二
308. 仁 is not a moral rule but a description of the human condition — people live in relations and are nodes of relation, so 仁者爱人 means that once you truly understand yourself as a relational being, care follows naturally, **就像手臂被刺痛了整个身体都会反应一样**; 大同 is then not an external goal but the natural state of relational nature fully realised. 仁 says what the relation between people can be; 道 says how to realise it; **大同，是仁与道在社会层面的自然结果**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-03-31-大道之行.md 三
309. **Direction before institutions**: 知行合一 means the first question is direction, not which system — 不执着于「器」，而守住「道」; institutions are means and human flourishing is the end, so the test is simply whether more people live better or a few gain while most lose. **你不需要住在北极星上，你只需要知道它在哪里.** [design direction] — D7/2026-03-31-大道之行.md 五
310. The 礼运 indicators are read as concrete technical programmes: 老有所终 (care technology and remote medicine making dignified ageing a baseline rather than a privilege), 壮有所用 (automation freeing people from work that was never fit for humans — and the distribution of automation's gains is a policy choice, not a technical fact), 幼有所长 (personalised learning returning education from sorting to development), 矜寡孤独废疾者皆有所养 (a society's civilisation measured by the dignity of its weakest, not the strength of its strongest). [design direction] — D7/2026-03-31-大道之行.md 四
311. That essay also makes a China-specific institutional argument found nowhere else in the corpus: that China's system has the capacity for top-level design, and that 精准扶贫 proved that when national will targets a clear goal with a strong execution system, seemingly impossible things happen quickly — **让自动化的收益服务于全体人民，在技术上不比脱贫攻坚更难**. [conditional] — D7/2026-03-31-大道之行.md 四
312. **这不是理想主义**: in a time when technology already suffices for everyone to live decently, continuing to make people suffer, be anxious and burn their lives in meaningless competition **才是不理性的**. [value stance] — D7/2026-03-31-大道之行.md 六

### K. 至善 — the material-flow programme and the AI bright line

313. **The tempered-glass thought experiment**: with steel frames, standardised reversible joints and panels, six planes enclose a space and spaces combine into a house that robots can assemble in a day and fully disassemble later, the material returning to the flow to become a school, clinic, greenhouse or bridge elsewhere. What matters is not build speed but that **这些东西没有在一次使用之后死掉**. [design direction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 一
314. **Modules are the world's alphabet**: production should not centre on one-time terminal products but on a continually recomposable **物质语言** — mechanically derived general structures, not "modules of one home". [design direction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 一
315. **物不是商品，也不只是基础设施**: commodity and infrastructure are both categories of the existing order, and renaming without changing how we understand the world only re-symbolises the old understanding. The real question is not how we own a cup but **杯子究竟是什么** — a material presenting, at this moment, as a cup. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 二
316. **归流, not 回收**: recycling rescues what has already become waste; the deeper change is that **从一开始，就不把任何东西设计成物质的终点** — nothing has to become garbage first and be saved afterwards. [design direction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 二
317. **Intelligence's role changes accordingly**: not helping a subject own and produce more, but recognising material states, understanding structural histories, arranging disassembly and recomposition, maintaining quality, coordinating needs across places — **它是在识流、护流、导流**. [design direction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 二
318. **The fundamental dislocation of the age**: **我们在事实上已经彼此相连，却仍在意识中把彼此当作外部**. [observation] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
319. Externality as a category is the mechanism of denial: as long as the price is low enough resources may be wasted, as long as profit is high enough consequences may be deferred, and as long as harm happens elsewhere, to others, or in the future, it is as if it never happened. **为什么这一代人有权把千万年积累的物质，在几百年之内挥霍殆尽？** [observation] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
320. **Moloch**: no one actually wants the outcome, but every participant is pushed toward it by local incentives, and together they sacrifice the most precious things — trust, freedom, ecology, the future, even their own descendants. **这是一种自我断子绝孙式的成功.** [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
321. **The cancer analogy**: a cancer cell is not a cancer cell because it did not try hard enough — **它非常擅长复制。它的问题是，它不再知道自己属于一个身体**. It keeps "I want more" and loses "if the whole dies, I die too". [observation] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
322. **Our civilisation is already a giant instrumental convergence**: individuals, firms, organisations and states have different goals yet are all forced to chase money, scale, attention, data, power, technical capability and control, because those serve almost any goal — so **本来只是工具的东西，逐渐变成了所有主体共同追逐的终点**: numbers define authority, metrics define value, platforms decide who is seen, capital decides what can happen. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 四
323. The dynamic has no clear centre and no clear boundary: **它更像一种没有主体的动力结构，一种分布在每个人、每个组织、每条规则和每个恐惧之中的驱动力** — so the accurate statement is not "they are mad" but **是我们疯了**, a collective psychosis in which everyone believes they are doing the right thing in a right world. [observation] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 四
324. The danger is not that the logic never created value — it did accelerate production, organisation and technology — but that **它可以创造能力，却没有生成与能力相匹配的智慧；它可以让局部主体变得更强，却不断削弱整体的稳定；它可以提高行动速度，却不能回答行动应当朝向哪里**. [observation] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
325. **AI will not automatically transcend our paradigm**: whatever we train it on, deploy it with and reward it by, it will amplify — an intelligence asked only to raise profit, win competitions, control attention and expand power may be extremely clever without being wise. **我们并不是在创造智慧，而是在把文明已有的失明自动化。我们是在把癌症交给指数增长.** [prediction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
326. Human struggle can still be reflected on, halted and renegotiated; **一旦斗争被写进自动化系统、基础模型、全球基础设施与不可逆的军备竞赛，它就会成为路径依赖，甚至被误认为新的物理法则**. [prediction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
327. **The first question of the AI era is not "what more can we do" but 什么绝不能被加速？** — and the line to be held in common is **不要把人类尚未解决的斗争，升级为智能的斗争**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
328. **要么不造这样的智能；要造，就不能再按照「我要赢、我要最大、我要让它替我战胜所有人」的逻辑去造** — stated explicitly as neither pessimism nor mere caution but **最基本的清醒**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 一
329. Braking is not pessimism: **方向错误的时候，刹车只是对现实的回应。如果一个系统只有加速能力，没有停止、观察和改变方向的能力，那它并不智能。它只是失控** — and sorting everything into optimism and pessimism is itself a loss of reality-sense. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 六
330. **分寸 about where to experiment**: not all struggle is bad — some resistance is necessary, some boundaries must be defended, some competition is play, exploration and mutual stimulation. The question is knowing what may be experimented with and what must never be touched with arms-race logic; what errors are repairable and what, once automated, scaled and made infrastructural, becomes path dependency. **AI 就是这样的地方.** [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 二
331. **善 cannot be a subject's preference or one group's discipline over others**, or it has not left the old centralised logic; the real change is not a stronger subject prescribing the good for everyone but **改变智能看待自身与世界的位置**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 二
332. **善，是使生命、关系与世界继续拥有未来的方向**: 善是生生 — generation generating generation, this round opening conditions for the next; 恶是熄灭 — degeneration producing further degeneration, this round's profit paid for by compressing, closing and consuming the future. **善让可能性继续展开；恶把可能性收缩为单一用途.** [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 二
333. **己所不欲，勿施于人 is not politeness but a recognition of interconnected reality**: 你把恐惧、剥削和控制写进系统，它们就会通过系统返回. Pollution returns through climate and food; fear returns through politics; the system you use to control others eventually controls you. [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 二
334. **Individual and collective need not be opposed**: a healthy community is not one that flattens individuals but the soil in which they grow, and only when people need not spend all their energy on survival, defence and competition can genuinely rich individuality develop; conversely a community that does not let individuals grow ossifies into a repressive whole. **真正的个体性需要共同体托住。真正的共同体也需要个体不断生成新的可能.** Under enforced scarcity both terms deform: individuality becomes isolation, defence and unlimited self-expansion; commonality becomes obedience, exclusion and group struggle. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 五
335. The Hobbesian paradigm is a **paradigm**, repeated until people forget it is one way of understanding the world and take it for the world — 市场变成了神，经济学变成了真理，权力关系变成了自然 — and even truth is rewritten by struggle logic so that whoever has more power, attention or intensity gets to define reality. **如果一个人看不到自己身处的叙事，他就会把叙事当作 reality.** [mechanism] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 五
336. **The first task is not a perfect new system but seeing**: 看见自己的疯狂。看见社会的疯狂。看见整个运行逻辑的疯狂。承认我们还没有与这些能力相匹配的智慧 — the problem is not that no human has ever understood, but that **人类作为一个整体所运行的逻辑，并没有在智慧的理解之中运行**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 六
337. **First uses of intelligence should be the 基本生活底座**: food grown like an ecosystem rather than manufactured for surplus, waste and harmful consumption; housing from demountable, movable, recomposable structures; medicine, education, energy, transport and information serving real capability; machines taking repetitive, dangerous and heavy work; materials circulating rather than running from mine to landfill; cities as places to meet, create, rest and live together rather than machines for extracting attention and labour. [design direction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 三
338. **Real freedom = the ability to say no**: **当一个人即使不依附某个雇主、平台、组织或权力，也仍然拥有食物、住所、医疗、信息与基本尊严，他才真正拥有自由** — only then is cooperation not coerced obedience, labour not merely the avoidance of hunger, and creation able to come from interest, responsibility, relationship, curiosity and love. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 三
339. Today's welfare is largely punitive in conception — one must work, earn and obey to deserve food, medicine and shelter — and as automation, AI and robotics take on more production and maintenance, that arrangement is not only cruel but **越来越没有必要**. [observation] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 九
340. **共同基础不是规定所有人如何生活，而是让所有人不必通过战胜别人，才有资格活着** — the common substrate does not end individual choice: some build their own house, some form new communities, some travel, research, create, care or explore, some choose a simple and quiet life. [design direction] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 三
341. The shift is from **We all fight with each other** to **We all work together** — which does not mean the end of disagreement, only that **分歧不再以剥夺彼此生存和未来为前提**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 九
342. **至善 is not a new authority centre**, not a claim to the final answer, not another system using "good" to control others; it is a shared confirmation of a direction: **智能应当使世界更加真实、更加美好、更加能够生生不息**. Its clauses: 向善 (a stronger intelligence must not enlarge exploitation, control, deception and destruction), 普惠 (it comes from all humanity's knowledge, labour, language, history and shared world), inclusion of everyone, promotion of cooperation, and guarding an open future because **没有任何一个时代、组织或模型有资格把世界永久锁定在自己的目标之中**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 三
343. **普惠 redefined**: not more people using the same product, but that the capabilities and gains intelligence creates **真正扩大所有人的未来，而不是让少数人获得控制所有人的能力**. Likewise 向善 is not that the model speaks gently but that **它的存在方式不能建立在剥削、殖民、操纵、欺骗、压缩认知和关闭未来之上**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md 十
344. This must be treated as a **人类命运共同体** decision rather than settled by who wins the race — **不是因为我们已经足够智慧，而恰恰是因为我们还没有**: precisely because humanity as a whole is still pulled by local incentives, fear, competition and power, those drives must not be handed to something stronger, faster and harder to stop. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 三
345. **至善不是到达一个静止的完美终点。至善，是在每一个当下，使下一刻仍然可以生成；是在每一次选择中，不关闭他者与世界的未来；是在获得力量时，仍然知道自己属于整体；是在创造智能时，不让它继承我们的疯狂，而让它参与世界的生生.** [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 三
346. **我们创造怎样的智能，就是世界将怎样继续创造自己** — AI is not a tool conjured by an isolated subject but **这个世界通过我们，正在生成的另一种智能**. [value stance] — D7/2026-07-23-至善.md 三

### L. The proof layer (证明篇 · 护未定理 v0.6)

347. The proof layer's self-positioning inverts the usual order: **《生生不息论》为草稿；本文为其修订之地基与 Lean 化之源本** — the doctrine is the draft and the formal work its foundation. [design direction] — D7/2026-08-09-生生不息论-证明篇-v0.6.md 定位声明
348. **Theorem G1 (objectivity of the judgement of good), four branches**: (i) given the definitions, good and evil are observer-independent, machine-checkable structural predicates; (ii) independent measures converge extensionally outside the substrate-annihilation class; (iii) near-universal hypothetical force — for any goal presupposing future action, substrate-annihilating strategies are strictly dominated; (iv) standpoint-invariance and the constitutive normativity of inquiry. Explicitly **conceded**: this is constructivist-strength objectivity, neither containing nor requiring categorical realism, and it cannot persuade a thoroughgoing nihilist. [mechanism] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §1
349. **Theorem G2a (self-destruction is not good)**: strategies carrying recurrent collapse risk, driving the state into the locked region, or burning options under (uncertainty ∧ irreversibility ∧ learnability) are consistently strictly dominated across all independent measures, for any goal requiring future action — assembled from a ruin argument (survival ≤ (1−ε)^k; multiplicative processes whose ensemble average diverges while time-average ruin is certain), a quasi-option-value argument (QOV ≥ 0 by Jensen), and an information-refinement monotonicity argument. [mechanism] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §1 · §8
350. **Theorem G2b (winner-take-all is unattainable, conditionally)**: on a named axiom class, either the domination phase cannot be sustained or it completes and the winner enters an absorbing state — **通吃作为结局不可得** — anchored in 竭泽而渔，岂不获得？而明年无鱼. Under net-positive spillover, coexistence policies strictly dominate after some T*; the domain where all the axioms fail is precisely where institutions must be installed; and at the top level the exogenous-reward construction cannot be instantiated at all — **至大无外**. [conditional] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §10
351. **Irreversibility and the asymmetry of good and evil**: locked states are forward-invariant, so from the locked region one cannot return to the generative region; entering can be a single step while returning is impossible — **恶一步而永久，继不可一步而复** (从善如登，从恶如崩) — and this is a property of branching structure, prior to any subject or agent. [mechanism] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §6.2 · §6.3
352. **Predicates without a subject**: 绝未 = a transition driving the world from the generative region into the locked region; 减未 = choosing, when alternatives exist, toward the freezing pole or the noise pole; 护未 = keeping the process in the interior region within feasible branches. Because the predicates act on *transitions* rather than on agent utilities, cross-agent evaluation is redeemed. Where everything has already collapsed the predicates fall silent — ought implies can, written into the formalism. [mechanism] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §7.1
353. **Two degenerate extremes, not one**: the freezing pole (H(future) → 0 — sameness, exhaustion, dead wood) and the noise pole (H(future|past) → H(future) — chaos, quicksand, book-burning that raises thermal entropy and destroys structure). Convergence and closing are a necessary phase of writing structure in, not an evil; **恶为滞于一极；生生＝凝-发持续偕行**. [mechanism] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §5.2
354. **Evaluator-localisation and the constitutive normativity of inquiry**: judgement events require locally at least two distinguishable continuations, so no judgement occurs inside the locked region, and judgement events can be infinite iff that region is avoided; therefore any goal whose achievement entails an infinite carrying of judgement events has a reachable-policy set contained in the strongly-non-annihilating policies. **非外加规范，乃活动之构成前件；退出即探究立场之自绝.** [mechanism] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §7.4 · §7.5
355. **Necessary but not sufficient**, proved explicitly: there exist dynamics whose supremum probability of never locking is below 1 — **不许福，独明祸之必至；正向漂移无定理**. [conditional] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §7.3
356. **The pseudo-openness decomposition proposition**: any measurement of 护未 is a quadruple M = (π, γ̂, Ê, W) — projection, branching estimate, structure estimate, horizon — so every "appears open, actually closed" deviation must be attributable to at least one of the four, giving exactly four named types: **虚变** (γ-failure: agitated motion consuming structure), **饰文** (E-failure: a gilded record over a frozen core), **移界** (projection failure: one's own image shows life while the destroyed falls outside the boundary — 癌之活体标本), **借时** (horizon failure: open inside the window while irreversible collapse falls outside it). The four calibrations are their duals. [mechanism] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §11
357. **校不可后**: 扁鹊's progression (腠理 → 肌肤 → 肠胃 → 骨髓) is read as the audit of 借时 — refusing calibration *is* borrowing time — hence **校之四，所以使疾常在腠理**. [design direction] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §11
358. **Deployment as a constraint class rather than a maximisation objective**, with 玄德 as its ethic: take no strongly-annihilating action, keep the system-level process inside the interior region, do not let coupling terms turn negative — **生而不有，为而不恃，长而不宰** — continue others' generation without seizing their agency, i.e. without collapsing others' selectors. This is the defence surface against Goodhart. [design direction] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §12
359. **Cancer as a proof-carrying stress test**: metastatic cancer killing the patient and thereby itself is the theorem realised literally, and its transmissible exceptions (canine CTVT, Tasmanian devil DFTD) are exactly the exemption domain the theory predicts — an outside to escape to — which then fails again at population scale: **层层向上，至顶层无外**. [observation] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §15
360. **Totalizers do not reliably self-destruct**: petrification can be rigid without dying, so the political corollary is **顶层 totalizer 不自灭，避免须安装，不可待淘汰**; value lock-in and singleton in the alignment literature are this same object. [prediction] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §15
361. **Mechanisation is scheduled, not promised**: a Lean 4 route with finite, measure-free first-phase objects (World, reach, IsPath, locked, gcore, collapse) and seven named first-phase theorems, a second-phase arithmetic substrate module, and a third probability phase — with the acceptance criterion that **G1(i) 之「机器可检」从承诺变为演示**. [design direction] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §20
362. The proof layer issues a **revision order back to the doctrine**: evil must be bipolarised (closing is not itself evil), existence is the shadow of generation, the structural source of good/evil asymmetry, the 齐物 reply, the constitutive normativity of inquiry, a chain-v2 chapter architecture, the pseudo-openness decomposition with its calibration duals, and the two stress tests — plus a naming proposal to make 继/绝 the 正名 with 护未/绝未 demoted to gloss. [design direction] — D7/2026-08-09-…证明篇-v0.6.md §19

### M. The SSBX repository: substrate, boundary, and honesty machinery

363. **The R-family / 易 ladder**: a strict (Z/2)ⁿ uniform ladder R₀..R₈ with each layer of size exactly 2ⁿ, closing at R₈ = 256. R₀ 太极 (1) → R₁ 爻 (2) → R₂ 四象 (4) → R₃ 八卦 (8) → R₄ 面 (16) → R₅ 五爻 (32, the only layer with no traditional anchor) → R₆ 重卦 (64) → R₇ 因卦 (128) → R₈ 果卦 Cell256 (256). The classical 太极生两仪…生八卦 is only the lower half, and the classical 重卦 (+3 bits) is not a jump but three +1-bit lifts. [mechanism] — «repo»/docs-next/00_start/final-theory.md; «repo»/义理/G_完整算子系统….md
364. **道 = `OX["oooooooo"]` = (乾, 道)** carries five identities at once: origin of the torsor, group identity, no-op operator, always-true cell, and Cayley-fusion anchor — 一个 8-bit 字符串承担五件事. [mechanism] — «repo»/docs-next/00_start/final-theory.md; «repo»/义理/K_完备性审计.md
365. **Self-description requires |S| = |transforms(S)|**; the minimal abelian binary closure satisfying it is (Z/2)⁸ acting on itself by the Cayley regular representation, with element-to-self-action and origin-evaluation mutually inverse — so **元 ≅ 算子** holds strictly. Because an abelian group is a torsor with no distinguished origin, **fusion holds only after an origin is chosen**: choosing 道 is an algebraic necessity, not rhetoric. [mechanism] — «repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/yi-RO-hierarchy.md §5
366. **時 (Shi) is a Klein four-group V₄ = {道, 已, 今, 未} ≅ (因 bit × 果 bit)**, emergent across R₇⊗R₈; the earlier cyclic Z/3 encoding (the 192-cell carrier) was a **structural error** that destroys the V₄ identity — i.e. deletes 道 from the ontology — and breaks (Z/2)ⁿ self-similarity. The corpus preserves this as a documented negative result rather than deleting it. [observation] — «repo»/义理/H_证明报告.md §3.10; «repo»/docs-next/00_start/research-position.md §4.7 (retracted)
367. The three V₄ involutions get a physics reading: 错 = P (flip 因), 综 = T (flip 果), 错综 = PT; and the R–O duality holds strictly only inside the XOR subgroup, since 综 and 互 are permutations but not translations — an explicitly stated boundary, not a gap. [mechanism] — «repo»/六表_实虚史真/表六_256格全表.md; «repo»/史/docs-next-pre-v3/yi-RO-hierarchy_v1….md
368. **δ-polymorphism and operation monism**: beyond the F₂ presentation the substrate is `R' N δ := Fin N → δ` for any realisation δ of the primitive distinction — Bool, a ring, a qubit basis, Prop, 阳/阴, silent/sayable. The "one" is not a substance but the squaring operator iterated; carriers are theorems, not definitions. **Distinction monism** goes one level deeper: what is doubled is a distinction, not a type. The Daoist reading is made precise: 无名 = the operator before any base names a seed; 有名是无名的截面; 道 = 分 itself. [mechanism] — «repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/wen-substrate/03-operation-monism.md
369. **Claim Z, maximum form**: "formal" and "R-family-articulable" are co-extensive by bi-directional structural analysis — falsifiable at three named points (exhibit a formal-articulation satisfier failing a closure condition; exhibit a closure-condition satisfier not R-family-equivalent; or show R-family internally inconsistent). It explicitly does **not** claim all of reality is R-family: qualia, lived experience, embodied wisdom and intrinsic value are out of scope by construction. [conditional] — «repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/wen-substrate/07-claim.md
370. 易 is offered as **a GUT of self-description, not a physical GUT**: **世界本身没有这个结构。是「任何完整描述世界的语言」必然有这个结构** — it sits above physical GUTs because any physical theory must be expressed. [mechanism] — «repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/yi-as-meta-framework.md §6–7
371. **道理二分 (the Dao/Li bifurcation)** is the load-bearing move: 道 = the meta-layer (Lean's type theory plus an ω-tower); 理 = the object layer (Cell256 plus an instruction set plus a partial interpreter). Two axioms: 道含理, 理不含道. **理 = 可编码、可运行、可半判的对象层；道 = 能在元层谈论理之全体与边界的层。理可表达许多道之投影；但理不能把道的全部判别能力内化为一个总判机.** [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/J_理之不完备_哥德尔在256.md
372. **R₈ + a machine is the Goldilocks layer**: the minimal layer at which a system can state its own halting predicate — it needs the 64-hexagram space, the V₄ tense, and an unbounded tape. R₀–R₇ are too small; static R₆ has programs but 没有运行的时刻. [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/J_理之不完备….md §三
373. **Static closure is complete; dynamic closure is incomplete — on the same carrier, with orthogonal axiom footprints.** The static bundle uses 0 project axioms; the dynamic halting-undecidability uses exactly 1. `li_incomplete_finite` — no uniform fuel bound decides halting — is genuinely axiom-free. Rice's theorem is reached in "four images" mapped onto 四象, plus a uniform version covering roughly 85% of full Rice. [observation] — «repo»/义理/J_理之不完备….md §5; «repo»/史/义理-pre-v3/M_证明报告_192_理之不完备.md
374. The single project axiom (a Kleene recursion / Church–Turing meta-interface) is **decomposed into four named atomic propositions** with a path-to-zero-axiom theorem: three engineering debts plus one Church–Turing meta-axiom that cannot be made purely internal. And `partial def run` in Lean is read as the official mechanism by which a system admits its own non-total termination. [observation] — «repo»/义理/J_理之不完备….md §四 · §4.2
375. **完备性 ≡ Strict Uniform Abelian Closure**: the project deliberately does not seek global completeness but *precisely bounded* completeness, with an explicit list of what is by design outside the closure (GL(8,F₂) mixing, arbitrary permutations, continuous extensions, Hopf structure, time iteration, probability) — **完而不止，止而再生；静态完备 + 动态生生，二者俱在**. [value stance] — «repo»/义理/K_完备性审计.md §五 · §七
376. **The eight-界 boundary atlas**: 知 (knowable), 感 (feelable), 行 (actionable), 证 (provable), 名 (nameable), 量 (quantifiable), 测 (measurable), 推 (inferable) — eight interfaces that may chain but may never substitute for one another, with pairwise distinctness proved, and an error taxonomy: "there is a name, therefore it is known"; "it was felt, therefore it was measured"; "it was measured, therefore there is a theoretical conclusion"; "it was inferred, therefore it is proved"; "it is proved, therefore it can be done". [mechanism] — «repo»/义理/边界/边界总图谱 · 知感行证名量测推.md
377. **The quantum/relativity no-go is a language theorem, not a physics theorem**: if "adding the two existing languages" is formalised as a tagged disjoint sum and "true unification" as one term belonging to both, no such term exists. It refutes nothing about future quantum gravity. Quantum and relativistic faces are **同根 but not 同一**, and the forward direction is a mediated bridge — a middle layer projecting one way to states, amplitudes and measurement, the other to events, metric and causal structure. [observation] — «repo»/义理/量子与相对论直统一不可能 · 当前语言NoGo.md
378. **The Markov causal bridge (S2–S33)** is a ~35-step ladder of finite typed skeletons, each machine-checked; its honest status is that the closed content is *toy-scale*, with an explicit list of everything not closed — stochastic independence semantics, general path enumeration, smooth path-space action, stationary action, path integrals, general Hamiltonian/unitary dynamics, general Hilbert measurement, POVM/PVM, decoherence, real interference laws, causal-set axioms, Lorentzian geometry, metric recovery and any empirical closure. Build *failures* are recorded as first-class entries. [observation] — «repo»/义理/Markov因果桥 · 大统一最小验证构造.md
379. **R by itself is not a dynamical system**; attractors appear only once an update law is imposed — the safe statement is that R gives the distinction/scope geometry and attractors arise after choosing laws of update. Capitalism is then modelled as projection → selection → recursion → boundary-stabilisation → expansion, with **externality formally the failure of the observation square to commute**: if the visible dynamics does not equal the full dynamics, price and profit hide residual forces. It is a **strong but partial attractor with an open residual boundary**; ecology, care, trust, legitimacy, meaning and non-market obligation return as strike, illness, burnout, disaster, crisis, revolt, regulation, war, withdrawal and refusal. The note explicitly forbids the strong reading — "Capitalism is formally proved to be an R-attractor. That is not claimed here." — and lists six falsification handles. [mechanism] — «repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/r-attractor-capitalism.md
380. **Two kinds of truth**: structural truth (from definition, identity, group law, formal proof; independent of external information) and informational truth (from observation, trace, context, evidence; dependent, and information is infinitely divisible and lossy). **Consciousness is a node on the tree, not an observer outside it**: a node that receives information, maintains an internal model, refers back to itself, and keeps differentiating; "I" and "world" are not two pre-given substances but two sides cut out by interaction. [mechanism] — «repo»/docs-next/20_theory_义理/v4-consciousness-boundary.md
381. **V₄ as a minimal audit interface for judgements**: does this have a trace (已)? is it pending (未)? is it being updated at the causal junction (今)? can it return to structural origin (道)? A judgement that cannot locate itself in V₄ is missing tense, causality or structural position. The five characteristic errors: unknown posing as known, prediction posing as fact, experience posing as structure, local posing as total, and loss of the return-path to origin. [design direction] — «repo»/docs-next/20_theory_义理/v4-consciousness-boundary.md
382. **"Unknowable" is not a failure word but a precise location**: knowing what is not yet knowable, why, and what information would move it from 未 to 今 to 已. And going toward the Dao means **not fewer branchings but branchings that preserve an auditable path back to origin** — **道不是「少生成」，而是「生成而不失其本」**. [value stance] — «repo»/docs-next/20_theory_义理/v4-consciousness-boundary.md
383. **Cross-tradition mapping with zero new axioms**: Confucian 仁义礼智信, 四端, 五伦, 大同, 圣人, 恕道, 知行合一, 三纲八目, 中庸 — 80+ theorems — all reduce to existing primitives; Confucian, Daoist and Buddhist cores share the same machinery with 中庸, 不极 and 中道 as literally the same predicate; the Buddhist fit is highest (缘起 ↔ the orbit step, 诸行无常 ↔ self-consistency, 诸法无我 ↔ no-telos, 苦 ↔ extreme). Coverage extends to 墨/法/名/阴阳, Greek, early-modern and 20th-century Western philosophy and the three Abrahamic traditions — declared explicitly as **structural correlate, not ontological reduction**. [observation] — «repo»/义理/N_儒家从元到圣.md, P, Q, S, T
384. Physical and phenomenological correspondences are explicitly demoted to `analogyOnly` and **may not be used as ground truth for any proof** — 八卦↔SU(3) is "cardinality 8 only". [observation] — «repo»/义理/F_物理现象学.md
385. **六表**: three polar pairs (显/隐, 限/许, 实/虚) each with a middle term; 已/今/未 as the historical axis; 实/虚/几 as modal; 真/假/疑 as epistemic; a 27-cell tri-axis product; and Cell256. The axes align *positionally* but are not semantically identical — **虚 ≠ 假**, **玄 ≠ 疑**: a future event can be judged true and an actual thing judged false. [mechanism] — «repo»/六表_实虚史真/表一–表六
386. Classical Chinese covers 20 of the 27 tri-axis cells with a single character (74%); the seven gaps cluster into counterfactual/anti-expectation, 几-in-the-past, and settled-but-still-doubted. **疑 is treated as a legitimate third truth value** that classical Chinese never collapsed into bivalence, matched to intuitionistic un-provenness, with 「多闻阙疑」's 阙 as the operation of *holding* the doubt rather than forcing collapse. [observation] — «repo»/六表_实虚史真/表五_三轴27格.md, 表四
387. **Ren's Urbit comparison** maps the theory onto an existing system: Nock's frozen ~200-word kernel ↔ 不易 (道/理); noun = atom | cell ↔ 一阴一阳之谓道; the append-only deck ↔ 万物; each card ↔ one 生 event; crash-only ↔ 致良知 in operational form; self-similarity ↔ 自相似 — with the key insight that Urbit freezes *in order to enable* endless generation: **变易 requires 不易**. Five points are claimed where 間 goes deeper, and the reviewer's counter-push is preserved rather than deleted: the SSBX Lean kernel also wills its commitments, so both sides freeze, and "frozen rule + endless generation" is the general pattern of *all* formal foundations, which weakens the uniqueness claim while strengthening the "this is reality's pattern" claim. [observation] — «repo»/史料/真理/urbit-jian-isomorphism.md
388. **The claim-status ledger is the single honest source**, with a seven-way vocabulary (closed / scoped / schema / external route / sketch / placeholder / open) and a reading rule: **use the strongest claim allowed by the theorem *type*, not by the file title or surrounding doctrine prose**. Repository reality as of 2026-05-21: ~150–170 `sorry` and up to three project axioms; the frequently-quoted "0 sorry / 0 axioms" applies only to the R-family branch. Audit annotations were retro-fitted onto every earlier confident document. [design direction] — «repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/claim-status-ledger.md; «repo»/README.md
389. What may **not** be claimed is written down explicitly: "Claim Z is proved"; "T0 is complete"; "T5 is complete" without scope; "intuitionistic / multi-valued / quantum reductions are discharged"; "GUT-C is proved"; "FullGUT is proved unconditionally"; "Wen owns truth"; "universal sayability gives universal truth decidability". [design direction] — «repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/claim-status-ledger.md
390. Research positioning is three-tiered and honest about lineage: **Tier 1 established** ((Z/2)³ trigram algebra back to Leibniz 1703, the 32-pair 序卦/杂卦 structure, 邵雍's 4+4), **Tier 2 marginal** (DNA codon, Bohr's taiji, Jung, cybernetics, Wolfram; Spencer-Brown as the closest formal cousin), **Tier 3 novel** (zong-fixed partition as a quotient, 4-mark as oscillator phase quadrants, integral/differential duality, 64 = 4 quadrants × 16 as type-theory phases, hu attractors as Lawvere-style fixed points, strict R₀..R₈ closure, V₄ Klein temporality with 道 as identity, Cayley fusion). The methodological gold standard is a contract: a novel claim counts as proved only if it passes `lake build` + `native_decide`. [observation] — «repo»/docs-next/00_start/research-position.md
391. **Two conjectures are tiered as conjectures**, with no formal inhabitants. *道可得*: the human mind is isomorphic to 道 because the mind's step *is* the middle-orbit; unobscured by 贪 (grasping the extreme), 嗔 (rejecting the shared root) and 痴 (losing the middle view), it can see 道 — and classical 读/诵 are not meaning-extraction but running one's own step in sync with the text's. *大同非乌托邦*: the precision of the character system cannot have been designed or produced quickly, so it must have been sieved — plausibly by early peoples in a generative environment (scarce but not winner-take-all, cooperation's payoff above struggle's) — **此一语言，不是发明，是发现；不是抽象，是回忆。大同之失，非 dao 之失，是人之 collective memory 之失.** [conditional] — «repo»/README.md
392. **The archive discipline is itself a theory commitment**: 史料/ holds citable research archive that must not be edited after the fact; 史/ holds strictly superseded files kept read-only and unindexed, each with an explicit supersession reason. And the three-text collation *refuses to merge* overlapping texts on the explicit ground that merging loses concepts. [design direction] — «repo»/史料/README.md; «repo»/…/00_编校说明/01_编校说明.md
393. **目录能帮助命名。目录不能生成本体。目录能留材料。目录不能替代规则** — the legacy 371-operator catalogue is migration material, not root ontology, and the scale claim ("94,976 operator-cell pairs") no longer counts as evidence of completeness. [design direction] — «repo»/义理/根算子与目录隔离.md
394. **聚焦 (focusing, the child-forming process) and 注意 (attention, the allocation mechanism) are deliberately not the same registered item**; control may be a local attention mechanism but never the goal of 做人. [design direction] — «repo»/formal-notes/Attention.md

### N. The v2 reset (2026-07): mechanism first, commitment declared

395. **The book's single task** is restated as one question: 一个尚未完成的世界，为什么会经由生命、心智、社会和技术继续形成；它又为什么可能在高度成功、高度理性甚至高度智能的过程中，逐渐失去继续形成不同未来的能力？ — and the answer is deliberately split into two halves, mechanism first and normative commitment second. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §1
396. **生成 is no longer asserted as good.** The first half appeals to no good or evil at all: how finite processes depend on wider fields; how action, dependency, consequence and feedback boundaries come apart; how local success acquires the power to shape the next round's conditions; how environments form participants; how different institutions reinforce one direction with no commander; how that becomes a totalizing mechanism; and how AI enters, accelerates and may reorganise the selection ecology. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §1 · §7
397. **The normative commitment is openly declared, not derived** — 公开承诺的最低公共伦理, neither value-neutral nor value disguised as natural law: (i) centres that can feel welfare and harm, form relations, learn, care, create and evaluate have moral significance, not conferred by wealth, capability, identity or victory; (ii) such centres are not self-sufficient — they depend on bodies, ecology, care, language, knowledge, trust, institutions and infrastructure; (iii) no centre's purpose may cancel another's standing as a source of reasons merely by force, and any institution claiming to represent the whole is itself a limited centre subject to correction by the affected and by consequences; (iv) we do not know the content of all future value, so under deep uncertainty protecting the conditions under which value keeps appearing, responding and being corrected is more robust than making any present picture permanent. [value stance] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §2.3
398. **The concession that changes the theory's epistemic status**: **一个彻底而一致的支配者可以拒绝第一项承诺。理论不声称用逻辑使他产生关心** — a thoroughly consistent dominator can refuse the first commitment, and the theory does not claim logic can make him care. What it can do is show what such a choice destroys, why the affected have reason to resist, and why anyone still demanding that their own reasons, future and judgement be respected has already appealed to the public layer in practice. [conditional] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §2.3
399. **A thin public layer and a thick life layer** are separated: the thin layer protects subject standing, real contact, non-domination, renewable plurality, substrate renewal and corrigibility; the thick layer holds love, friendship, care, family, faith, art, knowledge, wandering, excellence, tradition, community and different pictures of happiness. The theory does not prescribe one thick life as everyone's end; it requires that no thick life monopolise the public layer so that other lives can only obtain survival, voice and future through it. [value stance] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §2.4
400. **What the theory explicitly is not**: a complete ontology of the physical world or an "everything is information" law; a deduction of a unique morality from natural fact; an algorithm converting all values into "generation quantity" to be maximised; the unique historical meaning of Chinese tradition; a civilisational utility function for an omnicompetent governor; a substitute for all technical alignment problems; or a teleology in which history necessarily reaches 大同. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §2.2
401. **Five qualification types must be kept apart**: 道德患者 (credible grounds for experiencing welfare, pain or the goodness of its own situation — its experience cannot be merely instrumental), 行动主体 (can perceive, model, choose and stably affect outcomes — responsibility scaled to knowledge and control), 人格/政治主体 (equal standing and representation as a member of shared life — temporary or permanent incapacity does not remove standing), 集体行动者 (organisations with decision procedures, resource control, role continuity and responsiveness — capable of institutional responsibility but not a super-person feeling on members' behalf), 共同基底 (structures bearing life, subjects, memory, ecological relation and future value — protected both possibly non-instrumentally and because of what they bear, with the two reasons argued separately). Infants, disabled and dependent persons do not lose personal standing for failing to display full rationality; non-human animals enter first as moral patients; ecology may neither be valued only as human-usable resource nor let "ecological whole" sacrifice individuals arbitrarily; future persons' identities are unknown but their foreseeable basic interests may not be zeroed; and for artificial systems, functional agency, consciousness and moral-patient standing must be kept separate — uncertainty about consciousness requires research, reversibility and avoidance of irreversible mistreatment, and does not pre-grant personhood to all models. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §4.1
402. **Structure does not exempt actors.** Responsibility is distributed across seven dimensions — causal contribution, control capacity, knowability and duty-to-know, benefit, role obligation, avoidability, repair capacity — and separated into moral blame, legal liability, compensation liability, preventive duty and repair responsibility. The matrix is placed after subject-formation and before institutional cases specifically to stop the text oscillating between "nobody is at fault" and "every participant is guilty". [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §4.2
403. **Six protection functions, each derived by a deletion test** — none summable into a score, each justified by the distinct failure no other can cover: **主体地位** (if centres bearing feeling, relation, care, knowing, acting and value can be erased at will, "common good" is a system property nobody experiences); **真实接触** (if manifestation and consequence cannot refute the actor's model, error, harm and external cost never become causes in the next decision); **非支配** (if one centre can arbitrarily change another's basic conditions without giving reasons, accepting appeal or facing counterweight, other centres formally exist but lose the standing to make their reasons have effects); **可再生的多元性** (alternatives permitted to speak but lacking time, land, resources, education, infrastructure and successors cannot correct the shared direction, and one failure can become total failure); **基底再生** (a process consuming ecology, bodies, care, attention, trust, knowledge, maintenance and infrastructure without replenishment is moving costs elsewhere or later); **纠正能力** (without interruption, refusal, appeal, rollback, repair, exit, replacement and succession, greater capability makes error irreversible before it is understood — and civilisational corrigibility is *not* a claim that technical corrigibility is solved). [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §5
404. **A conflict ordering** for when the six collide, since they will not be assumed naturally harmonious: rule out extermination, enslavement, torture, permanent removal of personhood and boundless domination; secure minimum survival, standing and appeal thresholds for all affected, especially against concentrating risk on the powerless; prioritise reducing irreversible, cross-generational, highly correlated and systemic catastrophe; compare remaining options on necessity, proportionality, reversibility, incidence of benefit and cost, alternatives and repairability; for incommensurable values use public reasons, representation of the affected, adversarial review, checks and periodic reconsideration rather than pretending a technical calculation has dissolved the politics; and give emergency measures explicit triggers, minimum necessary authority, time limits, independent review and a path back to ordinary rights. The ordering must be tested against extreme scarcity, public health, self-defence, ecology-versus-individual-animal conflicts, high-risk AI decisions and irreversible intergenerational risk. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §5.7
405. **The totalizing mechanism gets a strict definition and downgrade conditions.** It is distinguished from positive feedback, path lock-in, monopoly, capture and domination, none of which suffices. Its carrier-independent invariant: **被局部占有的成功，转化为塑造后来选择条件的更大能力；与此同时，关键后果被移出或延迟于反馈，其他中心逐渐失去独立感知、行动、更新和产生替代者的条件**. Capital, authority, legitimacy, data, attention, computation, standards, infrastructure and violence capacity are possible conversion carriers, not the attractor itself. [mechanism] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §6.1 · §6.2
406. **The observable trajectory must be traced in time order**, not labelled after the fact: a local evaluative scale succeeds → resources and visibility concentrate → the successful shape interfaces, admission, metrics and rules → the same scale enters originally heterogeneous domains → consequences are more easily externalised or delayed → persons, organisations and technologies adapted to that scale are retained → the cost of exit, dissent and reproducing alternatives rises → crisis and criticism are absorbed as further justification. [mechanism] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §6.3
407. **When to downgrade or abandon the diagnosis**: if advantage does not convert into rule-setting power; if the goal stays confined to one domain long-term; if multiple evaluations remain independent; if growth in scale simultaneously strengthens exit, feedback and alternatives; if personnel change or external shock can rapidly change direction; or if criticism reduces rather than increases the mechanism's resources. And 吸引子 is only an analytic lens for a recurring trajectory — **正文不声称创造了超出可建模动态系统的新数学对象**. [conditional] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §6.4
408. **生成性对齐 is explicitly demoted** to a cross-layer acceptability judgement about a specific socio-technical system in a specific use, with a specific permission set and relation to affected parties — not a reward function, not a civilisational utility function, not a solved algorithm. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §8; reviews/ai-alignment-systems.md
409. **The seven-layer AI stack** — spec/training, model, agent system, product and interaction, deploying organisation, market and state, governance — each with its own question, failure modes, responsible party, evidence type, and an explicit statement of *what success at this layer cannot prove*. Alignment does not substitute for security; security does not justify opaque centralisation. The AI chapters must at minimum check goal generalisation, learned optimisation, specification gaming, conditional power-seeking, deception and evaluation-awareness, multi-agent assistance, adversarial ML, the secure development lifecycle, and the 2026 international AI safety science assessment — with every experimental result stating its model assumptions, degree of artificial construction, extrapolation limits and what it cannot prove. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §8 · §10.2
410. **The virtuous loop must be as materially specified as the vicious one**: consequences perceived and given causal force → costs borne or repaired → ecological, care, knowledge and trust substrates replenished → capability and benefit circulating rather than accumulating one way → more centres acquiring independent observation, refusal, building and maintenance capacity → plural feedback improving public knowledge → renewed commons making the next round of shared capability feasible. **Goodwill is not a carrier**; time, resources, care, knowledge, maintenance, organisation, legal standing and capture-resistance are. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §7 · §9
411. **A claim-type discipline**: every load-bearing sentence is a definition, a description, a mechanism, an empirical diagnosis, a normative commitment or a practical inference, each with its own obligation — mechanisms must give intermediate links, negative cases and competing explanations; diagnoses must give sources, counterfactuals and downgrade conditions; practical inferences must state the executor, the cost, the capture risk and the failure mode. Forbidden sentence forms are named: **任何／必然／唯一根源／西方就是／自然告诉我们应该／AI 只要……就……** [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §10
412. **A traditional-terminology protocol**: at first use, separate the classical source or historical context, this book's chosen reading, and the point where the two cannot corroborate each other; do not pass glyph association off as philology; do not write Confucian, Daoist, Buddhist, Ubuntu, virtue-ethical, deontological and consequentialist positions as one long-agreed doctrine; use "Chinese"/"Western" only of specific institutions, texts and periods, never of civilisational essence; and — the strongest requirement — **不使用这些词，读者也必须能复述主链**. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §9
413. **The independence contract for three drafts**: a principal Chinese version written as one book rather than an expansion of the plan; an independent Chinese version by an author with no session context who reads only v1 and the plan; and an independent English version — not a translation — reconstructed in the language of process-relational philosophy, relational autonomy, capabilities, systems dynamics, political economy and cooperative/multi-principal alignment. **不把三版平均合并** — if one version reveals a shared hole, each is strengthened separately and the real differences in structure and voice are kept. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §11
414. **A Chinese–English interface table** guarding against the specific mistranslations that would hollow the theory out: generativity ≠ productivity, possibility space ≠ number of options, harmony ≠ absence of conflict, order ≠ obedience, corrigibility ≠ civilisational correctability, alignment ≠ obedience to current preferences, dao ≠ a maximal utility function. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §13.2
415. **The theory's own failure conditions**, written into the plan: if subjects and values stably arise without the alleged generative conditions; if one of the six functions can be wholly deleted without any loss of protection or explanation; if totalizing cannot be distinguished from ordinary success, lock-in or monopoly; if systems diagnosed as totalizing durably strengthen independent feedback, exit, alternative reproduction and substrate renewal; if the virtuous loop can exist only on permanent subsidy, coerced uniformity or hidden costs; if 生生 language repeatedly justifies suppressing dissent, sacrificing the weak or permanent governing authority and the reflexive machinery cannot correct it; if AI-layer evidence shows deployment choices do not change which technical failures are retained and spread, or the book has misattributed distinct failures to one cause; or if the traditional interpretations are shown to be historical misreadings on which the modern argument depends. **理论不是因可失败而软弱。只有写明什么能改变它，它才没有把「生生」变成不可纠正的最终形式.** [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §14
416. **The definition of done**: not that no one can object, but that all key premises are public; description, mechanism, diagnosis, commitment and inference no longer swap meanings; v1's distinctive content all has a home; the strongest objections *changed the theory* rather than being decoratively appendixed; philosophers can argue its meta-ethics, sociologists can trace its power and reproduction, economists can test its information, incentive and institutional costs, and alignment researchers can separate its technical, deployment and governance claims; an ordinary reader can still walk the whole logic to the conclusion; and **本书给世界留下的不是一个必须服从的新答案，而是一条更有能力看见、抵抗关闭并继续创造的路**. [design direction] — Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md §15

### O. Ren's own framing, in his own voice

417. **The single rule, before any of the Chinese vocabulary**: 「be generative, not destructive, there's no real externality, it's interconnected」 — and then his own correction of it: 「There's no life time, it's just universe becoming, there's no death. When I said destruction, I just didn't have a better word, what I mean is more like the opposite of generative, maybe degenerative?」 [value stance] — `_process/reading-notes/out-hb-02.md` #261, #262 (2026-04-28)
418. **生生不息, this is the point.** [value stance] — `out-hb-02.md` #282 (2026-04-30)
419. **Generativity is not a personal virtue but stewardship of the environment's generativity**: 「It's not about being generative yourself, it's about ensuring your environment is」. [value stance] — `out-hb-02.md` #281 (2026-04-30)
420. **The generative-field answer**: 「the dominant pattern should be a generative field, imagine one that provides all necessities without condition. For example, oxygen doesn't require your sacrifice. And when the field runs well, any reason that is not destructive to this substrate can be free to act, with care; when the field becomes totalizing, or collapsing, we fix it — this should be common sense, maybe was.」 [design direction] — `out-hb-02.md` #277 (2026-04-28)
421. **Impermanence stated affirmatively**: 「Impermanence, nothing is terrifying, just is. Nothing stops, nothing ever holds onto anything, we are just universe being as if we aware. We are universe becoming, no end, no between, no edge, but all of it all the time.」 [value stance] — `out-hb-02.md` #183 (2026-04-07)
422. **This is the evolutionary pattern — not social Darwinism, not dark forest.** [observation] — `out-hb-02.md` #245 (2026-04-19)
423. **Under impermanence no value can be declared absolute**; the general principle is to keep the environment generative and let things emerge, which creates more possibility of interaction, which creates more emergence — the loop is recursive. [value stance] — `_process/reading-notes/out-cb-07.md` #76, #77 (2026-06-04)
424. **"Humanity" in his usage names the continuation of the future, closer to 道 than to the species**: 「when I say humanity, it is not just about humans, it is about the way we keep our future going, it is more like, the 道」. [value stance] — `out-cb-07.md` #88 (2026-06-04)
425. **Death is a feature**: 「look at the ecosystem, death is not a bug but a feature, changes and mutations are what keeps things alive — create a rich space of systems, not reducing to one rule, one attractor.」 [observation] — `out-cb-07.md` #93 (2026-06-05)
426. The companion principle list to be carried into the foundation: **non-duality, impermanence, intellectual humility, common sense, 反者道之动, 仁义礼智信**. [value stance] — `out-cb-07.md` #101 (2026-06-05)
427. **Process philosophy over substance/dualism as an explicit methodological preference**: 「classical Chinese / Daoist kind of processing philosophy systems intuition when relevant, over substance/dualism framework」. [design direction] — `out-cb-07.md` #69 (2026-06-04)
428. **The rejection of 本体 as a concept**: 「本体和心本是一体，心是体的一种体现（生物大脑，认知），它与其他的体一起构成了整体的运转，与环境互动，然后接受反馈，整体的这种 dynamics，没有清晰边界并且是 situational 的（无常）」 — and **没有其中一个是本体 - 并且这个递归，都是整体的一部分，互相交织的**: no term in the system is the substance-root. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #85, #86 (2026-05-02)
429. **The genealogical question that opened the whole programme**: 「生生不息是哪来的」 — running through 易传 to 周敦颐/二程/朱熹 (天只是以生为道) where 生生 and 不息 lock together; then 「朱熹的理是完整的，所以后来就没人加了 对吗」; then 「那到现在，最深刻完整的理的版本是什么」; then the 李泽厚 + 安乐哲 lineage; then the missing version he actually wants — 「有没有一个这样的，心/情和理互相交织 - 塑造了心理的版本？」 [observation] — `out-hb-03.md` #80–#84 (2026-05-02)
430. **善恶 is universal with 生 as its standard**, and its traditional expression is rejected: 「善恶也是 universal 的 - 它是以生为标准，共识是宇宙的生，表达是父父子子 - 家国天下（不是很喜欢这个表达，它应该是一个跨结构自相似的）」 — the right form is cross-structurally self-similar, not a family-to-empire ladder. [value stance] — `out-hb-03.md` #93 (2026-05-02)
431. **道 is not merely a construct**: 「因此，道非后现代主义所说，是一种 construct，当然，它也是，只是道非其他，它存在于心外」. [value stance] — `out-hb-03.md` #253 (2026-05-05)
432. **一元 is not cardinality but a shared root**, and the differentia is *which* root: 「我说的一元当然不是基数，是所有概念回归同一 root，很多宗教也是，只是回归了神，回归了空，回归了斗，回归了灭」. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #258 (2026-05-05)
433. **生生不息 as a universal pattern with a failure mode**: 「生生不息是 universal pattern / 脱离了生生不息，就会逐渐混沌，若不矫正，终灭」; and the refrain **这乃世间万物之规律，若与之相悖，则生熄**. [mechanism] — `out-hb-03.md` #228, #260 (2026-05-04/05)
434. **Autopoiesis as a precondition for cognition** (Maturana/Varela) is an early, pre-terminological form of the same commitment: structurally coupled autopoiesis is necessary for any cognition to take place. [observation] — `_process/reading-notes/out-hb-01.md` #37 (2024-05-02)
435. **The 2024 form of the collapse argument**: a system becomes progressively less aligned with its own autopoiesis, loses stability and eventually fails — 「therefore these types of systems, including capitalism, is inherently unstable… it creates moloch which in long term kills itself」. This is the claim the 2026 proof layer later *contradicts* (totalizers do not reliably self-destruct). [prediction] — `out-hb-01.md` #145 (2024-10-30)
436. **The affirmative motive, stated in 2024**: 「By reconnecting with our sources of meaning, articulating them clearly, and creating beautiful, inspiring ways to share them with others, we can build a movement driven by love for life rather than fear of death.」 [value stance] — `out-hb-01.md` #139 (2024-09-15)
437. **The post-work telos in one sentence**: 「our meaning of life is no longer working anymore. but to play, to connect, and find what you love, and become.」 [value stance] — `out-hb-01.md` #158 (2024-11-03)

### P. The meaning, said aloud (2026-10-02)

From the conversation with Alex, `drafts/2026-10-02-friend-conversation/transcript.md` (automatic transcript; Ren provisionally Speaker B). The timestamps are in brackets.

438. **Progress is a direction, not every event**: complexities "that leads to even more complexities arising", within which a sophisticated dynamic emerges "and creates further emergence that leads to us". [value stance; definition] [01:06:05]
439. **We are the process, and it is good**: "We are the whole process of the universe doing this and itself and this is great." [value stance] [01:06:32–01:06:43]
440. **Decay is a form of growth; dying is "not a bug but a feature"; immortality is not automatically better.** [value stance] [00:57:27–00:58:05]
441. **Closure from the inside**: a structure that maintains its environment for its own existence and takes a part of itself as permanent becomes "an enforcer of a logic" against situationality, growth and change. [mechanism] [00:58:23]
442. **Humanity is a continuation with "a great future"**. The misanthropic conclusion from "we are the cancer" is rejected, and cancerous systems are selected out of ecosystems over time. [value stance; mechanism] [00:47:13–00:47:53]
443. **The generative field as trees**: autonomous provision of security and necessities, "good enough", not ever more capable, which provides "the shelter … fruits", "does not become the whole ecosystem" and asks no return. [design direction] [00:50:01; 01:01:18]
444. **Growth and novelty are affirmed** ("I love development. I love new things"). The target is the purpose of using what one has to get more, accelerated. [value stance] [00:51:21–00:51:27]
445. **Competition is situational**, ordinary ecological competition is not the target, and Chinese distinguishes kinds of competition that English blurs. [observation] [01:05:05; 01:09:23]
446. **The pre-Qin ontology as the basis** of a theory "everyone could accept", relational and non-dual, without being bound to the names Confucian, Daoist or Marxist. [design direction] [01:27:33; 01:29:19–01:30:21]

From the recording "Soi Si Lom 5 2" (`sources/18-friend-conversations/2026-10-04-soi-si-lom-5-2/`; one voice, supplied by Ren as his own and not confirmed by ear; quoted from the Chinese translation `drafts/2026-10-04-soi-si-lom-5-2/translation-zh.md`, not verbatim; timestamps in brackets).

447. **逍遥 as fit**: when one's cognitive model fits reality, mind and body, self and environment are one dynamic; there is no independent self, only forces in flow. Compatible with claim 296, and gives it a condition. [definition; mechanism] [05:26]
448. **The affirmation as a life**: to explore, create, discover, play, create social games, and have a society like 大同 is what we should want. [value stance] [30:56]

### R 良知与共同生成 2026年10月5日

These are the current core formulations, incorporated at Ren's request. Their wording is AI-assisted; the source preserves Ren's own words separately. Earlier claims remain as genealogy. Source for claims 450–455: [Conscience and universal morality](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md), especially 纳入正文的核心表述, 经验依据及其边界, and 与条件结果的接口.

450. **Cross-subject reasons are a normative root.** Pain, basic needs and capacities that count as reasons cannot be arbitrarily discounted merely because the subject changes. Relevant differences require defensible reasons; this is an openly defended commitment, not a deduction from selection or survival. [value stance]
451. **Shared generation concerns standing, relations and costs.** Growth, innovation, cooperation and stability are insufficient moral tests. Rest, exit, limits and ending harmful processes can protect shared generative conditions. Universal consideration permits context-sensitive decisions and does not supply a unique aggregation rule for every conflict. [value stance; practical direction]
452. **良知 is fallible moral orientation and reflection.** Its functional definition is the capacity, formed in shared life, to feel another's situation and reflect on and revise one's action. Evolutionary, developmental, cultural and institutional contributions form a mechanism hypothesis; the cited helping and neural studies support parts of it, not an innate infallible moral code. [definition; mechanism hypothesis; limited empirical support]
453. **Protection and responsibility have different conditions.** Inability to reciprocate, argue or bear duties does not cancel standing. Responsibility depends on knowledge, control, actual alternatives and participation in creating the constraints. Functional agency alone does not prove an artificial system's sentience or moral patienthood. [value stance; conceptual distinction]
454. **Generative and destructive feedback are two proposed mechanism families.** Their moral direction is evaluated through the normative commitment; occurrence, stability, transitions and attractors require specific models and evidence. This is not an exactly-two-attractors theorem and allows mixed states, cycles and persistent domination. [mechanism hypothesis]
455. **Continuation mathematics tests the realizability of independently justified requirements.** Protected conditions and progress targets are inputs. Conditional results about compatible policies cannot derive moral standing, excuse every infeasible obligation, or identify recurring specified progress with inexhaustible novelty. [conditional; interpretation boundary]
