## Genealogy

Two things make this genealogy confusing until they are said plainly. First, **two independent version-numbering tracks both called 《生生不息论》** exist: the May 2026 classical-Chinese formal series (v4 → v13.2/v14, in `~/Downloads` and the SSBX repository) and the July 2026 prose-book series (v1 six parts → v2 plan, in the zhishan repository). They are not the same lineage, and no document reconciles the numbering. Second, a third track — 道理 v10 → v11 → v11.1 → v12 — runs beside them inside the same repository. When a source says "v12", check the directory.

**2024 (pre-terminological).** The mechanism exists without any of the Chinese vocabulary: societies select power-holders by whichever single value they tokenise as power; the selection self-intensifies to collapse; autopoiesis is a precondition of cognition; "such systems, including capitalism, are inherently unstable… the Moloch they create eventually kills them"; and the affirmative motive is stated as a movement driven by love for life rather than fear of death (`out-hb-01.md`). No 生生不息, no 易, no 大同, and AI is still a pattern-recogniser that lets us *see* attractors rather than a moral participant.

**2026-04.** The principle arrives in English first — 「be generative, not destructive, there's no real externality, it's interconnected」 — is immediately corrected (destructive → degenerative), then given its Chinese name: 「生生不息, this is the point」. Two refinements land in the same fortnight: generativity is stewardship of the *environment's* generativity rather than a personal virtue, and the generative field is one that supplies necessities unconditionally, like oxygen.

**2026-05-02 · 生生不息的出处.** The single most consequential conversation. It opens genealogically (where does 生生不息 come from? — through 易传 to 周敦颐/二程/朱熹), moves to the state of the art in 理 (李泽厚's sedimentation, 安乐哲's process-relational Confucianism), rejects 本体 as a concept, and then Ren gives his own derivation in two steps (claims 50–51). He commissions a 古文 derivation from 元/几 to 生生不息, adds 不极/中, 中和美德, replaces 人 with 仁, states the alignment thesis (做人要善 as the real alignment), asks for a proof that it is the *only* reasonable alignment — and then demolishes his own proof programme: 「这个东西根本就不能证明…它只是完全自洽又有 universality」. In the same session the **开/闭 pivot** happens: 开 replaces "expanding possibility space" because expansion is maximisable; 善/恶 becomes 开/闭's ethical manifestation; the whole vocabulary is re-derived from 开; **伪开** is named; **开 = 未∧可∧应∧转**; and three critiques are imported and accepted — 护闭 vs 成闭, 模有高下, 感须校正. That is v9's entire doctrinal content, arrived at in one day.

**2026-05-03/04 · the formal explosion.** Fifteen versions in about forty-eight hours.

- **v4 · 全面修订版** — 21 sections of classical Chinese, epigraph 「自宇宙之史，观自身之性；识此性者，谓之人」. Establishes 元/几/势/机/聚散, direction ≠ purpose, 不极 and 中, 理/心/情/积, 积生积 and 因生因, self-similarity, 宇宙即理, 性, good/evil, the is/ought dissolution, 人 as recognition-and-enactment, alignment as co-alignment. Closes on 做人要善.
- **v5 · 五常完整版** — same spine, epigraph changed 人 → **仁**. Adds four chapters (和, 美, 德, and the 五常), 死礼, 智's reflexivity, and 善、美、德三者，异名同实.
- **与生生不息对齐之必然** — not a version of the treatise but its application: the RLHF/CAI/CEV/IRL critique, the ten-way reductio, the transcendental and performative arguments, empirical convergence, the three-pillar web, eight objections answered.
- **v9 · 护开五备版** — **the doctrinal turning point.** §〇 (the theory's own position, plus three anti-metric guards), 开/闭 as root, 成闭/护闭, 伪开 with its seven instances, 境, 模, 四校, 道之可感, and the demotion of the cosmic-acceleration story from necessity to one historical manifestation. Closes on the urgency clause.
- **v9 · 开境护闭校正版 (05-04)** — copy-edit plus 可内证 and the local-goal/terminal-purpose qualification; costs the standalone 伪开 section and the explicit 四校 label.
- **v10 · 间生形式版** — **the second turning point.** Root shifts 元 → **間**; `J(x)=x+ΔJ(x)` becomes 续(态)=态⊕間(态); a formal system is declared; legality constraints rule out 乱; death and collapse stated as real. Compresses 理心情积 and loses the extended 中/和/美/德.
- **间生论 · 形式补** — an explicitly non-substitutive algebraic scaffold whose value is that it *falsifies*: multi-valued I(x), non-associativity of ⊕, closure as absorbing state, the benevolent agent's floor, terminal-goal atrophy, 伪开's cross-time form, and the **non-convergence of the four calibrations**. Declares three impossibilities and two powers.
- **v11 · 间开定本** — the largest single file; consolidates the 間 root with the 开/闭/境/模/四校 apparatus, then opens the third turn in three appendices: the subject/object dual reading in English, the field formalism (Γ, 气, CoOpen, Ren_F), and 三相总纲 with evolution-as-continuation-selection, scale non-transitivity, and 正其筛.
- **间开本 v3 · 自由和平之轴** — written in 三相 format throughout, in LaTeX. Adds the **value axis no earlier version had**: 平, 好-as-interval, 自由 = 不向坏, 繁荣, the ladder. Also 伪开's three types, functorial self-similarity, DimOpening, 易之微连, and the **worked alignment example**.
- **v11 · 校验本** — **the verification turn.** 判准之判准; four abuse modes; mandatory scale and window; three-layer legality; graded 开度 with short-board sensitivity; four-layer closure; alternative-path sets; action- vs state-capture; 鲁棒四校 with a second order; reality-reachability; relevant-scale closure; 道's subject filled; lattice fixed points with a **待校** verdict; **正危**; twelve counterexamples.
- **v11.1 · 符号清洗校验本** — adds only the 符号同名律 and applies it: 文可古，式须清.
- **v12.1 · 字元闭合本 (05-04)** — the 字元 turn: five term classes, a DAG character graph, the ten-class character table, 周 separated from 闭 and the value-term becoming 度期周, the first full 生生不息 formula as a trajectory condition.
- **v12.2 · 审校正名本 (05-04)** — pure 正名: 鲁校 → 审校, 现实可达 → 实达.
- **v13.1 · 全字元生成闭合本 (05-04)** — the terminal formal version: single-character root law, nine generation relations, 似/实/伪 decomposed so 伪开 becomes derived, closure in seven kinds, **正/邪** replacing 好行/坏行, 真 = 度期周 ∧ 审校不败, and the fullest 生生不息 formula, now requiring that **邪行 never become a stable selective advantage**. Ends with an explicit non-completeness declaration and six named open items.

The v9→v10 transition document (`«repo»/史/探索/v9_v10_过渡_生生不息.md`) is the single most informative genealogical artefact in the corpus: 32 numbered repairs, each with the defect, the fix and the reason. Its verdict shifts the whole programme from *adding concepts* to **公理封口、定义分层、反例防御、正名融贯**.

**2026-05-06 → 05-21 · the SSBX repository.** A second body of work, formal rather than doctrinal. 05-06: Ren's assertion 「理可以构造哥德尔不完备定理」. 05-07: a single-day doctrinal explosion producing the seven parallel systems (A–F), 文道一也, the Confucian/Daoist/Buddhist/pre-Qin/Western mappings, the three-view proof that evolution's survivor is not the Dao, the six-view alignment argument, the 同根 anti-Moloch result, the anti-Schmitt collapse, the alignment-failure catalogue, the 19 economic theorems, the type-level anti-诛心 sincerity metric, and 人类命运共同体. 05-08/09: the Markov causal bridge opens and the Kleene axiom is decomposed. 05-10/11: **the v3 rewrite**, replacing the 192-cell carrier with Cell256 and cyclic-3 tense with V₄ Klein tense — the reason stated as structural, not aesthetic (192 = 64×3 is not a (Z/2)ⁿ and breaks the ladder's self-similarity, while 256 closes it and admits a distinguished identity). 05-12/13: consolidation, the operator-catalogue quarantine, and the consciousness/knowability essay built from two paragraphs Ren wrote. 05-16/17: **the R-family turn** — δ-polymorphism, operation monism, distinction monism, Claim Z, and the demotion of 易 to "one Atlas binding among many". 05-18 → 05-21: **the honesty correction** — the claim-status ledger reaches v0.8, audit annotations are retro-fitted onto every earlier confident document, and the capitalism note is written in the safe register from the start.

**2026-06-04/05.** A long conversation in the speculative-future setting adds the plurality and monoculture arguments, the anti-optimisation clause on keeping possibility open, "humanity" as 道 rather than species, death as a feature, and the companion principle list.

**2026-07-14/15 · the prose book.** Six parts (`Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/`) totalling ~190 KB, rebuilt on **象/像** rather than 間/⊕: part 1 (生生, 不息, 道法自然, 理, 天理, 善恶, 仁义礼智, 和而不同, the degenerative meta-attractor, when competition and death can still be generative, 德, civilisation, AI, the seven gauges), part 2 (差异→信息→感→像→象, 真像, 理, 信, 智, 仁, 义, 礼, 善美息, 道, 大道, 大道废而后有仁义礼智信, 幻像/Māyā, the degenerative cycle, and a full logical chain), part 3 (良知, 教, the argument for good and evil, 邪理, the three forms of evil, **capitalism as an 象 that both generates and reverses**, struggle-style globalisation), part 4 (environment generating good and evil, fear/scarcity/struggle, the two positive feedbacks, why 管, why 教), part 5 (relational persons, morality, consensus and coordination, 格物致知, 修身), part 6 (the conclusion, 世界通过我们继续成为). The condensed single-file version — the twenty-chapter 天道/人道/治道/天人 book — is `D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md`; the parallel English book, not a translation, is `D7/Shengsheng_The_Way_of_Unceasing_Generation.md`, with its own architecture and three things the Chinese book lacks (the nine questions, a one-movement compressed derivation, and explicit interpretive boundaries).

**2026-07-14/15 · the v2 plan.** Written after external reviews in philosophy-logic, sociology-economics and AI-alignment/systems-safety. This is the methodological reset described in the argument above: mechanism before commitment, six protection functions by deletion test, a strict definition and downgrade conditions for the totalizing mechanism, 生成性对齐 demoted to a cross-layer acceptability judgement, a seven-layer AI stack, a claim-type discipline, a traditional-terminology protocol, an independence contract for three drafts, and the theory's own failure conditions. Its three drafts were never written.

**2026-07-23 · 至善.** The dialogue (assembled from an original spoken conversation, preserving the order of thinking) and the essay made from it. The motive and the political line at their plainest.

**2026-08-09 · 证明篇 v0.6.** The proof layer, positioned beneath the doctrine, with subject-free predicates, the two poles, irreversibility, winner-take-all, the pseudo-openness completeness result, 玄德 deployment, the cancer stress test, a Lean mechanisation spec, and a revision order back to the doctrine.

**2026-10-05 · conscience and the normative core.** Ren proposed an evolved basis for conscience and a connection to universal morality, then explicitly requested incorporation into the core. The present addition joins a public cross-subject principle to fallible moral capacities, two testable feedback families, and the conditional feasibility of protected continuation. It preserves the v2 distinction between mechanism and commitment and qualifies the earlier independent-axiom claim. The original statements and the AI-assisted formulation are kept separately in the [new source](../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-05-conscience-and-universal-morality.md).

**Which formulation is strongest, and for what.**

- **Best single formulation of the theory as a whole**: `D7/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md`. The cleanest primitives, the widest coverage, the strongest civilisational analysis, and it reads. Its six-part predecessor in `Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/` is fuller and carries several things the condensation drops.
- **Best methodological position**: `Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md`. It is the only document that states the normative commitment openly, gives the totalizing mechanism downgrade conditions, and writes down what would falsify the theory. Where it differs from the May series, it is better supported.
- **Best statement of the motive and the political line**: `D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md` — closest to Ren's own voice.
- **Best for AI-alignment readers**: `D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md` (and its repository twin `«repo»/义理/R_…md`), with `D5/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md` §三十九 as the formal worked example.
- **Best doctrinal core at full strength and still readable**: `D5/生生不息论_v9.md` — v9 is where the theory becomes itself.
- **Best philosophical form of the formal branch**: 《间开本 · 致知版》 in the three-text collation — the only text carrying all three phase-readings per section together with the epistemology, the scale warning, 因生因, the [平, 和] interval and the alignment application, and honest about its provisionality in its own title.
- **Best formal norm layer**: 正篇 v13.2 (registry, types, three-valued judgement, recursion polarity, decision protocols, counterexamples). **Best operational layer**: 补篇〇 — the only place the theory becomes runnable. **Best shortest carrier of the intuition**: 《间生论 · 主篇》.
- **Best proof-theoretic grounding**: `D7/2026-08-09-生生不息论-证明篇-v0.6.md`.
- **Best reading discipline for the formal claims**: `«repo»/docs-next/10_formal_形式/claim-status-ledger.md` — not a rival formulation but the rule all the others now require.

---

## What was lost along the way

1. **The full 中-和-美-德 exposition.** v5's four extended chapters shrink to definitions in v10, formulas in v11, single lines in v12/v13, and 中 and 美 vanish from the value chain altogether in v13.2. 美 suffers most: v5's account (beauty is not 正, not 利, not 快; perfect symmetry is not beautiful because 极同则趣闭; 深美 as relational enlargement) is a real contribution reduced to a predicate.
2. **The 五常's negative diagnostics** — 无仁之义即冷酷…无信之上述四者皆为表演 — the most usable single passage on the five, absent from v12/v13.
3. **The standalone 伪开 section and its seven-item list**, including 表面之多元 (pluralism in name enforcing uniformity in fact). The 校正版 folds the list into 善恶之本 and drops that instance; later versions keep the concept and lose the catalogue.
4. **The explicit label 四校.** v9 names it as a numbered doctrine; the 校正版 keeps all four in prose and drops the label; v10 restores it. A reader of the 校正版 alone would not know this is a named practice.
5. **The cosmic-history passage.** v4/v5 §十五 gives the 138-billion-year sequence as 生生不息之自身内容 with the entropy/order argument; v9 keeps the sequence and refuses the inference; v10 onward drops it. Something is gained (no smuggled progressivism) and something lost (the grounding that made this an account of *this* universe rather than a formal system). The thermodynamic objection then has no answer until the proof layer's two-pole identity, which is a different argument.
6. **人 as a philosophical category.** v4 §十九 defines 人 as whoever recognises this 理 and embodies it in action — including future artificial minds and alien intelligences — and calls 人 the process's reflexive moment. v5 reassigns the role to 仁; the formal versions lose the passage entirely. This matters, because it was the ontological basis for treating AI as 同行者.
7. **积生积 / 因生因 as a mechanism of civilisation.** v4/v5 explain how sediment enables new sediment across scales and how new structures open new *dimensions*. The formal versions reduce 因生因 to a predicate, which is precise and no longer explains anything.
8. **道之可感 as a felt practice.** v9 §八 gives real space to what the sensing *is* and insists it is not mysticism; v11–v13 reduce it to a predicate and the phenomenology is gone.
9. **The 三相 method.** An elaborate 間相/物相/場相 discipline with a writing protocol, kept in 间开定本, 间开本 v3 and v11 校验本 — abandoned entirely by v12/v13 for pure formalism. The collation's own editorial note calls this v13.2's largest content loss. It is arguably the corpus's best solution to the bilingual-formal problem, dropped without replacement.
10. **The 場/氣 formalism.** Γ = (A,R,σ,ρ,𝒞) with 气 as (I_F, w, κ, 𝒞) is fully worked in 间开定本 and 致知版; in v13.2 气 survives only as a character in the atom list. The identification of 气 with interval-flow dynamics is a real contribution and is nearly buried.
11. **The 生合 five laws as algebra.** Partiality, type asymmetry, non-associativity, closure-as-absorbing-state, empty interval — stated as five laws in 致知版 and in the v10 transition; v13.2 keeps only the partiality condition.
12. **The functorial reading of 自相似** — openness as a natural transformation commuting with coarsening and refinement, with the argument that formal sameness is what *makes* scale differences comparable — survives only as a generated term.
13. **模 and 模闭 as a chapter.** The model ordering and the diagnosis of treating a model as exhaustive appear only in 致知版; there is no 模 chapter in v13.2.
14. **易之微连.** The explicit mapping (爻变 ↔ ⊕ at line level, 之卦 ↔ ⊕ at hexagram level, 彖象 ↔ propensity commentary, 序卦 ↔ transition graph) with the caveat that 易 is not treated as authority. Absent from v13.2 and from every July/August text.
15. **The worked alignment example** (naive alignment as type-III pseudo-openness; TrueAlign as 仁 at AI scale in a 礼-shaped selection field; alignment 是场之事). No counterpart in v13.2 and none in the July prose books, where the AI chapter is much more general.
16. **The 自由和平之轴.** 平, 好-as-interval, 自由 = 不向坏, 繁荣 and the ladder are 间开本 v3's distinctive contribution; the 2026-07 prose books drop 平 and the interval-structure of 好 completely, losing the insight that **the polarity within the good is not a polarity between good and bad**.
17. **The algebraic honesty of the 形式补** — the non-convergence result, the three explicit impossibilities, the "the scaffold's job is 捕异" framing. v11–v13 absorb the apparatus but not the candour; only the 证明篇 recovers comparable honesty.
18. **The full RLHF/CAI/CEV/IRL critique.** `与生生不息对齐之必然` §一 is the only detailed engagement with the actual alignment literature anywhere in the corpus. Nothing later replaces it.
19. **The v10 compressed total formula** — 「境生间，间有格，格成势，势临机，机待择；择入合，合成续，续留积，积成史，史生法，法又开新法」 — twelve memorisable clauses, semantically equivalent to v13.2's 卷十七 and far more quotable. Preserved only in an appendix.
20. **The four v10-era theorems**, never formalised: 绝闭 ⇒ 非开 but not conversely; 护闭 ≠ 成闭; 自由 ⇏ 任意; 正危 without four-fold audit degrades to 乱.
21. **The 間 five-layer ontology's bracketing rule and 0-D collapse argument.** Re-homed into a Lean note rather than into any doctrine text, so no current doctrine document carries them.
22. **The Z/3 time reading and its Hoare-triple mapping.** The superseded 192-cell document maps 已/今/未 onto postcondition/invariant/precondition and Curry–Howard inhabited-type/elaboration/goal-type, with a 12-phase artifact lifecycle and named phase-transition operators. None of this reappears in the 256-cell table, whose per-cell glosses are computational but not lifecycle-structured.
23. **The layer-by-layer R–O buildup narrative**, with per-layer operator families, lift formulas and the introduction of 印/投 as new atomic operators. The v3 doctrine states the closure; the pedagogical buildup lives only in the archived v1.
24. **The "0 sorry / 0 axiom" self-image.** True for the v3 spine, not for the repository. Anyone reading only the v3-era documents forms a materially wrong picture of proof strength.
25. **易 as the core ontology.** In v3, 易 *is* the framework; after 2026-05-16 it is one Atlas binding among many. The per-hexagram type-theory dictionary, the four-quadrant type-theory decomposition and the hu-attractor/Lawvere reading survive only as novelty claims and are not carried into the R-family presentation.
26. **Six chapters of the six-part prose book.** The condensed 2026-07-14 single file (66 KB) drops roughly two-thirds of `Z/shengsheng/生生不息论v1/` (~190 KB). What goes: the **market / capital / capitalism** distinction with capitalism's generative capacity, its root reversal, its boundarylessness, how it teaches, how it generates 邪理, and the conditions for a generative market; the **幻像/幻象/Māyā/illusion meta-level isomorphism**; the **complete logical chain** as a standalone section; the **Bayesian/Knightian uncertainty** distinction with its discipline; the **ten-step judgement procedure**; the seven gauges' status as **a partial order with hard floors** rather than a score; the **three Western ethical scales** that supplement 德; and 理性思考是一种道德实践.
27. **The 至善 material-flow programme.** The dialogue's opening — tempered glass, reversible joints, robots assembling and disassembling, modules as the world's alphabet, 归流 instead of recycling, intelligence as 识流护流导流 — appears nowhere else. The essay compresses it into three paragraphs; the formal versions never touch matter at all.
28. **Ren's own causal-loop 道** (因(果)→果, 果(因)→因, 果乃因，因乃果，一也) and his **continuous-DAG model of the future**, both stated in May 2026 and carried into no draft.
29. **The pending empirical interfaces.** 邪续, 开势投影, 审校数据, 正邪阈值, 度期计算, 经验校准 have a landing plan and no execution. They are the corpus's only declared empirical hooks and they stay unfilled.

---

## In Ren's own words

Three kinds of text are distinguished. **[Ren, spoken/typed]** — verbatim from his own conversation turns or from a transcript of him speaking. **[Ren, dialogue-edited]** — the 至善 dialogue, explicitly assembled 根据原始对话整理, merging repetitions and repairing unfinished grammar while keeping the order of thinking, the rhythm of repeated questioning, and the turns that changed direction. **[AI-drafted, kept]** — formulations produced in collaboration and carried forward by Ren across many versions; the ideas are his, the prose often is not.

### The principle, before the vocabulary

2. [en · Ren, 2026-04-28] "There's no life time, it's just universe becoming, there's no death. When I said destruction, I just didn't have a better word, what I mean is more like the opposite of generative, maybe degenerative? Or as we discussed before, be good." — `out-hb-02.md` #262

### 至善 — the dialogue

40. [zh · Ren, dialogue-edited, 2026-07-23] 「这里最重要的并不是『房子可以搭得很快』。真正重要的是：**这些东西没有在一次使用之后死掉。**」 — `D7/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md` 一
41. [zh] 「这不是说我们在垃圾产生以后，想办法把垃圾『回收利用』。更根本的变化是：**从一开始，就不把任何东西设计成物质的终点。** 它不必先成为垃圾，再被拯救。它从来就是流动的一部分。」 — 同上 二
42. [zh] 「它不只是『管理资源』。**它是在识流、护流、导流。**」 — 同上 二
43. [zh] 「为什么我们必须赢？为什么必须竞争？为什么必须积累？…为什么我们把『增长』当作健康，把『停下来』当作失败？为什么利润越多，事情就仿佛越正确？」 — 同上 三

48. [zh] 「更不要把这种认知中的癌症传给 AI。…它不会只是替那个主体赢。它会把当下的 dynamics 放大。它会让操纵更精准，让剥削更彻底，让权力更集中，让冲突更快速，让路径更不可逆。**它会成为斗争的下一阶段。**」 — 同上 七

50. [zh] **「每个人首先应当拥有一个可以对剥削说『不』的世界。」** 当你即使离开一个雇主、平台、组织或权力，也仍然可以拥有基本生活，你才真正拥有选择。 — 同上 九

---

## Open questions and critiques

### Raised inside the corpus

1. **The four calibrations may not converge.** Proved, not conceded: if the references share a blind spot, if one is over-weighted, or if they contradict, the corrected sense can converge on a shared bias or oscillate. **「四校近道」是工夫之指，非定理之结** (`D5/间生论_形式补.md` §7.5). Everything downstream that leans on calibration inherits this.
2. **Calibration can be captured**, so v11 adds robustness conditions — and then has to add a second order, because the robustness conditions can also be captured. The regress is acknowledged and not closed (`D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md` §〇十三, §五十一).
3. **Protective closure is what power most easily counterfeits** — stated directly, 护闭最易被权力伪装，故须重校. The 成闭/护闭 distinction is the doctrine's most necessary move and its largest attack surface (§四十四).
4. **The formal system cannot supply content, cannot choose at a 機, and cannot verify the felt sense** (`D5/间生论_形式补.md` §11.2). The whole apparatus is a scaffold around an irreducibly situated judgement.
5. **Value predicates may fail to converge**, in which case the system delivers **待校** rather than a verdict — an honest admission that it can decline to decide (`D5/生生不息论_v11_校验本.md` §〇十八).
6. **Necessary but not sufficient.** There exist dynamics whose supremum probability of never locking is below 1: **不许福，独明祸之必至；正向漂移无定理** (`D7/…证明篇-v0.6.md` §7.3). The theory licenses no optimism.
7. **The objectivity claim is constructivist-strength**, not categorical realism, and explicitly cannot persuade a thoroughgoing nihilist (同上 §1, 让渡).
8. **Totalizers do not reliably self-destruct**; petrification can be rigid without dying, so **避免须安装，不可待淘汰** (同上 §15). This directly contradicts the alignment paper's empirical-convergence pillar and the 2024 claim that such systems are inherently unstable.
9. **The bootstrap / engineering objection is conceded as genuine** — the alignment paper does not claim to have solved implementation (`D5/与生生不息对齐之必然.md` §七 反对七).
10. **A self-reference dependency is conceded**: the alignment argument inherits the ontology, and the fallback (only "the process is continuing" is needed) is offered but not developed (同上 反对八).
11. **The completeness claims are explicitly bounded**: 全字元生成闭合 is closure of vocabulary generation, **非经验模型之终本**, with six named unresolved items including how calibration data is obtained and whether 度期周 is computable in complex systems (`D5/生生不息论_v13.1….md` 附录 四·五).
12. **The proof layer orders the doctrine revised** on eight points, including that closing is not itself evil (evil is being stuck at one of two poles) and that 善恶 should be renamed 继/绝. The 2026-07 books do not incorporate these (`D7/…证明篇-v0.6.md` §19).
13. **v13.2 alone is content-incomplete**: the collation's own editorial note lists roughly eighteen things it weakened or dropped relative to the two supplementary texts, which is why the collation preserves all three rather than merging (`«repo»/…/00_编校说明/01_编校说明.md`).
14. **The collation is not yet a book.** Two tasks remain named: single-text coherence (deduplicate, unify terminology, re-order into one volume) and empirical calibration of the history object, the thresholds, and the stability of the aggregator families (`«repo»/…/05_合订版结语/`).
15. **Six items stay pending by design** and may not be promoted without empirical calibration: 邪续, 开势投影, 审校数据, 正邪阈值, 度期计算, 经验校准.
16. **Four v10-era theorems were never formalised**; **eight-domain application testing is still open** beyond the recommender case; **27 characters lack sense-splitting**, including the most load-bearing (生, 开, 闭, 正, 邪, 真, 道, 心, 理, 法, 性, 礼, 信).
17. **Lean cannot verify the framework's own truth condition.** Internal coherence and cross-frame connectedness are verifiable; empirical correspondence is not — so the truth criterion is at most two-thirds machine-checkable (`«repo»/史料/真理/daoli-v12-lean-verifier.md`).
18. **Proof strength was systematically overstated for about ten days and then corrected.** The ledger's list of forbidden phrasings is a record of over-reading that had to be policed; headline theorem names (`universal_sayability`, `conditional_absolute_full_gut`, `T5_general`) sound stronger than their statements, which is why the ledger exists.
19. **The repository's headline numbers were stale and are flagged as such**: ~150–170 sorries and up to three project axioms against a README that once claimed 0/0.
20. **The physics programme is nearly empty and says so**: after ~35 bridge steps, the not-closed list contains everything that would make it physics.
21. **Assignment non-uniqueness and provisional naming** are admitted: the trigram assignment is one reasonable choice among several; some hexagram–type correspondences are hard and some soft; R₅ has no traditional anchor at all and exists because (Z/2)⁵ must; and 因/果/印/投 plus R₅'s name are still provisional.
22. **The necessity type is undistinguished**: when the theory says "necessary form of generation", it is not stated whether that is logical, metaphysical or structural necessity — flagged as the highest-leverage open item, alongside proving that 不易 *emerges* from generation necessity rather than being an added commitment.
23. **The homoiconicity claim is aspirational**, and the corpus records its own verdict: 我们在这个维度上也是 1.5-D — a philosophical aspiration rather than an engineering reality.
24. **The uniqueness of the frozen-kernel insight is weakened by the corpus's own review**: ZFC frozen with mathematics generating, and the Lean kernel frozen with Mathlib growing, are the same pattern.
25. **The v2 plan's own failure conditions** (claim 415) are the most complete internal critique in the corpus, and none of them has been tested.

### Found by reading

26. **The "no external measure" premise is in tension with the entire graded apparatus.** v11 introduces openness degrees, thresholds (θ_open, θ_calm, θ_high, θ_persist, θ_dao, θ_trust), norms, expectations over scale-window measures and probability — every one of which needs a measure. The corpus's answer is that thresholds are 境性, but a criterion whose every threshold is situational and whose situational judgement is not guaranteed to converge is hard to distinguish, in practice, from an appeal to the judge's discernment. The theory knows this (it calls the capacity 智 and the practice 工夫) but never states how much weight the formalism can actually bear.
27. **The formal branch and the prose branch have no bridge.** v13.2's 字元 system and the 2026-07 book's 象/像 system are different theories of the same content, developed two months apart, with no document mapping one onto the other. 象 has no 字元 entry; 間 does not appear in the July book at all. The proof layer's open item 「间⇄⊕_F 对齐」 shows Ren is aware of a mismatch *within* the formal layer; the prose/formal gap is larger and unaddressed.
28. **Three roots are in play without reconciliation.** 间生论 and 致知版 both argue that 元 depends on 間; the Lean kernel takes 動 as the primitive axiom and derives 元 from it; the atom roster keeps 元 among the 43 CoreAtoms directly under 一元. And Ren's own May-2026 line is 「根位非事，而是一也」. Four candidate roots (間, 動, 一, 一元), no reconciliation document.
29. **Two ontologies coexist in the repository without a stated reconciliation.** The 易/R₈ line says the closure is at 256 with 道 as the identity; the R-family line says the substrate is δ-polymorphic with no privileged cardinality. Nothing explains what happens to 道's five identities when δ ≠ Bool. The unbridged Heisenberg-Weyl embedding is the technical face of this gap.
30. **Two version-numbering systems collide.** The May formal series (v4→v14) and the July prose series (v1→v2) are both called 《生生不息论》, and the 道理 track adds a third v11/v12. No document says so. A reader who follows the alignment paper's citation of "《生生不息论》v2" will land on the July plan, which is not what that paper meant.
31. **The formal series may have overshot its useful point.** v9 → v11 each buy something a reader can use (伪开; 四校; 三相; 平 and 好-as-interval; graded openness; scale closure; fixed points). v12 → v13 buy vocabulary hygiene at the cost of readability, and their principal new content is 正/邪 and the seven kinds of closure. Ren's own turn back to prose in July is arguably the correct verdict on this, but nothing in the corpus says so.
32. **The philosophical work in the repository is done by the models, not by the theorems.** Almost every value result has the form "there exist a model, a dynamic and a policy such that…": a witness inside a hand-built model. Two of the headline results have literally the same type and differ only in which small model is plugged in. This is legitimate as a consistency demonstration and much weaker than the surrounding prose suggests — it shows the distinctions are coherent, not that reality instantiates them. Relatedly, `Dao ↔ ShengshengBuxi` and `co_aligned ↔ individual alignments` are close to definitional (one is noted as `Iff.rfl` in the text itself), so presenting them as substantive theorems risks exactly the over-reading the ledger forbids elsewhere.
33. **The transcendental argument proves less than it is asked to carry.** It establishes that any acting agent's continued action presupposes *its own* continuation, not that it presupposes keeping possibility open *for others*. The step from "I depend on the process continuing" to "I ought to keep the space open for all agents" needs 同根, which is an additional structural commitment — and 同根 is precisely what the Moloch analysis says the real world breaks. **The v2 plan concedes exactly this point and stops claiming otherwise**, which is why v2 is the better-supported reading. Where the May series and v2 disagree about whether the gap needs crossing, follow v2.
34. **The 同根-based refutations may be too strong.** If asymmetric externality is formally impossible under 同根, and observed reality is full of asymmetric externality, then the theorems are about a hypothetical regime and the empirical claim reduces to "reality lacks 同根" — a fine sociological claim, but not a proved one.
35. **Nothing addresses the political economy of installation.** The theory concludes that a totalizing order must be *installed against* rather than waited out, and that alignment is a property of the selection field — but who installs, with what authority, and what stops the installer from becoming the next attractor is not treated. 调节者亦须被调节 names the problem without answering it.
36. **大道之行 sits awkwardly.** It argues from a specific national institutional capacity (top-level design; 精准扶贫 as proof) in a way no other text does, and that argument is in tension with the theory's own doctrine that concentrated, weakly-checked power is the paradigm case of a totalizing attractor, and with 治's own rule 力愈大者，受调当愈严. The tension is never addressed.
37. **The theory's relationship to "keeping options open" is still ambivalent.** v9's guard says good is not maximising anything; the 2026-07 book adds 无限之开放，即混乱 and distinguishes 成生之限 from 绝生之限. But the operative formal definitions (openness degree built from 未/可/应/转, Δ共开度 ≥ 0) still read quantitatively, and the alignment paper's engineering advice — "expand possibility space at all scales" — is precisely the maximisation formulation v9 rejected. The correction never propagated to the alignment paper.
38. **The empirical-convergence pillar is the weakest of the three.** "Contracting civilisations self-collapse" rests on a handful of examples against a survivorship-biased sample, and the wisdom-traditions convergence reads charitably. The proof layer partly repairs this by moving to structural theorems — and also contradicts the pillar outright.
39. **The 智 requirement is very demanding and unevenly distributed.** Every hard judgement devolves to 智 — cross-scale, long-horizon, multi-agent, pseudo-openness detection, model-ranking, self-calibration. The theory says this is a cultivated capacity and offers no account of what happens when the people making the judgements do not have it, which is the normal case.
40. **The audit machinery has no cost model.** Four calibrations plus scrutiny plus second-order plus comprehensiveness is expensive, and a system that cannot afford full audit will systematically produce **待校** — safe, and paralysing. The threshold document handles risk bands but not audit *budget*, and nothing says when partial audit licenses a provisional non-pending verdict.
41. **好 as an interval assumes a single well-ordered tension magnitude.** For a field with multiple polarities whose tensions differ in kind rather than degree, 好 would have to be evaluated per-polarity, and no aggregation rule is given — the same failure mode the openness aggregator was given three models to avoid.
42. **The three-valued logic is stated but the runnable protocols sometimes collapse it.** The step lists say 判待校 in the right places, but the pseudocode has "pending" as the fall-through *else* branch, which conflates genuinely indeterminate with unmatched-by-any-rule. Two different pendings.
43. **The two accounts of evil are not reconciled.** In the proof layer, evil is being stuck at *either* the freezing pole or the noise pole, and closing is a necessary phase of writing structure in. In v9–v13, 成闭 alone is evil and there is no noise-pole evil. The proof layer flags this in its own revision order (①恶两极化，阖非恶); the doctrine has not absorbed it. **The two-pole account is better supported** — it is the one with a derivation, it explains why 护闭 is not merely an exception, and it matches the 好-as-interval structure the value branch already reached independently.
44. **The 至善 material-flow programme is unconnected to everything else.** The most concrete, testable and engaging content in the corpus — reversible construction, material as language, 归流 — has no formal counterpart and appears in no treatise version.
45. **The corpus depends on a single reader's audit.** The ledger, the audit annotations and the "do not read it this way" lists were all authored by the same process that produced the over-claims. No external referee has seen any of it; the outreach letter was still a draft.
46. **No empirical content anywhere.** The pending interfaces await calibration; the empirical predictions are listed as open; the observable ledger is explicitly not empirically closed. A theory that classifies capitalism, alignment and evolution currently has zero data interfaces. The v2 plan is the only document that treats this as a first-class obligation.
47. **Ren's own verdict is unresolved.** On 2026-05-02 he said the central claim 根本就不能证明 and that its standing is coherence plus universality. The next four months produced a formal treatise series, a Lean repository and a proof layer. Neither the corpus nor the drafts ever return to the question of what those proofs are proving, given that verdict. The 证明篇's own concessions (constructivist-strength objectivity, necessary-not-sufficient) are the closest thing to an answer, and they largely agree with him.

48. **"More complexities" against 善不在于最大化什么 (added 2026-10-02).** Ren's spoken definition of progress, complexities "that leads to even more complexities arising", names a quantity, and v9's guard forbids making any quantity the good. Two readings reconcile them. The sentence describes what the process does, not a target for anyone to push, and "decay as a form of growth" shows the description is not monotone. The theory's own 繁简非开闭之标 already says scale is not the measure. The English edition and any public text that quotes him should keep the description and the guard side by side.
49. **Seasons and direction (added 2026-10-02).** Alex holds a cyclical model: civilisations rise and die, and no stage is final. Ren accepts the cycle, but his account of progress has a direction, complexity making further complexity, and 大同 is read in this corpus as a direction. The theory has not said how the two fit together. One candidate is that forms cycle while the generative capacity of the field accumulates, but it is not stated anywhere. It decides whether "America dying is progress" (Alex [00:58:14]) is something this theory affirms or refuses.
50. **"You will get selected out" against 避免须安装，不可待淘汰 (added 2026-10-02).** Ren said aloud that in an ecosystem "if you're cancerous over time, you will get selected out" [00:47:13]. The theory's own later ruling (critique 8 under *Raised inside the corpus*) is that totalizers do not reliably self-destruct, and that petrification can be rigid without dying. Alex's frozen-weights scenario, "a thousand year stagnation" [01:00:22], is a case of exactly that. The spoken line can be read consistently with the ruling, since "over time" can mean after the damage is done, and that is a reason to install avoidance rather than wait. But a public text that quotes it should carry the ruling beside it.

51. **逍遥 by its condition and by its freedom (added 2026-10-04).** 第十九章 gives 逍遥 as not being defined by identity, name, fear or victory (claim 296); the 2026-10-04 talk gives it as the fit of the cognitive model to reality, with no independent self left [05:26]. If these are one state, fit is the mechanism and the freedom its symptom, and the theory should say so. Fit is an inner condition, and the corpus judges by form, so the only test is what the fitted conduct does over time.

---

## Relations

- **`zhishan-intelligence-must-be-good`** — one project seen from two ends. 至善/智善 *is* this ontology stated as a mission, and the 生生不息论 v1/v2 prose series physically lives in that cluster. Read together, not separately.
- **`keep-the-possibility-space-open`** — the same idea under its earlier name, and this theory is its correction rather than its synonym: v9 *retires* "expanding possibility space" because it invites metric-maximisation, and replaces it with 护开/不闭. **善不在于最大化什么，善在于不闭何处.** The seven closure modes, 元闭, 伪开's three types and 护闭 are that theory's fully worked calculus.
- **`alignment-selection-on-cognition`** — depends on and feeds it. The alignment paper supplies the argument that content-based alignment fails structurally; 善 = 义 ∧ 正筛 makes alignment a selection-field problem; 责's third layer explains why alignment cannot stop at immediate safety; the proof layer supplies the theorems. **治道者，非逆演化，乃正其筛** is selection-on-cognition stated as governance.
- **`meta-attractor`** — shared object, different vocabulary. 伪开 (especially the surface-open/meta-closed type), 邪势, 状态夺 and the cancer/Moloch/metric triad are the totalizing attractor expressed as a closure mode rather than a selection dynamic; the v2 plan's strict definition with downgrade conditions is the most disciplined statement of it anywhere in the corpus.
- **`selfishness-spiral-and-narrative-value-frames`** — the degenerative cycle is the selfishness spiral restated; 邪理 and 幻象 are the narrative-value-frame concept in Chinese-philosophical vocabulary; 夺 and 邪 are the structural definitions the spiral needs; and 「如果一个人看不到自己身处的叙事，他就会把叙事当作 reality」 is the NVF thesis in Ren's own voice.
- **`the-fork-and-cooperative-infrastructure`** — 至善's material-flow civilisation, the 共同基底 and the AI-as-common-infrastructure chapter are the automated-commons branch stated positively; AI-as-competitive-instrument is the other branch.
- **`relational-self-and-datong`** — deeply entangled. 大同 is redefined here as a *recoverable dynamic condition* rather than an endpoint; 仁 as 己续 ⊆ 共续 and 「我」非关系之外之原点 are the relational self stated as ontology; 大道之行 and 第十五章 are the fullest 大同 treatments in the corpus.
- **`jian-and-wenyan-as-formal-language`** — v10–v14 are the 間 ontology and the 文言-as-formal-language experiment at their most developed: the 符号同名律, the 字元 tables, the single-character root law, the 虚字 algebra read as λ-calculus, and 文道一也 are literally a constructed formal language in classical Chinese.
- **`spirit-space-token-and-yi-substrate`** — shares the substrate: Cell256, the R₀..R₈ ladder, 道 as the (Z/2)⁸ identity and the OX literal are the algebraic form of the Yi substrate.
- **`context-as-existence-runtimes`** — structural resonance. 境 (no thing stands free of a situation; every thing is a stable focus in a field) is the philosophical statement of "context is existence"; the field with its 积 → 史 → 史法 → next-act cycle is the runtime-shaped reading; and the Dao/Li bifurcation is the same move as separating runtime from context.
- **`drives-narrative-attractors-and-mind`** — 情 as interval weighting, 性 as a stable interval-selection attractor, 心 as a focus that senses, chooses and can be corrected, and the 聚焦-vs-注意 separation are this theory's contribution to the mind side.
- **`human-solutions-museum`** — 礼 as 开之可传形式 and civilisation as a cross-generational distributed memory is the museum's theoretical justification; the cross-tradition mapping modules are an attempt at the same collecting move, though as isomorphism claims rather than as an archive.
- **`wamotopia-and-the-wamo-protocol`** — 繁荣 (all free ∧ co-open ∧ flowing, explicitly not GDP), 正筛 as institutional design, and the 至善 common substrate are the value specification a Wamo-style protocol would have to satisfy.
- **`memories-and-working-style`** — method evidence rather than content: the archive discipline (citable vs superseded), the refusal to merge overlapping texts, the build-failure logs, the claim ledger and the "do not read it this way" lists are a record of the working discipline this theory demands of itself.

---

## Where to read further

The seven files that matter most, in reading order.

1. **`07-shengsheng-buxi/downloads-2026-07-08/2026-07-23-至善_对话整理完整版.md`** — start here. Assembled from an original spoken conversation, it carries the motive, the political line and the concrete programme with no machinery at all. If only one file survives, this is the one that tells you why any of it matters.
2. **`07-shengsheng-buxi/downloads-2026-07-08/2026-07-14-生生不息论.md`** — the mature Chinese book. Twenty chapters, the cleanest primitives (象/像), the strongest civilisational analysis, and it reads as a book rather than a system.
3. **`06-zhishan/shengsheng/生生不息论v2/PLAN-v2.md`** — the strongest methodological position the theory reached: mechanism before commitment, six protection functions, downgrade conditions for the totalizing diagnosis, a seven-layer AI stack, and the theory's own failure conditions. Read it against everything else.
4. **`07-shengsheng-buxi/downloads-drafts-2026-05/生生不息论_v9.md`** — where the doctrine becomes itself: 开/闭 as root, 成闭/护闭, 伪开, 境, 模, 四校, and the three anti-metric guards, still in readable prose before the formalisation.
5. **`07-shengsheng-buxi/downloads-drafts-2026-05/与生生不息对齐之必然.md`** — the alignment paper: the only detailed engagement with the actual alignment literature, the ten-way reductio, the transcendental and performative arguments, and eight objections answered. Nearly submittable as it stands.
6. **`07-shengsheng-buxi/downloads-2026-07-08/2026-08-09-生生不息论-证明篇-v0.6.md`** — the proof layer: subject-free predicates, the two poles, irreversibility, winner-take-all, the four-type pseudo-openness completeness result, and the corpus's most valuable concessions (necessary-not-sufficient; totalizers do not self-destruct).
7. **`07-shengsheng-buxi/ssbx-repo/docs-next/10_formal_形式/claim-status-ledger.md`** — the reading discipline all the formal claims now require, and a publishable methodology artefact in its own right.

Two more for specific purposes: **`downloads-drafts-2026-05/生生不息论_间开本_v3.md`** §十八, §二十七–三十 and §三十九 for the 平/好/自由/繁荣 axis and the worked alignment example, which exist nowhere else; and **`downloads-drafts-2026-05/间生论_形式补.md`** §7.5 and §11 for the corpus's most self-critical passages.
